Vākyapadīya transcribed by Somadeva Vasudeva (start - 3.7) and Yves Ramseier (3.8 - end), based on the Rau 1977 edition.
Bhartṛharis Vākyapadīya
Wilhelm Rau
Wiesbaden
Deutsche Morgenlaendischen Gesellschaft
1977
Copyright (C) 1977
Franz Steiner Verlag GmbH
. (See URL: target="http:copyright.gov.in/Documents/handbook.html)
The electronic text below is in a lossless transliteration using the Latin alphabet. The transliteration scheme used is the IAST (
The International Alphabet of Sanskrit Transliteration
). IAST differs in small ways from ISO 15919, but is preferred by most working Sanskrit scholars. Conversion of this file to ISO 15919 can be achieved by performing the following replacements throughout the file:
ṛ -> r̥ and ṃ -> ṁ
Text division separates words with spaces ("ity evam" not "ityevam"), where sandhi permits.
Avagraha is represented by +a. Thus "so 'pi" -> "so+api".
Vākyapadīya 1.1 - 3.7 entered by Somadeva Vasudeva
Whole Vākyapadīya, 1.1 - 3.14 entered by Yves Ramseier and released on the web
Added the Vasudeva transcription up to 3.7 to the Ramseier transcription from 3.8 to the end to make a composite e-text.
Added TEI encoding.
Changed avagrahas to +a
Replaced the dollar/percent/ampersand tagging of the original transcription by the rs elements, with abcd as attributes.
Normalized verse numbering.
added divisions and headers for the kāṇḍas, samuddeśas and adhikāras.
Revisions to the TEI header.
Updated markup according to the SARIT Guidelines: Replaced p-elements with lg, and l. Removed rs-elements. Added trailers and xml:id's.
Added labels to lg's.

Brahmakāṇḍa
B1.1/ anādinidhanaṃ brahma śabdatattvaṃ yad akṣaram
BL2/ vivartate+arthabhāvena prakriyā jagato yataḥ
B1.2/ ekam eva yad āmnātaṃ bhinnaśaktivyapāśrayāt
BL4/ apṛthaktve+api śaktibhyaḥ pṛthaktveneva vartate
B1.3/ adhyāhitakalāṃ yasya kālaśaktim upāśritāḥ
BL6/ janmādayo vikārāḥ ṣaḍ bhāvabhedasya yonayaḥ
B1.4/ ekasya sarvabījasya yasya ceyam anekadhā
BL8/ bhoktṛbhoktavyarūpeṇa bhogarūpeṇa ca sthitiḥ
B1.5/ prāptyupāyo+anukāraś ca tasya vedo maharṣibhiḥ
BL10/ eko+apy anekavartmeva samāmnātaḥ pṛthak pṛthak
B1.6/ bhedānāṃ bahumārgatvaṃ karmaṇy ekatra cāṅgatā
BL12/ śabdānāṃ yataśaktitvaṃ tasya śākhāsu dṛśyate
B1.7/ smṛtayo bahurūpāś ca dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaprayojanāḥ
BL14/ tam evāśritya liṅgebhyo vedavidbhiḥ prakalpitāḥ
B1.8/ tasyārthavādarūpāṇi niśritāḥ svavikalpajāḥ
BL16/ ekatvināṃ dvaitināṃ ca pravādā bahudhāgatāḥ
B1.9/ satyā viśuddhis tatroktā vidyaivaikapadāgamā
BL18/ yuktā praṇavarūpeṇa sarvavādāvirodhinā
B1.10/ vidhātus tasya lokānām aṅgopāṅganibandhanāḥ
BL20/ vidyābhedāḥ pratāyante jñānasaṃskārahetavaḥ
B1.11/ āsannaṃ brahmaṇas tasya tapasām uttamaṃ tapaḥ
BL22/ prathamaṃ chandasām aṅgam āhur vyākaraṇaṃ budhāḥ
B1.12/ prāptarūpavibhāgāyā yo vācaḥ paramo rasaḥ
BL24/ yat tat puṇyatamaṃ jyotis tasya mārgo+ayam āñjasaḥ
B1.13/ arthapravṛttitattvānāṃ śabdā eva nibandhanam
BL26/ tattvāvabodhaḥ śabdānāṃ nāsti vyākaraṇād ṛte
B1.14/ tad dvāram apavargasya vāṅmalānāṃ cikitsitam
BL28/ pavitraṃ sarvavidyānām adhividyaṃ prakāśate
B1.15/ yathārthajātayaḥ sarvāḥ śabdākṛtinibandhanāḥ
BL30/ tathaiva loke vidyānām eṣā vidyā parāyaṇam
B1.16/ idam ādyaṃ padasthānaṃ siddhisopānaparvaṇām
BL32/ iyaṃ sā mokṣamāṇānām ajihmā rājapaddhatiḥ
B1.17/ atrātītaviparyāsaḥ kevalām anupaśyati
BL34/ chandasyaś chandasāṃ yonim ātmā chandomayīṃ tanum
B1.18/ pratyastamitabhedāyā yad vāco rūpam uttamam
BL36/ yad asminn eva tamasi jyotiḥ śuddhaṃ vivartate
B1.19/ vaikṛtaṃ samatikrāntā mūrtivyāpāradarśanam
BL38/ vyatītyālokatamasī prakāśaṃ yam upāsate
B1.20/ yatra vāco nimittāni cihnānīvākṣarasmṛteḥ
BL40/ śabdapūrveṇa yogena bhāsante pratibimbavat
B1.21/ atharvaṇām aṅgirasāṃ sāmnām ṛgyajuṣasya ca
BL42/ yasminn uccāvacā varṇāḥ pṛthaksthitaparigrahāḥ
B1.22/ yad ekaṃ prakriyābhedair bahudhā pravibhajyate
BL44/ tad vyākaraṇam āgamya paraṃ brahmādhigamyate
B1.23/ nityāḥ śabdārthasaṃbandhās tatrāmnātā maharṣibhiḥ
BL46/ sūtrāṇāṃ sānutantrāṇāṃ bhāṣyāṇāṃ ca praṇetṛbhiḥ
B1.24/ apoddhārapadārthā ye ye cārthāḥ sthitalakṣaṇāḥ
BL48/ anvākhyeyāś ca ye śabdā ye cāpi pratipādakāḥ
B1.25/ kāryakāraṇabhāvena yogyabhāvena ca sthitāḥ
BL50/ dharme ye pratyaye cāṅgaṃ saṃbandhāḥ sādhvasādhuṣu
B1.26/ te liṅgaiś ca svaśabdaiś ca śāstre+asminn upavarṇitāḥ
BL52/ smṛtyartham anugamyante ke cid eva yathāgamam
B1.27/ śiṣṭebhya āgamāt siddhāḥ sādhavo dharmasādhanam
BL54/ arthapratyāyanābhede viparītās tv asādhavaḥ
B1.28/ nityatve kṛtakatve vā teṣām ādir na vidyate
BL56/ prāṇinām iva sā caiṣā vyavasthānityatocyate
B1.29/ nānarthikām imāṃ kaś cid vyavasthāṃ kartum arhati
BL58/ tasmān nibadhyate śiṣṭaiḥ sādhutvaviṣayā smṛtiḥ
B1.30/ na cāgamād ṛte dharmas tarkeṇa vyavatiṣṭhate
BL60/ ṛṣīṇām api yaj jñānaṃ tad apy āgamapūrvakam
B1.31/ dharmasya cāvyavacchinnāḥ panthāno ye vyavasthitāḥ
BL62/ na tāṃl lokaprasiddhatvāt kaś cit tarkeṇa bādhate
B1.32/ avasthādeśakālānāṃ bhedād bhinnāsu śaktiṣu
BL64/ bhāvānām anumānena prasiddhir atidurlabhā
B1.33/ nirjñātaśakter dravyasya tāṃ tām arthakriyāṃ prati
BL66/ viśiṣṭadravyasaṃbandhe sā śaktiḥ pratibadhyate
B1.34/ yatnenānumito+apy arthaḥ kuśalair anumātṛbhiḥ
BL68/ abhiyuktatarair anyair anyathaivopapādyate
B1.35/ pareṣām asamākhyeyam abhyāsād eva jāyate
BL70/ maṇirūpyādivijñānaṃ tadvidāṃ nānumānikam
B1.36/ pratyakṣam anumānaṃ ca vyatikramya vyavasthitāḥ
BL72/ pitṛrakṣaḥpiśācānāṃ karmajā eva siddhayaḥ
B1.37/ āvirbhūtaprakāśānām anupaplutacetasām
BL74/ atītānāgatajñānaṃ pratyakṣān na viśiṣyate
B1.38/ atīndriyān asaṃvedyān paśyanty ārṣeṇa cakṣuṣā
BL76/ ye bhāvān vacanaṃ teṣāṃ nānumānena bādhyate
B1.39/ yo yasya svam iva jñānaṃ darśanaṃ nātiśaṅkate
BL78/ thitaṃ pratyakṣapakṣe taṃ katham anyo nivartayet
B1.40/ idaṃ puṇyam idaṃ pāpam ity etasmin padadvaye
BL80/ ācaṇḍālamanuṣyāṇām alpaṃ śāstraprayojanam
B1.41/ caitanyam iva yaś cāyam avicchedena vartate
BL82/ āgamas tam upāsīno hetuvādair na bādhyate
B1.42/ hastasparśād ivāndhena viṣame pathi dhāvatā
BL84/ anumānapradhānena vinipāto na durlabhaḥ
B1.43/ tasmād akṛtakaṃ śāstraṃ smṛtiṃ ca sanibandhanām
BL86/ āśrityārabhyate śiṣṭaiḥ sādhutvaviṣayā smṛtiḥ
B1.44/ dvāv upādānaśabdeṣu śabdau śabdavido viduḥ
BL88/ eko nimittaṃ śabdānām aparo+arthe prayujyate
B1.45/ avibhakto vibhaktebhyo jāyate+arthasya vācakaḥ
BL90/ śabdas tatrārtharūpātmā saṃbhedam upagacchati
B1.46/ ātmabhedaṃ tayoḥ ke cid astīty āhuḥ purāṇagāḥ
BL92/ buddhibhedād abhinnasya bhedam eke pracakṣate
B1.47/ araṇisthaṃ yathā jyotiḥ prakāśāntarakāraṇam
BL94/ tadvac chabdo+api buddhisthaḥ śrutīnāṃ kāraṇaṃ pṛthak
B1.48/ vitarkitaḥ purā buddhyā kva cid arthe niveśitaḥ
BL96/ karaṇebhyo vivṛttena dhvaninā so+anugṛhyate
B1.49/ nādasya kramajātatvān na pūrvo na paraś ca saḥ
BL98/ akramaḥ kramarūpeṇa bhedavān iva jāyate
B1.50/ pratibimbaṃ yathānyatra sthitaṃ toyakriyāvaśāt
BL100/ tatpravṛttim ivānveti sa dharmaḥ sphoṭanādayoḥ
B1.51/ ātmarūpaṃ yathā jñāne jñeyarūpaṃ ca dṛśyate
BL102/ artharūpaṃ tathā śabde svarūpaṃ ca prakāśate
B1.52/ āṇḍabhāvam ivāpanno yaḥ kratuḥ śabdasaṃjñakaḥ
BL104/ vṛttis tasya kriyārūpā bhāgaśo bhajate kramam
B1.53/ yathaikabuddhiviṣayā mūrtir ākriyate paṭe
BL106/ mūrtyantarasya tritayam evaṃ śabde+api dṛśyate
B1.54/ yathā prayoktuḥ prāg buddhiḥ śabdeṣv eva pravartate
BL108/ vyavasāyo grahītṝṇām evaṃ teṣv eva jāyate
B1.55/ arthopasarjanībhūtān abhidheyeṣu keṣu cit
BL110/ caritārthān parārthatvān na lokaḥ pratipadyate
B1.56/ grāhyatvaṃ grāhakatvaṃ ca dve śaktī tejaso yathā
BL112/ tathaiva sarvaśabdānām ete pṛthag avasthite
B1.57/ viṣayatvam anāpannaiḥ śabdair nārthaḥ prakāśyate
BL114/ na sattayaiva te+arthānām agṛhītāḥ prakāśakāḥ
B1.58/ ato+anirjñātarūpatvāt kim āhety abhidhīyate
BL116/ nendriyāṇāṃ prakāśye+arthe svarūpaṃ gṛhyate tathā
B1.59/ bhedenāvagṛhītau dvau śabdadharmāv apoddhṛtau
BL118/ bhedakāryeṣu hetutvam avirodhena gacchataḥ
B1.60/ vṛddhyādayo yathā śabdāḥ svarūpopanibandhanāḥ
BL120/ ādaicpratyāyitaiḥ śabdaiḥ saṃbandhaṃ yānti saṃjñibhiḥ
B1.61/ agniśabdas tathaivāyam agniśabdanibandhanaḥ
BL122/ agniśrutyaiti saṃbandham agniśabdābhidheyayā
B1.62/ yo ya uccāryate śabdo niyataṃ na sa kāryabhāk
BL124/ anyapratyāyane śaktir na tasya pratibadhyate
B1.63/ uccaran paratantratvād guṇaḥ kāryair na yujyate
BL126/ tasmāt tadarthaiḥ kāryāṇāṃ saṃbandhaḥ parikalpyate
B1.64/ sāmānyam āśritaṃ yad yad upamānopameyayoḥ
BL128/ tasya tasyopamāneṣu dharmo+anyo vyatiricyate
B1.65/ guṇaḥ prakarṣahetur yaḥ svātantryeṇopadiśyate
BL130/ tasyāśritād guṇād eva prakṛṣṭatvaṃ pratīyate
B1.66/ tasyābhidheyabhāvena yaḥ śabdaḥ samavasthitaḥ
BL132/ tasyāpy uccāraṇe rūpam anyat tasmād vivicyate
B1.67/ prāk samjñinābhisaṃbandhāt saṃjñā rūpapadārthikā
BL134/ ṣaṣṭhyāś ca prathamāyāś ca nimittatvāya kalpate
B1.68/ trārthavattvāt prathamā saṃjñāśabdād vidhīyate
BL136/ asyeti vyatirekaś ca tadarthād eva jāyate
B1.69/ svaṃ rūpam iti kaiś cit tu vyaktiḥ saṃjñopadiśyate
BL138/ jāteḥ kāryāṇi saṃsṛṣṭā jātis tu pratipadyate
B1.70/ saṃjñinīṃ vyaktim icchanti sūtre grāhyām athāpare
BL140/ jātipratyāyitā vyaktiḥ pradeśeṣūpatiṣṭhate
B1.71/ kāryatve nityatāyāṃ vā ke cid ekatvavādinaḥ
BL142/ kāryatve nityatāyāṃ vā ke cin nānātvavādinaḥ
B1.72/ padabhede+api varṇānām ekatvaṃ na nivartate
BL144/ vākyeṣu padam ekaṃ ca bhinneṣv apy upalabhyate
B1.73/ na varṇavyatirekeṇa padam anyac ca vidyate
BL146/ vākyaṃ varṇapadābhyāṃ ca vyatiriktaṃ na kiṃ ca na
B1.74/ pade na varṇā vidyante varṇeṣv avayavā na ca
BL148/ vākyāt padānām atyantaṃ pravibhāgo na kaś ca na
B1.75/ bhinnadarśanam āśritya vyavahāro+anugamyate
BL150/ tatra yan mukhyam ekeṣāṃ tatrānyeṣāṃ viparyayaḥ
B1.76/ sphoṭasyābhinnakālasya dhvanikālānupātinaḥ
BL152/ grahaṇopādhibhedena vṛttibhedaṃ pracakṣate
B1.77/ svabhāvabhedān nityatve hrasvadīrghaplutādiṣu
BL154/ prākṛtasya dhvaneḥ kālaḥ śabdasyety upacaryate
B1.78/ śabdasya grahaṇe hetuḥ prākṛto dhvanir iṣyate
BL156/ sthitibhedanimittatvaṃ vaikṛtaḥ pratipadyate
B1.79/ śabdasyordhvam abhivyakter vṛttibhedaṃ tu vaikṛtāḥ
BL158/ dhvanayaḥ samupohante sphoṭātmā tair na bhidyate
B1.80/ indriyasyaiva saṃskāraḥ śabdasyaivobhayasya vā
BL160/ kriyate dhvanibhir vādās trayo+abhivyaktivādinām
B1.81/ indriyasyaiva saṃskāraḥ samādhānāñjanādibhiḥ
BL162/ viṣayasya tu saṃskāras tadgandhapratipattaye
B1.82/ cakṣuṣaḥ prāpyakāritve tejasā tu dvayor api
BL164/ viṣayendriyayor iṣṭaḥ saṃskāraḥ sa kramo dhvaneḥ
B1.83/ sphoṭarūpāvibhāgena dhvaner grahaṇam iṣyate
BL166/ kaiś cid dhvanir asaṃvedyaḥ svatantro+anyaiḥ prakalpitaḥ
B1.84/ yathānuvākaḥ śloko vā soḍhatvam upagacchati
BL168/ āvṛttyā na tu sa granthaḥ pratyāvṛtti nirūpyate
B1.85/ pratyayair anupākhyeyair grahaṇānuguṇais tathā
BL170/ dhvaniprakāśite śabde svarūpam avadhāryate
B1.86/ nādair āhitabījāyām antyena dhvaninā saha
BL172/ āvṛttaparipākāyāṃ buddhau śabdo+avadhāryate
B1.87/ asataś cāntarāle yāñ śabdān astīti manyate
BL174/ pratipattur aśaktiḥ sā grahaṇopāya eva saḥ
B1.88/ bhedānukāro jñānasya vācaś copaplavo dhruvaḥ
BL176/ kramopasṛṣṭarūpā vāg jñānaṃ jñeyavyapāśrayam
B1.89/ [jñeyena na vinā jñānaṃ vyavahāre+avatiṣṭhate
BL178/ nālabdhakramayā vācā kaś cid artho+abhidhīyate]
B1.90/ yathādyasaṃkhyāgrahaṇam upāyaḥ pratipattaye
BL180/ saṃkhyāntarāṇāṃ bhede+api tathā śabdāntaraśrutiḥ
B1.91/ pratyekaṃ vyañjakā bhinnā varṇavākyapadeṣu ye
BL182/ teṣām atyantabhede+api saṃkīrṇā iva śaktayaḥ
B1.92/ yathaiva darśanaiḥ pūrvair dūrāt saṃtamase+api vā
BL184/ anyathākṛtya viṣayam anyathaivādhyavasyati
B1.93/ vyajyamāne tathā vākye vākyābhivyaktihetubhiḥ
BL186/ bhāgāvagraharūpeṇa pūrvaṃ buddhiḥ pravartate
B1.94/ yathānupūrvīniyamo vikāre kṣīrabījayoḥ
BL188/ tathaiva pratipattṝṇāṃ niyato buddhiṣu kramaḥ
B1.95/ bhāgavatsv api teṣv eva rūpabhedo dhvaneḥ kramāt
BL190/ nirbhāgeṣv abhyupāyo vā bhāgabhedaprakalpanam
B1.96/ anekavyaktyabhivyaṅgyā jātiḥ sphoṭa iti smṛtā
BL192/ kaiś cid vyaktaya evāsyā dhvanitvena prakalpitāḥ
B1.97/ avikārasya śabdasya nimittair vikṛto dhvaniḥ
BL194/ upalabdhau nimittatvam upayāti prakāśavat
B1.98/ na cānityeṣv abhivyaktir niyamena vyavasthitā
BL196/ āśrayair api nityānāṃ jātīnāṃ vyaktir iṣyate
B1.99/ deśādibhiś ca saṃbandho dṛṣṭaḥ kāyavatām api
BL198/ deśabhedavikalpe+api na bhedo dhvaniśabdayoḥ
B1.100/ grahaṇagrāhyayoḥ siddhā yogyatā niyatā yathā
BL200/ vyaṅgyavyañjakabhāve+api tathaiva sphoṭanādayoḥ
B1.101/ sadṛśagrahaṇānāṃ ca gandhādīnāṃ prakāśakam
BL202/ nimittaṃ niyataṃ loke pratidravyam avasthitam
B1.102/ prakāśakānāṃ bhedāṃś ca prakāśyo+artho +anuvartate
BL204/ tailodakādibhede tat pratyakṣaṃ pratibimbake
B1.103/ viruddhaparimāṇeṣu vajrādarśatalādiṣu
BL206/ parvatādisarūpāṇāṃ bhāvānāṃ nāsti saṃbhavaḥ
B1.104/ tasmād abhinnakāleṣu varṇavākyapadādiṣu
BL208/ vṛttikālaḥ svakālaś ca nādabhedād vibhajyate
B1.105/ yaḥ saṃyogavibhāgābhyāṃ karaṇair upajanyate
BL210/ sa sphoṭaḥ śabdajāḥ śabdā dhvanayo+anyair udāhṛtāḥ
B1.106/ alpe mahati vā śabde sphoṭakālo na bhidyate
BL212/ paras tu śabdasaṃtānaḥ pracayāpacayātmakaḥ
B1.107/ dūrāt prabheva dīpasya dhvanimātraṃ tu lakṣyate
BL214/ ghaṇṭādīnāṃ ca śabdeṣu vyakto bhedaḥ sa dṛśyate
B1.108/ dravyābhighātāt pracitau bhinnau dīrghaplutāv api
BL216/ kampe tūparate jātā nādā vṛtter viśeṣakāḥ
B1.109/ anavasthitakampe+api karaṇe dhvanayo+apare
BL218/ sphoṭād evopajāyante jvālā jvālāntarād iva
B1.110/ vāyor aṇūnāṃ jñānasya śabdatvāpattir iṣyate
BL220/ kaiś cid darśanabhedo hi pravādeṣv anavasthitaḥ
B1.111/ [labdhakriyaḥ prayatnena vaktur icchānuvartinā
BL222/ sthāneṣv abhihato vāyuḥ śabdatvaṃ pratipadyate
B1.112/ tasya kāraṇasāmarthyād vegapracayadharmaṇaḥ
BL224/ saṃnipātād vibhajyante sāravatyo+api mūrtayaḥ
B1.113/ aṇavaḥ sarvaśaktitvād bhedasaṃsargavṛttayaḥ
BL226/ chāyātapatamaḥśabda- bhāvena pariṇāminaḥ
B1.114/ svaśaktau vyajyamānāyāṃ prayatnena samīritāḥ
BL228/ abhrāṇīva pracīyante śabdākhyāḥ paramāṇavaḥ
B1.115/ athāyam āntaro jñātā sūkṣmavāgātmani sthitaḥ
BL230/ vyaktaye svasya rūpasya śabdatvena vivartate
B1.116/ sa manobhāvam āpadya tejasā pākam āgataḥ
BL232/ vāyum āviśati prāṇam athāsau samudīryate
B1.117/ antaḥkaraṇatattvasya vāyur āśrayatāṃ gataḥ
BL234/ taddharmeṇa samāviṣṭas tejasaiva vivartate
B1.118/ vibhajan svātmano granthīñ śrutirūpaiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ
BL236/ prāṇo varṇān abhivyajya varṇeṣv evopalīyate
B1.119/ ātmā buddhyā samarthyārthān mano yuṅkte vivakṣayā
BL238/ manaḥ kāyāgnim āhanti sa prerayati mārutam]
B1.120/ ajasravṛttir yaḥ śabdaḥ sūkṣmatvān nopalabhyate
BL240/ vyajanād vāyur iva sa svanimittāt pratīyate
B1.121/ tasya prāṇe ca yā śaktir yā ca buddhau vyavasthitā
BL242/ vivartamānā sthāneṣu saiṣā bhedaṃ prapadyate
B1.122/ śabdeṣv evāśritā śaktir viśvasyāsya nibandhanī
BL244/ yannetraḥ pratibhātmāyaṃ bhedarūpaḥ pratāyate
B1.123/ śabdādibhedaḥ śabdena vyākhyāto rūpyate yataḥ
BL246/ tasmād arthavidhāḥ sarvāḥ śabdamātrāsu niśritāḥ
B1.124/ śabdasya pariṇāmo+ayam ity āmnāyavido viduḥ
BL248/ chandobhya eva prathamam etad viśvaṃ pravartate
B1.125/ vibhajya bahudhātmānaṃ sa cchandasyaḥ prajāpatiḥ
BL250/ chandomayībhir mātrābhir bahudhaiva viveśa tam
B1.126/ sādhvī vāg bhūyasī yeṣu puruṣeṣu vyavasthitā
BL252/ adhikaṃ vartate teṣu puṇyaṃ rūpaṃ prajāpateḥ
B1.127/ prājāpatyaṃ mahat tejas tatpātrair iva saṃvṛtam
BL254/ śarīrabhede viduṣāṃ svāṃ yonim upadhāvati
B1.128/ yad etan maṇḍalaṃ bhāsvad dhāma citrasya rādhasaḥ
BL256/ tadbhāvam abhisaṃbhūya vidyāyāṃ pravilīyate
B1.129/ itikartavyatā loke sarvā śabdavyapāśrayā
BL258/ yāṃ pūrvāhitasaṃskāro bālo+api pratipadyate
B1.130/ yaḥ karaṇavinyāsaḥ prāṇasyordhvaṃ samīraṇam
BL260/ sthānānām abhighātaś ca na vinā śabdabhāvanām
B1.131/ na so+asti pratyayo loke yaḥ śabdānugamād ṛte
BL262/ anuviddham iva jñānaṃ sarvaṃ śabdena bhāsate
B1.132/ vāgrūpatā ced utkrāmed avabodhasya śāśvatī
BL264/ na prakāśaḥ prakāśeta sā hi pratyavamarśinī
B1.133/ sā sarvavidyāśilpānāṃ kalānāṃ copabandhanī
BL266/ tadvaśād abhiniṣpannaṃ sarvaṃ vastu vibhajyate
B1.134/ saiṣā saṃsāriṇāṃ saṃjñā bahir antaś ca vartate
BL268/ tanmātrām avyatikrāntaṃ caitanyaṃ sarvajātiṣu
B1.135/ arthakriyāsu vāk sarvān samīhayati dehinaḥ
BL270/ tadutkrāntau visaṃjño+ayaṃ dṛśyate kāṣṭhakuḍyavat
B1.136/ bhedodgrāhavivartena labdhākāraparigrahā
BL272/ āmnātā sarvavidyāsu vāg eva prakṛtiḥ parā
B1.137/ ekatvam anatikrāntā vāṅnetrā vāṅnibandhanāḥ
BL274/ pṛthak pratyavabhāsante vāgvibhāgā gavādayaḥ
B1.138/ ṣaḍdvārāṃ ṣaḍadhiṣṭhānāṃ ṣaṭprabodhāṃ ṣaḍavyayām
BL276/ te mṛtyum ativartante ye vai vācam upāsate
B1.139/ pravibhāge yathā kartā tayā kārye pravartate
BL278/ avibhāge tathā saiva kāryatvenāvatiṣṭhate
B1.140/ pravibhajyātmanātmānaṃ sṛṣṭvā bhāvān pṛthagvidhān
BL280/ sarveśvaraḥ sarvamayaḥ svapne bhoktā pravartate
B1.141/ svamātrā paramātrā vā śrutyā prakramyate yathā
BL282/ tathaiva rūḍhatām eti tayā hy artho vidhīyate
B1.142/ atyantam atathābhūte nimitte śrutyapāśrayāt
BL284/ dṛśyate+alātacakrādau vastvākāranirūpaṇā
B1.143/ api prayoktur ātmānaṃ śabdam antar avasthitam
BL286/ prāhur mahāntam ṛṣabhaṃ yena sāyujyam iṣyate
B1.144/ tasmād yaḥ śabdasaṃskāraḥ sā siddhiḥ paramātmanaḥ
BL288/ tasya pravṛttitattvajñas tad brahmāmṛtam aśnute
B1.145/ prāṇavṛttim atikrānte vācas tattve vyavasthitaḥ
BL290/ kramasaṃhārayogena saṃhṛtyātmānam ātmani
B1.146/ vācaḥ saṃskāram ādhāya vācaṃ jñāne niveśya ca
BL292/ vibhajya bandhanāny asyāḥ kṛtvā tāṃ chinnabandhanām
B1.147/ jyotir āntaram āsādya cchinnagranthiparigrahaḥ
BL294/ kāraṇajyotiṣaikatvaṃ chittvā granthīn pravartate
B1.148/ na jātv akartṛkaṃ kaś cid āgamaṃ pratipadyate
BL296/ bījaṃ sarvāgamāpāye trayy evāto vyavasthitā
B1.149/ astaṃ yāteṣu vādeṣu kartṛṣv anyeṣv asatsv api
BL298/ śrutismṛtyuditaṃ dharmaṃ loko na vyativartate
B1.150/ jñāne svābhāvike nārthaḥ śāstraiḥ kaś ca na vidyate
BL300/ dharmo jñānasya hetuś cet tasyāmnāyo nibandhanam
B1.151/ vedaśāstrāvirodhī ca tarkaś cakṣur apaśyatām
BL302/ rūpamātrād dhi vākyārthaḥ kevalaṃ nātitiṣṭhati
B1.152/ sato+avivakṣā pārārthyaṃ vyaktir arthasya laiṅgikī
BL304/ iti nyāyo bahuvidhas tarkeṇa pravibhajyate
B1.153/ śabdānām eva sā śaktis tarko yaḥ puruṣāśrayaḥ
BL306/ sa śabdānugato nyāyo +anāgameṣv anibandhanaḥ
B1.154/ yad udumbaravarṇānāṃ ghaṭīnāṃ maṇḍalaṃ mahat
BL308/ pītaṃ na gamayet svargaṃ kiṃ tat kratugataṃ nayet
B1.155/ rūpādayo yathā dṛṣṭāḥ pratyarthaṃ yataśaktayaḥ
BL310/ śabdās tathaiva dṛśyante viṣāpaharaṇādiṣu
B1.156/ yathaiṣāṃ tatra sāmarthyaṃ dharme+apy evaṃ pratīyatām
BL312/ sādhūnāṃ sādhubhis tasmād vācyam abhyudayārthinām
B1.157/ sarvo+adṛṣṭaphalān arthān āgamāt pratipadyate
BL314/ viparītaṃ ca sarvatra śakyate vaktum āgame
B1.158/ sādhutvajñānaviṭayā seyaṃ vyākaraṇasmṛtiḥ
BL316/ avicchedena śiṣṭānām idaṃ smṛtinibandhanam
B1.159/ vaikharyā madhyamāyāś ca paśyantyāś caitad adbhutam
BL318/ anekatīrthabhedāyās trayyā vācaḥ paraṃ padam
B1.160/ gaur iva prakṣaraty ekā rasam uttamaśālinī
BL320/ divyādivyena rūpeṇa bhāratī gauḥ śucismitā
B1.161/ etayor antaraṃ paśya sūkṣmayoḥ spandamānayoḥ
BL322/ prāṇāpānāntare nityam ekā sarvasya tiṣṭhati
B1.162/ anyā tv apreryamāṇaiva vinā prāṇena vartate
BL324/ jāyate hi tataḥ prāṇo vācam āpyāyayan punaḥ
B1.163/ prāṇenāpyāyitā saivaṃ vyavahāranibandhanī
BL326/ sarvasyocchvāsam āsādya na vāg vadati karhi cit
B1.164/ ghoṣiṇī jātanirghoṣā aghoṣā ca pravartate
BL328/ tayor api ca ghoṣiṇyā nirghoṣaiva garīyasī
B1.165/ sthāneṣu vivṛte vāyau kṛtavarṇaparigrahā
BL330/ vaikharī vāk prayoktṝṇāṃ prāṇavṛttinibandhanā
B1.166/ kevalaṃ buddhyupādāna- kramarūpānupātinī
BL332/ prāṇavṛttim atikramya madhyamā vāk pravartate
B1.167/ avibhāgā tu paśyantī sarvataḥ saṃhṛtakramā
BL334/ svarūpajyotir evāntaḥ sūkṣmā vāg anapāyinī
B1.168/ pīyūṣāpūryamāṇāpi nityam āgantubhir malaiḥ
BL336/ antyā kaleva somasya nātyantam abhibhūyate
B1.169/ yasyāṃ dṛṣṭasvarūpāyām adhikāro nivartate
BL338/ puruṣe ṣoḍaśakale tām āhur amṛtāṃ kalām
B1.170/ prāptoparāgarūpā sā viplavair anuṣaṅgibhiḥ
BL340/ vaikharī sattvamātreva guṇair na vyavakīryate
B1.171/ tadvibhāgāvibhāgābhyāṃ kriyamāṇam avasthitam
BL342/ svabhāvajñais tu bhāvānāṃ dṛśyante śabdaśaktayaḥ
B1.172/ anādim avyavacchinnāṃ śrutim āhur akartṛkām
BL344/ śiṣṭair nibadhyamānā tu na vyavacchidyate smṛtiḥ
B1.173/ avibhāgād vivṛttānām abhikhyā svapnavac chrutau
BL346/ bhāvatattvaṃ tu vijñāya liṅgebhyo vihitā smṛtiḥ
B1.174/ kāyavāgbuddhiviṣayā ye malāḥ samavasthitāḥ
BL348/ cikitsālakṣaṇādhyātma- śāstrais teṣāṃ viśuddhayaḥ
B1.175/ śabdaḥ saṃskārahīno yo gaur iti prayuyukṣyate
BL350/ tam apabhraṃśam icchanti viśiṣṭārthaniveśinam
B1.176/ asvagoṇyādayaḥ śabdāḥ sādhavo viṣayāntare
BL352/ nimittabhedāt sarvatra sādhutvaṃ ca vyavasthitam
B1.177/ te sādhuṣv anumānena pratyayotpattihetavaḥ
BL354/ tādātmyam upagamyeva śabdārthasya prakāśakāḥ
B1.178/ na śiṣṭair anugamyante paryāyā iva sādhavaḥ
BL356/ te yataḥ smṛtiśāstreṇa tasmāt sākṣād avācakāḥ
B1.179/ aṃbvaṃbv iti yathā bālaḥ śikṣamāṇo+apabhāṣate
BL358/ avyaktaṃ tadvidāṃ tena vyaktau bhavati niścayaḥ
B1.180/ evaṃ sādhau prayoktavye yo+apabhraṃśaḥ prayujyate
BL360/ tena sādhuvyavahitaḥ kaś cid artho+abhidhīyate
B1.181/ pāraṃparyād apabhraṃśā viguṇeṣv abhidhātṛṣu
BL362/ prasiddhim āgatā yena teṣāṃ sādhur avācakaḥ
B1.182/ daivī vāg vyatikīrṇeyam aśaktair abhidhātṛbhiḥ
BL364/ anityadarśināṃ tv asmin vāde buddhiviparyayaḥ
B1.183/ ubhayeṣām avicchedād anyaśabdavivakṣayā
BL366/ yo+anyaḥ prayujyate śabdo na so+arthasyābhidhāyakaḥ

<B 2>

Vākyakāṇḍam
B2.1/ ākhyātaṃ śabdasaṃghāto jātiḥ saṃghātavartinī
BL368/ eko+anavayavaḥ śabdaḥ kramo buddhyanusaṃhṛtiḥ
B2.2/ padam ādyaṃ pṛthak sarvaṃ padaṃ sāpekṣam ity api
BL370/ vākyaṃ prati matir bhinnā bahudhā nyāyadarśinām
B2.3/ nighātādivyavasthārthaṃ śāstre yat paribhāṣitam
BL372/ sākāṅkṣāvayavaṃ tena na sarvaṃ tulyalakṣaṇam
B2.4/ sākāṅkṣāvayavaṃ bhede parānākāṅkṣaśabdakam
BL374/ karmapradhānaṃ guṇavad ekārthaṃ vākyam ucyate
B2.5/ saṃbodhanapadaṃ yac ca tat kriyāyā viśeṣakam
BL376/ vrajāni devadatteti nighāto+atra tathā sati
B2.6/ yathānekam api ktvāntaṃ tiṅantasya viśeṣakam
BL378/ tathā tiṅantaṃ tatrāhus tiṅantasya viśeṣakam
B2.7/ yathaika eva sarvārtha- prakāśaḥ pravibhajyate
BL380/ dṛśyabhedānukāreṇa vākyārthāvagamas tathā
B2.8/ citrasyaikasya rūpasya yathā bhedanidarśanaiḥ
BL382/ nīlādibhiḥ samākhyānaṃ kriyate bhinnalakṣaṇaiḥ
B2.9/ tathaivaikasya vākyasya nirākāṅkṣasya sarvataḥ
BL384/ śabdāntaraiḥ samākhyānaṃ sākāṅkṣair anugamyate
B2.10/ yathā pade vibhajyante prakṛtipratyayādayaḥ
BL386/ apoddhāras tathā vākye padānām upapadyate
B2.11/ varṇāntarasarūpatvaṃ varṇabhāgeṣu dṛśyate
BL388/ padāntarasarūpāś ca padabhāgā iva sthitāḥ
B2.12/ bhāgair anarthakair yuktā vṛṣabhodakayāvakāḥ
BL390/ anvayavyatirekau tu vyavahāranibandhanam
B2.13/ śabdasya na vibhāgo+asti kuto+arthasya bhaviṣyati
BL392/ vibhāgaiḥ prakriyābhedam avidvān pratipadyate
B2.14/ brāhmaṇārtho yathā nāsti kaś cid brāhmaṇakambale
BL394/ devadattādayo vākye tathaiva syur anarthakāḥ
B2.15/ sāmānyārthas tirobhūto na viśeṣe+avatiṣṭhate
BL396/ upāttasya kutas tyāgo nivṛttaḥ kvāvatiṣṭhatām
B2.16/ aśābdo yadi vākyārthaḥ padārtho+api tathā bhavet
BL398/ evaṃ sati ca saṃbandhaḥ śabdasyārthena hīyate
B2.17/ viśeṣaśabdāḥ keṣāṃ cit sāmānyapratirūpakāḥ
BL400/ śabdāntarābhisaṃbandhād vyajyante pratipattṛṣu
B2.18/ teṣāṃ tu kṛtsno vākyārthaḥ pratibhedaṃ samāpyate
BL402/ vyaktopavyañjanā siddhir arthasya pratipattṛṣu
B2.19/ sa vyaktaḥ kramavāñ śabda upāṃśu yam adhīyate
BL404/ akramas tu vitatyeva buddhir yatrāvatiṣṭhate
B2.20/ yathotkṣepaviśeṣe+api karmabhedo na gṛhyate
BL406/ āvṛttau vyajyate jātiḥ karmabhir bhramaṇādibhiḥ
B2.21/ varṇavākyapadeṣv evaṃ tulyopavyañjanā śrutiḥ
BL408/ atyantabhede tattvasya sarūpeva pratīyate
B2.22/ nityeṣu ca kutaḥ pūrvaṃ paraṃ vā paramārthataḥ
BL410/ ekasyaiva tu sā śaktir yad evam avabhāsate
B2.23/ ciraṃ kṣipram iti jñāne kālabhedād ṛte yathā
BL412/ bhinnakāle prakāśete sa dharmo hrasvadīrghayoḥ
B2.24/ na nityaḥ kramamātrābhiḥ kālo bhedam ihārhati
BL414/ vyāvartinīnāṃ mātrāṇām abhāve kīdṛśaḥ kramaḥ
B2.25/ tābhyo yā jāyate buddhir ekā sā bhāgavarjitā
BL416/ sā hi svaśaktyā bhinneva kramapratyavamarśinī
B2.26/ kramollekhānuṣaṅgeṇa tasyāṃ yad bījam āhitam
BL418/ tattvanānātvayos tasya niruktir nāvatiṣṭhate
B2.27/ bhāvanāsamaye tv etat kramasāmarthyam akramam
BL420/ vyāvṛttabhedo yenārtho bhedavān upalabhyate
B2.28/ padāni vākye tāny eva varṇās te ca pade yadi
BL422/ varṇeṣu varṇabhāgānāṃ bhedaḥ syāt paramāṇuvat
B2.29/ bhāgānām anupaśleṣān na varṇo na padaṃ bhavet
BL424/ teṣām avyapadeśyatvāt kim anyad vyapadiśyatām
B2.30/ yad antaḥśabdatattvaṃ tu bhāgair ekaṃ prakāśitam
BL426/ tam āhur apare śabdaṃ tasya vākye tathaikatām
B2.31/ arthabhāgais tathā teṣām āntaro+arthaḥ prakāśyate
BL428/ asyaivātmano bhedau śabdārthāv apṛthaksthitau
B2.32/ prakāśakaprakāśyatvaṃ kāryakāraṇarūpatā
BL430/ antarmātrātmanas tasya śabdatattvasya sarvadā
B2.33/ tasyaivāstitvanāstitve sāmarthye samavasthite
BL432/ akrame kramanirbhāse vyavahāranibandhane
B2.34/ saṃpratyayapramāṇatvāt padārthāstitvakalpane
BL434/ padārthābhyuccaye tyāgād ānarthakyaṃ prasajyate
B2.35/ rājaśabdena rājārtho bhinnarūpeṇa gamyate
BL436/ vṛttāv ākhyātasadṛśaṃ padam anyat prayujyate
B2.36/ yathāśvakarṇa ity ukte vinaivāśvena gamyate
BL438/ kaś cid eva viśiṣṭo+arthaḥ sarveṣu pratyayas tathā
B2.37/ vākyeṣv arthāntaragateḥ sādṛśyaparikalpane
BL440/ keṣāṃ cid rūḍhiśabdatvaṃ śāstra evānugamyate
B2.38/ upādāyāpi ye heyās tān upāyān pracakṣate
BL442/ upāyānāṃ ca niyamo nāvaśyam avatiṣṭhate
B2.39/ arthaṃ kathaṃ cit puruṣaḥ kaś cit saṃpratipadyate
BL444/ saṃsṛṣṭā vā vibhaktā vā bhedā vākyanibandhanāḥ
B2.40/ so+ayam ity abhisaṃbandho buddhyā prakramyate yadā
BL446/ vākyārthasya tadaiko+api varṇaḥ pratyāyakaḥ kva cit
B2.41/ kevalena padenārtho yāvān evābhidhīyate
BL448/ vākyasthaṃ tāvato+arthasya tad āhur abhidhāyakam
B2.42/ saṃbandhe sati yat tv anyad ādhikyam upajāyate
BL450/ vākyārtham eva taṃ prāhur anekapadasaṃśrayam
B2.43/ sa tv anekapadastho+api pratibhedaṃ samāpyate
BL452/ jātivat samudāye+api saṃkhyāvat kalpyate+aparaiḥ
B2.44/ sarvabhedānuguṇyaṃ tu sāmānyam apare viduḥ
BL454/ tad arthāntarasaṃsargād bhajate bhedarūpatām
B2.45/ bhedān ākāṅkṣatas tasya yā pariplavamānatā
BL456/ avacchinatti saṃbandhas tāṃ viśeṣe niveśayan
B2.46/ kāryānumeyaḥ saṃbandho rūpaṃ tasya na vidyate
BL458/ asattvabhūtam atyantam atas taṃ pratijānate
B2.47/ niyataṃ sādhane sādhyaṃ kriyā niyatasādhanā
BL460/ sa saṃnidhānamātreṇa niyamaḥ saṃprakāśate
B2.48/ guṇabhāvena sākāṅkṣaṃ tatra nāma pravartate
BL462/ sādhyatvena nimittāni kriyāpadam apekṣate
B2.49/ santa eva viśeṣā ye padārtheṣu vyavasthitāḥ
BL464/ te kramād anugamyante na vākyam abhidhāyakam
B2.50/ śabdānāṃ kramamātre ca nānyaḥ śabdo+asti vācakaḥ
BL466/ kramo hi dharmaḥ kālasya tena vākyaṃ na vidyate
B2.51/ ye ca saṃbhavino bhedāḥ padārtheṣv avibhāvitāḥ
BL468/ saṃnidhāne vyajyante na tu varṇeṣv ayaṃ kramaḥ
B2.52/ varṇānāṃ ca padānāṃ ca kramamātraniveśinī
BL470/ padākhyā vākyasaṃjñā ca śabdatvaṃ neṣyate tayoḥ
B2.53/ samāne+api tu śabdatve dṛṣṭaḥ saṃpratyayaḥ padāt
BL472/ prativarṇaṃ tv asau nāsti padasyārtham ato viduḥ
B2.54/ yathā sāvayavā varṇā vinā vācyena kena cit
BL474/ arthavantaḥ samuditā vākyam apy evam iṣyate
B2.55/ anarthakāny apāyatvāt padārthenārthavanti vā
BL476/ krameṇoccaritāny āhur vākyārthaṃ bhinnalakṣaṇam
B2.56/ nityatve samudāyānāṃ jāter vā parikalpane
BL478/ ekasyaikārthatām āhur vākyasyāvyabhicāriṇīm
B2.57/ abhedapūrvakā bhedāḥ kalpitā vākyavādibhiḥ
BL480/ bhedapūrvān abhedāṃs tu manyante padadarśinaḥ
B2.58/ padaprakṛtibhāvaś ca vṛttibhedena varṇyate
BL482/ padānāṃ saṃhitā yoniḥ saṃhitā vā padāśrayā
B2.59/ padāmnāyaś ca yady anyaḥ saṃhitāyā nidarśakaḥ
BL484/ nityas tatra kathaṃ kāryaṃ padaṃ lakṣaṇadarśanāt
B2.60/ prativarṇam asaṃvedyaḥ padārthapratyayo yathā
BL486/ padeṣv evam asaṃvedyaṃ vākyārthasya nirūpaṇam
B2.61/ vākyārthaḥ saṃniviśate padeṣu sahavṛttiṣu
BL488/ yathā tathaiva varṇeṣu padārthaḥ sahavṛttiṣu
B2.62/ sūkṣmaṃ grāhyaṃ yathānyena saṃsṛṣṭaṃ saha gṛhyate
BL490/ varṇo+apy anyena varṇena saṃbaddho vācakas tathā
B2.63/ padasyoccāraṇād artho yathā kaś cin nirūpyate
BL492/ varṇānām api sāṃnidhyāt tathā so+arthaḥ pratīyate
B2.64/ prāptasya yasya sāmarthyān niyamārthā punaḥ śrutiḥ
BL494/ tenātyantaṃ viśeṣeṇa sāmānyaṃ yadi bādhyate
B2.65/ yajeteti tato dravyaṃ prāptaṃ sāmarthyalakṣaṇam
BL496/ vrīhiśrutyā nivarteta na syāt pratinidhis tathā
B2.66/ tasmād vrīhitvam adhikaṃ vrīhiśabdaḥ prakalpayet
BL498/ dravyatvam aviruddhatvāt prāptyarthaḥ san na bādhate
B2.67/ tena cāpi vyavacchinne dravyatve sahacāriṇi
BL500/ asaṃbhavād viseṣāṇaṃ tatrānyeṣam adarsanam
B2.68/ na ca sāmānyavat sarve kriyāśabdena lakṣitāḥ
BL502/ viśeṣā na hi sarveṣāṃ satāṃ śabdo+abhidhāyakaḥ
B2.69/ śuklādayo guṇāḥ santo yathā tatrāvivakṣitāḥ
BL504/ tathāvivakṣā bhedānaṃ dravyatvasahacāriṇām
B2.70/ asaṃnidhau pratinidhir mā bhūn nityasya karmaṇaḥ
BL506/ kāmyasya vā pravṛttasya lopa ity upapadyate
B2.71/ viśiṣṭaiva kriyā yena vākyārthaḥ parikalpyate
BL508/ dravyābhāve pratinidhau tasya tat syāt kriyāntaram
B2.72/ nirjñātārthaṃ padaṃ yac ca tadarthe pratipādite
BL510/ pikādi yad avijñātaṃ tat kim ity anuyujyate
B2.73/ sāmarthyaprāpitaṃ yac ca vyaktyartham anuṣajyate
BL512/ śrutir evānuṣaṅgeṇa bādhikā liṅgavākyayoḥ
B2.74/ aprāpto yas tu śuklādiḥ saṃnidhānena gamyate
BL514/ sa yatnaprāpito vākye śrutidharmavilakṣaṇaḥ
B2.75/ abhinnam eva vākyaṃ tu yady abhinnārtham iṣyate
BL516/ tat sarvaṃ śrutibhūtatvān na śrutyaiva virotsyate
B2.76/ vākyānāṃ samudāyaś ca ya ekārthaprasiddhaye
BL518/ sākāṅkṣāvayavas tatra vākyārtho+api na vidyate
B2.77/ prāsaṅgikam idaṃ kāryam idaṃ tantreṇa labhyate
BL520/ idam āvṛttibhedābhyām atra bādhasamuccayau
B2.78/ ūho+asmin viṣaye nyāyyaḥ saṃbandho+asya na bādhyat
BL522/ sāmānyasyātideśo+ayaṃ viśeṣo+atrātidiśyate
B2.79/ arthitvam atra sāmarthyam asminn artho na bhidyate
BL524/ śāstrāt prāptādhikāro+ayaṃ vyudāso+asya kriyāntare
B2.80/ iyaṃ śrutyā kramaprāptir iyam uccāraṇād iti
BL526/ kramo+ayam atra balavān asmiṃs tu na vivakṣitaḥ
B2.81/ idaṃ parāṅgaiḥ saṃbaddham aṅgānām aprayojakam
BL528/ prayojakam idaṃ teṣām atredaṃ nāntarīyakam
B2.82/ idaṃ pradhānaṃ śeṣo+ayaṃ viniyogakramas tv ayam
BL530/ sākṣād asyopakārīdam idam ārād viśeṣakam
B2.83/ śaktivyāpārabhedo+asmin phalam atra tu bhidyate
BL532/ saṃbandhāj jñānabhedo+ayaṃ bhedas tatrāvivakṣitaḥ
B2.84/ prasajyapratiṣedho+ayaṃ paryudāso+ayam atra tu
BL534/ idaṃ gauṇam idaṃ mukhyaṃ vyāpīdaṃ guru laghv idam
B2.85/ bhedenāṅgāṅgibhāvo+asya bahudhedaṃ vikalpyate
BL536/ idaṃ niyamyate+asyātra yogyatvam upajāyate
B2.86/ asya vākyāntare dṛṣṭāl liṅgād bhedo+anumīyate
BL538/ ayaṃ śabdair apoddhṛtya padārthaḥ pravibhajyate
B2.87/ iti vākyeṣu ye dharmāḥ padārthopanibandhanāḥ
BL540/ te sarve na prakalperan padaṃ cet syād avācakam
B2.88/ avibhakte+api vākyārthe śaktibhedād apoddhṛte
BL542/ vākyāntaravibhāgena yathoktaṃ na virudhyate
B2.89/ yathaivaikasya gandhasya bhedena parikalpanā
BL544/ puṣpādiṣu tathā vākye+apy arthabhedo+abhidhīyate
B2.90/ gavaye narasiṃhe cāpy ekajñānādṛte yathā
BL546/ bhāgaṃ jātyantarasyaiva sadṛśaṃ pratipadyate
B2.91/ aprasiddhaṃ tu yaṃ bhāgam adṛṣṭam anupaśyati
BL548/ tāvaty asaṃvidaṃ mūḍhaḥ sarvatra pratipadyate
B2.92/ tathā pikādiyogena vākye +atyantavilakṣaṇe
BL550/ sadṛśasyaiva saṃjñānam asato+arthasya manyate
B2.93/ ekasya bhāge sādṛśyaṃ bhāge bhedaś ca lakṣyate
BL552/ nirbhāgasya prakāśasya nirbhāgeṇaiva cetasā
B2.94/ tathaiva bhāge sādṛśyaṃ bhāge bhedo+avasīyate
BL554/ bhāgābhāve+api vākyānām atyantaṃ bhinnadharmaṇām
B2.95/ rūpanāśe padānāṃ syāt kathaṃ cāvadhikalpanā
BL556/ agṛhītāvadhau śabde kathaṃ cārtho vivicyate
B2.96/ saṃsarga iva rūpāṇāṃ śabde+anyatra vyavasthitaḥ
BL558/ nānārūpeṣu tad rūpaṃ tantreṇāparam iṣyate
B2.97/ tasminn abhede bhedānāṃ saṃsarga upavartate
BL560/ rūpaṃ rūpāntarāt tasmād ananyat pravibhajyate
B2.98/ śāstre pratyāyakasyāpi kva cid ekatvam āśritam
BL562/ pratyāyyena kva cid bhedo grahaṇagrāhyayoḥ sthitaḥ
B2.99/ ū ity abhedam āśritya yathāsaṃkhyaṃ prakalpitam
BL564/ lṛluṭor grahaṇe bhedo grāhyābhyāṃ saha kalpitaḥ
B2.100/ yasyety etad aṇo rūpaṃ saṃjñinām abhidhāyakam
BL566/ na hi pratīyamānena grahaṇasyāsti saṃbhavaḥ
B2.101/ ū ity etad abhinnaṃ ca bhinnavākyanibandhanam
BL568/ bhedena grahaṇaṃ yasya pararūpam iva dvayoḥ
B2.102/ plutasyāṅgavivṛddhiṃ ca samāhāram acos tathā
BL570/ vyudasyatā punar bhedaḥ śabdeṣv atyantam āśritaḥ
B2.103/ ardharcādiṣu śabdeṣu rūpabhedaḥ kramād yathā
BL572/ tantrāt tathaikaśabdatve bhinnānāṃ śrutir anyathā
B2.104/ saṃhitāviṣaye varṇāḥ svarūpeṇāvikāriṇaḥ
BL574/ śabdāntaratvaṃ yāntīva śaktyantaraparigrahāt
B2.105/ indriyādivikāreṇa dṛṣṭaṃ grāhyeṣu vastuṣu
BL576/ ātmatyāgād ṛte bhinnaṃ grahaṇaṃ sa kramaḥ śrutau
B2.106/ abhidhānakriyābhedāc chabdeṣv avikṛteṣv api
BL578/ rūpam atyantabhedena tad evaikaṃ prakāśate
B2.107/ ṛco vā gītimātraṃ vā sāma dravyāntaraṃ na tu
BL580/ gītibhedāt tu gṛhyante tā eva vikṛtā ṛcaḥ
B2.108/ upāyāc chrutisaṃhāre bhinnānām ekaśeṣiṇām
BL582/ antreṇoccāraṇe teṣāṃ śāstre sādhutvam ucyate
B2.109/ parigṛhya śrutiṃ caikāṃ rūpabhedavatām api
BL584/ tantreṇoccāraṇaṃ kāryam anyathā te na sādhavaḥ
B2.110/ sarūpāṇāṃ ca vākyānāṃ śāstreṇāpratipāditam
BL586/ tantreṇoccāraṇād ekaṃ rūpaṃ sādhūpalabhyate
B2.111/ ekasyānekarūpatvaṃ nālikādiparigrahāt
BL588/ yathā tathaiva tantrāt syād bahūnām ekarūpatā
B2.112/ yathā padasarūpāṇāṃ vākyānāṃ saṃbhavaḥ pṛthak
BL590/ tathā vākyāntarābhāve syād eṣāṃ pṛthagarthatā
B2.113/ abhidheyaḥ padasyārtho vākyasyārthaḥ prayojanam
BL592/ yasya tasya na saṃbandho vākyānām upapadyate
B2.114/ tatra kriyāpadāny eva vyapekṣante parasparam
BL594/ kriyāpadānuṣaktas tu saṃbandho+atha pratīyate
B2.115/ āvṛttir anuvādo vā padārthavyaktikalpane
BL596/ pratyekaṃ tu samāpto+arthaḥ sahabhūteṣu vartate
B2.116/ avikalpitavākyārthe vikalpā bhāvanāśrayāḥ
BL598/ atrādhikaraṇe vādāḥ pūrveṣāṃ bahudhā matāḥ
B2.117/ abhyāsāt pratibhāhetuḥ sarvaḥ śabdo+aparaiḥ smṛtaḥ
BL600/ bālānāṃ ca tiraścāṃ ca yathārthapratipādane
B2.118/ anāgamaś ca so+abhyāsaḥ samayaḥ kaiś cid iṣyate
BL602/ anantaram idaṃ kāryam asmād ity upadarśakaḥ
B2.119/ asty arthaḥ sarvaśabdānāṃ iti pratyāyyalakṣaṇam
BL604/ apūrvadevatāsvargaiḥ samam āhur gavādiṣu
B2.120/ prayogadarśanābhyāsād ākārāvagrahas tu yaḥ
BL606/ na sa śabdasya viṣayaḥ sa hi yatnāntarāśrayaḥ
B2.121/ ke cid bhedāḥ prakāśyante śabdais tadabhidhāyibhiḥ
BL608/ anuniṣpādinaḥ kāṃś cic chabdārthān iti manyate
B2.122/ jāteḥ pratyāyake śabde yā vyaktir anuṣaṅgiṇī
BL610/ na tadvyaktigatān bhedāñ jātiśabdo+avalambate
B2.123/ ghaṭādīnāṃ na cākārān pratyāyayati vācakaḥ
BL612/ vastumātraniveśitvāt tadgatir nāntarīyakā
B2.124/ kriyā vinā prayogeṇa na dṛṣṭā śabdacoditā
BL614/ prayogas tv anuniṣpādī śabdārtha iti gamyate
B2.125/ niyatās tu prayogā ye niyataṃ yac ca sādhanam
BL616/ teṣāṃ śabdābhidheyatvam aparair anugamyate
B2.126/ samudāyo+abhidheyo vāpy avikalpasamuccayaḥ
BL618/ asatyo vāpi saṃsargaḥ śabdārthaḥ kaiś cid iṣyate
B2.127/ asatyopādhi yat satyaṃ tad vā śabdanibandhanām
BL620/ śabdo vāpy abhijalpatvam āgato yāti vācyataṃ
B2.128/ so+ayam ity abhisaṃbandhād rūpam ekīkṛtaṃ yadā
BL622/ śabdasyārthena taṃ śabdam abhijalpaṃ pracakṣate
B2.129/ tayor apṛthagātmatve rūḍhir avyabhicāriṇī
BL624/ kiṃ cid eva kva cid rūpaṃ prādhānyenāvatiṣṭhate
B2.130/ loke+artharūpatāṃ śabdaḥ pratipannaḥ pravartate
BL626/ śāstre tūbhayarūpatvaṃ pravibhaktaṃ vivakṣayā
B2.131/ aśakteḥ sarvaśakter vā śabdair eva prakalpitā
BL628/ ekasyārthasya niyatā kriyādiparikalpanā
B2.132/ yo vārtho buddhiviṣayo bāhyavastunibandhanaḥ
BL630/ sa bāhyaṃ vastv iti jñātaḥ śabdārtha iti gamyate
B2.133/ ākāravantaḥ saṃvedyā vyaktismṛtinibandhanāḥ
BL632/ ete pratyavabhāsante saṃvinmātraṃ tv ato+anyathā
B2.134/ yathendriyaṃ saṃnipatad vaicitryeṇopadarśakam
BL634/ tathaiva śabdād arthasya pratipattir anekadhā
B2.135/ vaktrānyathaiva prakrānto bhinneṣu pratipattṛṣu
BL636/ svapratyayānukāreṇa śabdārthaḥ pravibhajyate
B2.136/ asminn api dṛśye+arthe darśanaṃ bhidyate pṛthak
BL638/ kālāntareṇa caiko+api taṃ paśyaty anyathā punaḥ
B2.137/ ekasyāpi ca śabdasya nimittair avyavasthitaiḥ
BL640/ ekena bahubhiś cārtho bahudhā parikalpyate
B2.138/ tasmād adṛṣṭatattvānāṃ sāparādhaṃ bahucchalam
BL642/ darśanaṃ vacanaṃ vāpi nityam evānavasthitam
B2.139/ ṛṣīṇāṃ darśanaṃ yac ca tattve kiṃ cid avasthitam
BL644/ na tena vyavahāro+asti na tac chabdanibandhanam
B2.140/ talavad dṛśyate vyoma khadyoto havyavāḍ iva
BL646/ naiva cāsti talaṃ vyomni na khadyote hutāśanaḥ
B2.141/ tasmāt pratyakṣam apy arthaṃ vidvān īkṣeta yuktitaḥ
BL648/ na darśanasya prāmāṇyād dṛśyam arthaṃ prakalpayet
B2.142/ asamākhyeyatattvānām arthānāṃ laukikair yathā
BL650/ vyavahāre samākhyānaṃ tat prajño na vikalpayet
B2.143/ vicchedagrahaṇe+arthānāṃ pratibhānyaiva jāyate
BL652/ vākyārtha iti tām āhuḥ ē̃ padārthair upapāditām
B2.144/ idaṃ tad iti sānyeṣām anākhyeyā kathaṃ ca na
BL654/ pratyātmavṛtti siddhā sā kartrāpi na nirūpyate
B2.145/ upaśleṣam ivārthānāṃ sā karoty avicāritā
BL656/ sārvarūpyam ivāpannā viṣayatvena vartate
B2.146/ sākṣāc chabdena janitāṃ bhāvanānugamena vā
BL658/ itikartavyatāyāṃ tāṃ na kaś cid ativartate
B2.147/ pramāṇatvena tāṃ lokaḥ sarvaḥ samanugacchati
BL660/ samārambhāḥ pratāyante tiraścām api tadvaśāt
B2.148/ yathā dravyaviśeṣāṇāṃ paripākair ayatnajāḥ
BL662/ madādiśaktayo dṛṣṭāḥ pratibhās tadvatāṃ tathā
B2.149/ svaravṛttiṃ vikurute madhau puṃskokilasya kaḥ
BL664/ jantvādayaḥ kulāyādi- karaṇe śikṣitāḥ katham
B2.150/ āhāraprītyapadveṣa- plavanādikriyāsu kaḥ
BL666/ jātyanvayaprasiddhāsu prayoktā mṛgapakṣiṇām
B2.151/ bhāvanānugatād etad āgamād eva jāyate
BL668/ āsattiviprakarṣābhyām āgamas tu viśiṣyate
B2.152/ svabhāvavaraṇābhyāsa- yogādṛṣṭopapāditām
BL670/ viśiṣṭopahitāṃ ceti pratibhāṃ ṣaḍvidhāṃ viduḥ
B2.153/ yathā saṃyogibhir dravyair lakṣite+arthe prayujyate
BL672/ gośabdo na tv asau teṣāṃ viśeṣāṇāṃ prakāśakaḥ
B2.154/ ākāravarṇāvayavaiḥ saṃsṛṣṭeṣu gavādiṣu
BL674/ śabdaḥ pravartamāno+api na tān aṅgīkaroty asau
B2.155/ saṃsthānavarṇāvayavair viśiṣṭe+arthe prayujyate
BL676/ śabdo na tasyāvayave pravṛttir upalabhyate
B2.156/ durlabhaṃ kasya cil loke sarvāvayavadarśanam
BL678/ kaiś cit tv avayavair dṛṣṭair arthaḥ kṛtsno+anumīyate
B2.157/ tathā jātyutpalādīnāṃ gandhena sahacāriṇām
BL680/ nityasaṃbandhināṃ dṛṣṭaṃ guṇānām avadhāraṇam
B2.158/ saṃkhyāpramāṇasaṃsthāna- nirapekṣaḥ pravartate
BL682/ bindau ca samudāye ca vācakaḥ salilādiṣu
B2.159/ saṃskārādiparicchinne tailādau yo vyavasthitaḥ
BL684/ āhaikadeśaṃ tattvena tasyāvayavavartinā
B2.160/ yenārthenābhisaṃbaddham abhidhānaṃ prayujyate
BL686/ tadarthāpagame tasya prayogo vinivartate
B2.161/ yāṃs tu saṃbhavino dharmān antarṇīya prayujyate
BL688/ śabdas teṣāṃ na sāṃnidhyaṃ niyamena vyapekṣate
B2.162/ yathā romaśaphādīnāṃ vyabhicāre+api dṛśyate
BL690/ gośabdo na tathā jāter viprayoge pravartate
B2.163/ tasmāt saṃbhavino+arthasya śabdāt saṃpratyaye sati
BL692/ adṛṣṭaviprayogārthaḥ saṃbandhitvena gamyate
B2.164/ vācikā dyotikā vā syur dvitvādīnāṃ vibhaktayaḥ
BL694/ syād vā saṃkhyāvato+arthasya samudāyo+abhidhāyakaḥ
B2.165/ vinā saṃkhyābhidhānād vā saṃkhyābhedasamanvitān
BL696/ arthān svarūpabhedena kāṃś cid āhur gavādayaḥ
B2.166/ ye śabdā nityasaṃbandhā viveke jñātaśaktayaḥ
BL698/ anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ teṣām artho vibhajyate
B2.167/ yāvac cāvyabhicāreṇa tayoḥ śakyaṃ prakalpanam
BL700/ niyamas tatra na tv evaṃ niyamo nuṭśabādiṣu
B2.168/ saṃbhave nābhidhānasya lakyaṇatvaṃ prakalpate
BL702/ āpekṣikyo hi saṃsarge niyatāḥ śabdaśaktayaḥ
B2.169/ kūpasūpayūpānām anvayo+arthasya dṛśyate
BL704/ ato+arthāntaravācitvaṃ saṃghātasyaiva gamyate
B2.170/ anvākhyānāni bhidyante śabdavyutpattikarmasu
BL706/ bahūnāṃ saṃbhave+arthānāṃ nimittaṃ kiṃ cid iṣyate
B2.171/ vairavāsiṣṭhagiriśās tathaikāgārikāda yaḥ
BL708/ kaiś cit kathaṃ cid ākhyātā nimittāvadhisaṃkaraiḥ
B2.172/ yathā pathaḥ samākhyānaṃ vṛkṣavalmīkaparvataiḥ
BL710/ aviruddhaṃ gavādīnāṃ bhinnaiś ca sahacāribhiḥ
B2.173/ anyathā ca samākhyānam avasthābhedadarśibhiḥ
BL712/ kriyate kiṃśukādīnām ekadeśāvadhāraṇam
B2.174/ kaiś cin nirvacanaṃ bhinnaṃ girater garjater gameḥ
BL714/ gavater gadater vāpi gaur ity atrānudarśitam
B2.175/ gaur ity eva svarūpād vā gośabdo goṣu vartate
BL716/ vyutpādyate na vā sarvaṃ kaiś cic cobhayatheṣyate
B2.176/ sāmānyenopadeśaś ca śāstre laghvartham āśritaḥ
BL718/ jātyantaravad anyasya viśeṣāḥ pratipādakāḥ
B2.177/ arthāntare ca yad vṛttaṃ tat prakṛtyantaraṃ viduḥ
BL720/ tulyarūpaṃ na tad rūḍhāv anyasminn anuṣajyate
B2.178/ bhinnāv ijiyajī dhātū niyatau viṣayāntare
BL722/ kaiś cit kathaṃ cid uddiṣṭau citraṃ hi pratipādanam
B2.179/ evaṃ ca vālavāyādi jitvarīvad upācaret
BL724/ bhedābhedābhyupagame na virodho+asti kaś ca na
B2.180/ aḍādīnāṃ vyavasthārthaṃ pṛthaktvena prakalpanam
BL726/ dhātūpasargayoḥ śāstre dhātur eva tu tādṛśaḥ
B2.181/ tathā hi saṃgrāmayateḥ sopasargād vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ
BL728/ kriyāviśeṣāḥ saṃghāte prakramyante tathāvidhāḥ
B2.182/ kāryāṇām antaraṅgatvam evaṃ dhātūpasargayoḥ
BL730/ sādhanair yāti saṃbandhaṃ tathābhūtaiva sā kriyā
B2.183/ prayogārheṣu siddhaḥ san bhettavyo+artho viśiṣyate
BL732/ prāk ca sādhanasaṃbandhāt kriyā naivopajāyate
B2.184/ dhātoḥ sādhanayogasya bhāvinaḥ prakramād yathā
BL734/ dhātutvaṃ karmabhāvaś ca tathānyad api dṛśyatām

BL736/ varṇādipariṇāmena phalānām upakurvate

B2.191/ tathaiva svārthikāḥ ke cit saṃghātāntaravṛttayaḥ
BL748/ anarthakena saṃsṛṣṭāḥ prakṛtyarthānuvādinaḥ

BL752/ teṣu prayujyamāneyu bhinnārtheṣv api sarvathā

BL754/ pratyayo vācakatve+api kevalo na prayujyate

BL756/ asattvabhūto bhāvaś ca tiṅpadair abhidhīyate

BL758/ guṇaiḥ padānāṃ saṃbandhaḥ paratantrās tu cādayaḥ

BL766/ yujyate vir yathā tasya likhāv anupasargatā

BL774/ nāpi kriyāpadākṣepī saṃbandhasya tu bhedakaḥ
B2.205/ anarthakānāṃ saṃghātaḥ sārthako+anarthakas tathā

BL986/ jñāyatāṃ kāla ity etat sopāyam abhidhīyate
B2.311/ vidhyaty adhanuṣety atra viśeṣeṇa nidarśyate
