BL1232/ paktiḥ karaṇarūpaṃ tu sādhyatvena pratīyate
B2.434/ yo+aṃśo yenopakāreṇa prayoktṝṇāṃ vivakṣitaḥ
BL1234/ arthasya sarvaśaktitvāt sa tathaiva vyavasthitaḥ
B2.435/ ārādvṛttiṣu saṃbandhaḥ kadā cid abhidhīyate
BL1236/ āśliṣṭo yo+anupaśliṣṭaḥ sa kadā cit pratīyate
B2.436/ saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ vibhaktatvaṃ saṃsargaś ca vivekinām
BL1238/ nānātmakānām ekatvaṃ nānātvaṃ ca viparyaye
B2.437/ sarvātmakatvād arthasya nairātmyād vā vyavasthitam
BL1240/ atyantayataśaktitvāc chabda eva nibandhanam
B2.438/ vastūpalakṣaṇaḥ śabdo nopakārasya vācakaḥ
BL1242/ na svaśaktiḥ padārthānāṃ saṃspraṣṭuṃ tena śakyate
B2.439/ saṃbandhidharmā saṃyogaḥ svaśabdenābhidhīyate
BL1244/ saṃbandhaḥ samavāyas tu saṃbandhitvena gamyate
B2.440/ lakṣaṇād vyavatiṣṭhante padārthā na tu vastutaḥ
BL1246/ upakārāt sa evārthaḥ kathaṃ cid anugamyate
B2.441/ vākyārtho yo+abhisaṃbandho na tasyātmā kva cit sthitaḥ
BL1248/ vyavahāre padārthānāṃ tam ātmānaṃ pracakṣate
B2.442/ padārthe samudāye vā samāpto naiva vā kva cit
BL1250/ padārtharūpabhedena tasyātmā pravibhajyate
B2.443/ anvākhyānāya yo bhedaḥ pratipattinibandhanam
BL1252/ sākāṅkṣāvayavaṃ bhede tenānyad upavarṇyate
B2.444/ anekaśakter ekasya pravibhāgo+anugamyate
BL1254/ ekārthatvaṃ hi vākyasya mātrayāpi pratīyate
B2.445/ saṃpratyayārthād bāhyo+arthaḥ sann asan vā vibhajyate
BL1256/ bāhyīkṛtya vibhāgas tu śaktyapoddhāralakṣaṇaḥ
B2.446/ pratyayārthātmaniyatāḥ śaktayo na vyavasthitāḥ
BL1258/ anyatra ca tato rūpaṃ na tāsām upalabhyate
B2.447/ bahuṣv api tiṅanteṣu sākāṅkṣeṣv ekavākyatā
BL1260/ tiṅā tiṅbhyo nighātasya paryudāsas tathārthavān
B2.448/ ekatiṅ yasya vākyaṃ tu śāstre niyatalakṣaṇam
BL1262/ tasyātiṅgrahaṇenārtho vākyabhedān na vidyate
B2.449/ tiṅantāntarayukteṣu yuktayukteṣu vā punaḥ
BL1264/ mṛgaḥ paśyata yātīti bhedābhedau na tiṣṭhataḥ
B2.450/ itikartavyatārthasya sāmarthyād yatra kāṅkṣyate
BL1266/ aśabdalakṣaṇākāṅkṣaṃ samāptārthaṃ tad ucyate
B2.451/ tattvānvākhyānamātre tu yāvān artho+anuṣajyate
BL1268/ vināpi tatprayogeṇa śruter vākyaṃ samāpyate
B2.452/ caṅkramyamāṇo+adhīṣvātra japaṃś caṅkramaṇaṃ kuru
BL1270/ tādarthyasyāviśeṣe+api śabdādbhedaḥ pratīyate
B2.453/ phalavantaḥ kriyābhedāḥ kriyāntaranibandhanāḥ
BL1272/ asaṃkhyātāḥ kramoddeśair ekākhyātanidarśitāḥ
B2.454/ nivṛttabhedā sarvaiva kriyākhyāte+abhidhīyate
BL1274/ śruter aśakyā bhedānāṃ pravibhāgaprakalpanā
B2.455/ aśvamedhena yakṣyante rājānaḥ sattram āsate
BL1276/ brāhmaṇā iti nākhyāta- rūpād bhedaḥ pratīyate
B2.456/ sakṛc chrutā saptadaśasv anāvṛttāpi yā kriyā
BL1278/ prājāpatyeṣu sāmarthyāt sā bhedaṃ pratipadyate
B2.457/ devadattādiṣu bhujiḥ pratyekam avatiṣṭhate
BL1280/ pratisvatantraṃ vākyaṃ vā bhedena pratipadyate
B2.458/ uccāraṇe tu vākyānām anyad rūpaṃ na gṛhyate
BL1282/ pratipattau tu bhinnānām anyad rūpaṃ pratīyate
B2.459/ ekaṃ grahaṇavākyaṃ ca sāmānyenābhidhīyate
BL1284/ kartarīti yathā tac ca paśvādiṣu vibhajyate
B2.460/ yady ākāṅkṣā nivarteta tadbhūtasya sakṛc chrutau
BL1286/ naivānyenābhisaṃbandhaṃ tad upeyāt kathaṃ ca na
B2.461/ ekarūpam anekārthaṃ tasmād upanibandhanam
BL1288/ yonir vibhāgavākyānāṃ tebhyo+ananyad iva sthitam
B2.462/ kva cit kriyā vyaktibhāgair upakāre pravartate
BL1290/ sāmānyabhāga evāsyāḥ kva cid arthasya sādhakaḥ
B2.463/ kālabhinnāś ca ye bhedā ye cāpy uṣṭrāsikādiṣu
BL1292/ prakrame jātibhāgasya śabdātmā tair na bhidyate
B2.464/ ekasaṃkhyeṣu bhedeṣu bhinnā jātyādibhiḥ kriyāḥ
BL1294/ bhedena viniyujyante tacchabdasya sakṛc chrutau
B2.465/ akṣādiṣu yathā bhinnā bhakṣibhañjidivikriyāḥ
BL1296/ prayogakālābhede+api pratibhedaṃ pṛthak sthitāḥ
B2.466/ akṣāṇāṃ tantriṇāṃ tantram upāyas tulyarūpatā
BL1298/ eṣāṃ kramo vibhaktānāṃ tannibaddhā sakṛc chrutiḥ
B2.467/ dvāv apy upāyau śabdānāṃ prayoge samavasthitau
BL1300/ kramo vā yaugapadyaṃ vā yau loko nātivartate
B2.468/ krame vibhajyate rūpaṃ yaugapadye na bhidyate
BL1302/ kriyā tu yaugapadye+api kramarūpānupātinī
B2.469/ bhedasaṃsargaśaktī dve śabdād bhinne iva sthite
BL1304/ yaugapadye+apy anekena prayoge bhidyate śrutiḥ
B2.470/ abhinno rūpabhedena ya eko+artho vivakṣitaḥ
BL1306/ tasyāvayavadharmeṇa samudāyo+anugṛhyate
B2.471/ bhedanirvacane tv asya pratyekaṃ vā samāpyate
BL1308/ śrutir vacanabhinnā vā vākyabhede+avatiṣṭhate
B2.472/ tatraikavacanānto vā so+akṣaśabdaḥ prayujyate
BL1310/ pratyekaṃ vā bahutvena pravibhāgo yathāśruti
B2.473/ dviṣṭhāni yāni vākyāni teṣv apy ekatvadarśinām
BL1312/ anekaśakter ekasya svaśaktiḥ pravibhajyate
B2.474/ atyantabhinnayor vā syāt prayoge tantralakṣaṇaḥ
BL1314/ upāyas tatra saṃsargaḥ pratipattṛṣu bhidyate
B2.475/ bhedenādhigatau pūrvaṃ śabdau tulyaśrutī punaḥ
BL1316/ tantreṇa pratipattāraḥ prayoktrā pratipāditāḥ
B2.476/ ekasyāpi vivakṣāyām anuniṣpadyate paraḥ
BL1318/ vinābhisaṃdhinā śabdaḥ śaktirūpaḥ prakāśate
B2.477/ anekā śaktir ekasya yugapac chrūyate kva cit
BL1320/ agniḥ prakāśadāhābhyām ekatrāpi niyujyate
B2.478/ āvṛttiśaktibhinnārthe vākye sakṛd api śrute
BL1322/ liṅgād vā tantradharmād vā vibhāgo vyavatiṣṭhate
B2.479/ saṃprasāraṇasaṃjñāyāṃ liṅgābhyāṃ varṇavākyayoḥ
BL1324/ pravibhāgas tathā sūtra ekasminn eva jāyate
B2.480/ tathā dvirvacane+acīti tantropāyād alakṣaṇaḥ
BL1326/ ekaśeṣeṇa nirdeśo bhāṣya eva pradarśitaḥ
B2.481/ prāyeṇa saṃkṣeparucīn alpavidyāparigrahān
BL1328/ saṃprāpya vaiyākaraṇān saṃgrahe+astam upāgate
B2.482/ kṛte+atha pātañjalinā guruṇā tīrthadarśinā
BL1330/ sarveṣāṃ nyāyabījānāṃ mahābhāṣye nibandhane
B2.483/ alabdhagādhe gāmbhīryād uttāna iva sauṣṭhavāt
BL1332/ tasminn akṛtabuddhīnāṃ naivāvāsthita niścayaḥ
B2.484/ vaijisaubhavaharyakṣaiḥ śuṣkatarkānusāribhiḥ
BL1334/ ārṣe viplāvite granthe saṃgrahapratikañcuke
B2.485/ yaḥ pātañjaliśiṣyebhyo bhraṣṭo vyākaraṇāgamaḥ
BL1336/ kālena dākṣiṇātyeṣu granthamātro vyavasthitaḥ
B2.486/ parvatād āgamaṃ labdhvā bhāṣyabījānusāribhiḥ
BL1338/ sa nīto bahuśākhatvaṃ cāndrācāryādibhiḥ punaḥ
B2.487/ nyāyaprasthānamārgāṃs tān abhyasya svaṃ ca darśanam
BL1340/ praṇīto guruṇāsmākam ayam āgamasaṃgrahaḥ
B2.488/ vartmanām atra keṣāṃ cid vastumātram udāhṛtam
BL1342/ kāṇḍe tṛtīye nyakṣeṇa bhaviṣyati vicāraṇā
B2.489/ prajñā vivekaṃ labhate bhinnair āgamadarśanaiḥ
BL1344/ kiyad vā śakyam unnetuṃ svatarkam anudhāvatā
B2.490/ tat tad utprekṣamāṇānāṃ purāṇair āgamair vinā
BL1346/ anupāsitavṛddhānāṃ vidyā nātiprasīdati

<B 3>

Padakāṇḍam
<B 4>

3.1. jātisamuddeśaḥ
B3.1.1/ dvidhā kaiś cit padaṃ bhinnaṃ caturdhā pañcadhāpi vā
BL1348/ apoddhṛtyaiva vākyebhyaḥ prakṛtipratyayādivat
B3.1.2/ padārthānām apoddhāre jātir vā dravyam eva vā
BL1350/ padārthau sarvaśabdānāṃ nityāv evopavarṇitau
B3.1.3/ keṣāṃ cit sāhacaryeṇa jātiḥ śaktyupalakṣaṇam
BL1352/ khadirādiṣv aśakteṣu śaktaḥ pratinidhīyate
B3.1.4/ asvātantryaphalo bandhiḥ pramāṇādīva śiṣyate
BL1354/ ato jātyabhidhāne+api śaktihīnaṃ na gṛhyate
B3.1.5/ saṃśleṣamātraṃ badhnātir yadi syāt tu vivakṣitaḥ
BL1356/ śaktyāśraye tato liṅgaṃ pramāṇādyanuśāsanam
B3.1.6/ svajātiḥ prathamaṃ śabdaiḥ sarvair evābhidhīyate
BL1358/ tato+arthajātirūpeṣu tadadhyāropakalpanā
B3.1.7/ yathā rakte guṇe tattvaṃ kaṣāye vyapadiśyate
BL1360/ saṃyogisaṃnikarṣāc ca vastrādiṣv api gṛhyate
B3.1.8/ tathā śabdārthasaṃbandhāc chabde jātir avasthitā
BL1362/ vyapadeśe+arthajātīnāṃ jātikāryāya kalpate
B3.1.9/ jātjśabdaikaśeṣe sā jātīnāṃ jātir iṣyate
BL1364/ śabdajātaya ity atra tajjātiḥ śabdajātiṣu
B3.1.10/ yā śabdajātiśabdeṣu śabdebhyo bhinnalakṣaṇā
BL1366/ jātiḥ sā śabdajātitvam avyatikramya vartate
B3.1.11/ arthajātyabhidhāne+api sarve jātyabhidhāyinaḥ
BL1368/ vyāpāralakṣaṇā yasmāt padārthāḥ samavasthitāḥ
B3.1.12/ jātau padārthe jātir vā viseṣo vāpi jātivat
BL1370/ śabdair apekṣyate yasmād atas te jātivācinaḥ
B3.1.13/ dravyadharmā padārthe tu dravye sarvo+artha ucyate
BL1372/ dravyadharmāśrayād dravyam ataḥ sarvo+artha iṣyate
B3.1.14/ anupravṛttidharmo vā jātiḥ syāt sarvajātiṣu
BL1374/ vyāvṛttidharmasāmānyaṃ viśeṣe jātir iṣyate
B3.1.15/ saṃyogidharmabhedena deśe ca parikalpite
BL1376/ teṣu deśeṣu sāmānyam ākāśasyāpi vidyate
B3.1.16/ adeśānāṃ ghaṭādīnāṃ deśāḥ saṃbandhino yathā
BL1378/ ākāśasyāpy adeśasya deśāḥ saṃyoginas tathā
B3.1.17/ bhinnavastvāśrayā buddhiḥ saṃyogiṣv anuvartate
BL1380/ samavāyiṣu bhedasya grahaṇaṃ vinivartate
B3.1.18/ ataḥ saṃyogideśānāṃ gauṇatvaṃ parikalpyate
BL1382/ avivekāt pradeśebhyo mukhyatvaṃ samavāyinām
B3.1.19/ anupravṛttirūpā yā prakhyā tām ākṛtiṃ viduḥ
BL1384/ ke cid vyāvṛttirūpāṃ tu dravyatvena pracakṣate
B3.1.20/ bhinnā iti paropādhir abhinnā iti vā punaḥ
BL1386/ bhāvātmasu prapañco+ayaṃ saṃsṛṣṭeṣv eva jāyate
B3.1.21/ naikatvaṃ nāpi nānātvaṃ na sattvaṃ na ca nāstitā
BL1388/ ātmatattveṣu bhāvānām asaṃsṛṣṭeṣu vidyate
B3.1.22/ sarvaśaktyātmabhūtatvam ekasyaiveti nirṇaye
BL1390/ bhāvānām ātmabhedasya kalpanā syād anarthikā
B3.1.23/ tasmād dravyādayaḥ sarvāḥ śaktayo bhinnalakṣaṇāḥ
BL1392/ saṃsṛṣṭāḥ puruṣārthasya sādhikā na tu kevalāḥ
B3.1.24/ yathaiva cendriyādīnām ātmabhūtā samagratā
BL1394/ tathā saṃbandhisaṃbandha- saṃsarge+api pratīyate
B3.1.25/ na tad utpadyate kiṃ cid yasya jātir na vidyate
BL1396/ ātmābhivyaktaye jātiḥ kāraṇānāṃ prayojikā
B3.1.26/ kāraṇeṣu padaṃ kṛtvā nityānityeṣu jātayaḥ
BL1398/ kva cit kāryeṣv abhivyaktim upayānti punaḥ punaḥ
B3.1.27/ nirvartyamānaṃ yat karma jātis tatrāpi sādhanam
BL1400/ svāśrayasyābhinicpattyai sā kriyāyāḥ prayojikā
B3.1.28/ vidhau vā pratiṣedhe vā brāhmaṇatvādi sādhanam
BL1402/ vyaktyāśritāśritā jāteḥ saṃkhyājātir viśeṣikā
B3.1.29/ yathā jalādibhir vyaktaṃ mukham evābhidhīyate
BL1404/ tathā dravyair abhivyaktā jātir evābhidhīyate
B3.1.30/ yathendriyagato bheda indriyagrahaṇād ṛte
BL1406/ indriyārtheṣvadṛśyo+api jñānabhedāya kalpate
B3.1.31/ tathātmarūpagrahaṇāt keṣāṃ cid vyaktayo vinā
BL1408/ sāmānyajñānabhedānām upayānti nimittatām
B3.1.32/ satyāsatyau tu yau bhāgau pratibhāvaṃ vyavasthitau
BL1410/ satyaṃ yat tatra sā jātir asatyā vyaktayaḥ smṛtāḥ
B3.1.33/ saṃbandhibhedāt sattaiva bhidyamānā gavādiṣu
BL1412/ jātir ity ucyate tasyāṃ sarve śabdā vyavasthitāḥ
B3.1.34/ tāṃ prātipadikārthaṃ ca dhātvarthaṃ ca pracakṣate
BL1414/ sā nityā sā mahān ātmā tām āhus tvatalādayaḥ
B3.1.35/ prāptakramā viśeṣeṣu kriyā saivābhidhīyate
BL1416/ kramarūpasya saṃhāre tat sattvam iti kathyate
B3.1.36/ saiva bhāvavikāreu ṣaḍ avasthāḥ prapadyate
BL1418/ krameṇa śaktibhiḥ svābhir evaṃ pratyavabhāsate
B3.1.37/ ātmabhūtaḥ kramo+apy asyā yatredaṃ kāladarśanam
BL1420/ paurvāparyādirūpeṇa pravibhaktam iva sthitam
B3.1.38/ tirobhāvābhyupagame bhāvānāṃ saiva nāstitā
BL1422/ labdhakrame tirobhāve naśyatīti pratīyate
B3.1.39/ pūrvasmāt pracyutā dharmād aprāptā cottaraṃ padam
BL1424/ tadantarāle bhedānām āśrayāj janma kathyate
B3.1.40/ āśrayaḥ svātmamātrā vā bhāvā vā vyatirekiṇaḥ
BL1426/ svaśaktayo vā sattāyā bhedadarśanahetavaḥ
B3.1.41/ pṛthivyādiṣv abhivyaktau na saṃsthānam apekṣate
BL1428/ anucchinnāśrayāj jātir anitye+apy āśraye sthitā
B3.1.42/ anucchedyāśrayām eke sarvāṃ jātiṃ pracakṣate
BL1430/ na yaugapadyaṃ pralaye sarvasyeti vyavasthitāḥ
B3.1.43/ prakṛtau pravilīneṣu bhedeṣv ekatvadarśinām
BL1432/ dravyasattvaṃ prapadyante svāśrayā eva jātayaḥ
B3.1.44/ brāhmaṇatvādayo bhāvāḥ sarvaprāṇiṣv avasthitāḥ
BL1434/ abhivyaktāḥ svakāryāṇāṃ sādhakā ity api smṛtiḥ
B3.1.45/ citrādiṣv apy abhivyaktir jātīnāṃ kaiś cid iṣyate
BL1436/ prāṇyāśritās tu tāḥ prāptau nimittaṃ puṇyapāpayoḥ
B3.1.46/ jñānaṃ tv asmadviśiṣṭānāṃ tāsu sarvendriyaṃ viduḥ
BL1438/ abhyāsān maṇirūpyādi- viśeṣeṣv iva tadvidām
B3.1.47/ jātyutpalādigandhādau bhedatattvaṃ yad āśritam
BL1440/ tad bhāvapratyayair loke +anityatvān nābhidhīyate
B3.1.48/ asvaśabdābhidhānās tu narasiṃhādijātayaḥ
BL1442/ sarūpāvayavevānyā tāsu śrutir avasthitā
B3.1.49/ jātyavasthāparicchede saṃkhyā saṃkhyātvam eva vā
BL1444/ viprakarṣe+api saṃsargād upakārāya kalpate
B3.1.50/ lakṣaṇā śabdasaṃskāre vyāpāraḥ kāryasiddhaye
BL1446/ saṃkhyākarmādiśaktīnāṃ śrutisāmye+api dṛśyate
B3.1.51/ na vinā saṃkhyayā kaś cit sattvabhūto+artha ucyate
BL1448/ ataḥ sarvasya nirdeśe saṃkhyā syād avivakṣitā
B3.1.52/ ekatvaṃ vā bahutvaṃ vā keṣāṃ cid avivakṣitam
BL1450/ tad dhi jātyabhidhānāya dvitvaṃ tu syād vivakṣitam
B3.1.53/ yady etau vyādhitau syātāṃ deyaṃ syād idam auṣadham
BL1452/ ity evaṃ lakṣaṇe+arthasya dvitvaṃ syād avivakṣitam
B3.1.54/ ekādiśabdavācyāyāḥ karmasv aṅgatvam iṣyate
BL1454/ saṃkhyāyāḥ khanati dvābhyām iti rūpād dhi sāśritā
B3.1.55/ yajeta paśunety atra saṃskārasyāpi saṃbhave
BL1456/ yathā jātis tathaikatvaṃ sādhanatvena gamyate
B3.1.56/ liṅgāt tu syād dvitīyādes tad ekatvaṃ vivakṣitam
BL1458/ ekārthaviṣayatve ca talliṅgaṃ jātisaṃkhyayoḥ
B3.1.57/ anyatrāvihitasyaiva sa vidhiḥ prathamaṃ paśoḥ
BL1460/ kriyāyām aṅgabhāvaś ca tat tv etasmād vivakṣitam
B3.1.58/ grahās tv anyatra vihitā bhinnasaṃkhyāḥ pṛthak pṛthak
BL1462/ prājāpatyā navety evam- ādibhedasamanvitāḥ
B3.1.59/ aṅgatvena pratītānāṃ saṃmārge tv aṅgināṃ punaḥ
BL1464/ nirdeśaṃ prati yā saṃkhyā sā kathaṃ syād vivakṣitā
B3.1.60/ nānyatra vidhir astīti saṃskāro nāpi cāṅgitā
BL1466/ hetuḥ saṃkhyāvivakṣāyā yatnāt sā hi vivakṣitā
B3.1.61/ saṃmārjane viśeṣaś ca na grahe kva cid āśritaḥ
BL1468/ vihītās te ca saṃskāryāḥ sarveṣām āśrayas tataḥ
B3.1.62/ pratyāśrayaṃ samāptāyāṃ jātāv ekena cet kriyā
BL1470/ paśunā na prakalpeta tat syād eva prakalpanam
B3.1.63/ ekena ca prasiddhāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ yadi saṃbhavāt
BL1472/ paśvantaram upādeyam upādānam anarthakam
B3.1.64/ yathaivāhitagarbhāyāṃ garbhādhānam anarthakam
BL1474/ tathaikena prasiddhāyāṃ paśvantaram anarthakam
B3.1.65/ tāvatārthasya siddhatvād ekatvasyāvyatikramam
BL1476/ ke cid icchanti na tv atra saṃkhyāṅgatvena gṛhyate
B3.1.66/ dvitīyādi tu yal liṅgam uktanyāyānuvādi tat
BL1478/ nasaṃkhyā sādhanatvena jātivat tena gamyate
B3.1.67/ anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ saṃkhyābhyupagame sati
BL1480/ yuktaṃ yat sādhanatvaṃ syān na tv anyārthopalakṣaṇam
B3.1.68/ sādhanatve padārthasya sāmarthyaṃ na prahīyate
BL1482/ saṃkhyāvyāpāradharmo+atas tena liṅgena gamyate
B3.1.69/ apūrvasya vidheyatvāt prādhānyam avasīyate
BL1484/ vihitasya parārthatvāc cheṣabhāvaḥ pratīyate
B3.1.70/ saṃmārgasya vidheyatvād anyatra vihite grahe
BL1486/ vidhivākye śrutā saṃkhyā lakṣaṇāyāṃ na bādhyate
B3.1.71/ vidhivākyāntare saṃkhyā paśor nāsti virodhinī
BL1488/ tasmāt saguṇa evāsau sahaikatvena gamyate
B3.1.72/ nirjñātadravyasaṃbandhe yaḥ karmaṇy upadiśyate
BL1490/ guṇas tenārthitā tasya dravyeṇeva pratīyate
B3.1.73/ kaś cid eva guṇo dravye yathā sāmarthyalakṣaṇaḥ
BL1492/ ādhāro+api guṇasyaivaṃ prāptaḥ sāmarthyalakṣaṇaḥ
B3.1.74/ tayos tu pṛthagarthitve saṃbandho yaḥ pratīyate
BL1494/ na tasminn upaghāto+asti kalpyam anyan na cāśrutam
B3.1.75/ kriyayā yo+abhisaṃbandhaḥ sa śrutiprāpitas tayoḥ
BL1496/ āśrayāśrayiṇor vākyān niyamas tv avatiṣṭhate
B3.1.76/ tatra dravyaguṇābhāve pratyekaṃ syād vikalpanam
BL1498/ śrutiprāpto hi saṃbandho balavān vākyalakṣaṇāt
B3.1.77/ yadā tu jātiḥ śaktir vā kriyāṃ praty upadiśyate
BL1500/ sāmarthyāt saṃnidhīyete tatra dravyaguṇau tadā
B3.1.78/ jātīnāṃ ca guṇānāṃ ca tulye+aṅgatve kriyāṃ prati
BL1502/ guṇāḥ pratinidhīyante chāgādīnāṃ na jātayaḥ
B3.1.79/ vyaktiśakteḥ samāsannā jātayo na tathā guṇāḥ
BL1504/ sākṣād dravyaṃ kriyāyogi guṇas tasmād vikalpate
B3.1.80/ sāmyenānyatarābhāve vikalpaḥ kaiś cid iṣyate
BL1506/ atadguṇo+ataś chāgaḥ syān meṣo vā tadguṇo bhavet
B3.1.81/ jāter āśritasaṃkhyāyāḥ pravṛttir upalabhyate
BL1508/ saṃkhyāviśeṣam utsṛjya kva cit saiva pravartate
B3.1.82/ parāṅgabhūtaṃ sāmānyaṃ yujyate dravyasaṃkhyayā
BL1510/ svārthaṃ pravartamānaṃ tu na saṃkhyām avalambate
B3.1.83/ yajeta paśunety atra yajyarthāyāṃ paśuśrutau
BL1512/ kṛtārthaikena paśunā pradhānaṃ bhavati kriyā
B3.1.84/ yāvatāṃ saṃbhavo yasya sa kuryāt tāvatāṃ yadi
BL1514/ ālambhanaṃ guṇais tena pradhānaṃ syāt prayojitam
B3.1.85/ saṃmṛjyamānatantre tu grahe yatra kriyāśrutiḥ
BL1516/ saṃkhyāviśeṣagrahaṇaṃ naiva tatrādriyāmahe
B3.1.86/ śiṣyamāṇapare vākye yad ekagrahaṇaṃ kṛtam
BL1518/ śeṣe viśiṣṭasaṃkhye+api vyaktaṃ talliṅgadarśanam
B3.1.87/ samāsapratyayavidhau yathā nipatitā śrutiḥ
BL1520/ guṇānāṃ paratantrāṇāṃ nyāyenaivopapadyate
B3.1.88/ guṇe+api nāṅgīkriyate pradhānāntarasiddhaye
BL1522/ saṃkhyā kartā tathā karmaṇy aviśiṣṭaḥ pratīyate
B3.1.89/ yasyānyasya prasaktasya niyamārthā punaḥ śrutiḥ
BL1524/ sarūpasamudāyāt tu vibhaktir yā vidhīyate
B3.1.90/ nivṛttau caritārthatvāt saṃkhyā tatrāvivakṣitā
BL1526/ ekas tatrārthavān siddhaḥ samudāyasya vācakaḥ
B3.1.91/ pratyayasya pradhānasya samāsasyāpi vā vidhau
BL1528/ siddhaḥ saṃkhyāvivakṣāyāṃ sarvathānugraho guṇe
B3.1.92/ abhedarūpaṃ sādṛśyam ātmabhūtāś ca śaktayaḥ
BL1530/ jātiparyāyavācitvam eṣām apy upavarṇyate
B3.1.93/ daṇḍopāditsayā daṇḍaṃ yady api pratipadyate
BL1532/ na tasmād eva sāmarthyāt sa daṇḍīti pratīyate
B3.1.94/ necchānimittād icchāvān iti jñānaṃ pravartate
BL1534/ tasmāt saty api sāmarthye buddhir arthāntarāśrayā
B3.1.95/ svabhāvo+avyapadeśyo vā sāmarthyaṃ vāvatiṣṭhate
BL1536/ sarvasyānte yatas tasmād vyavahāro na kalpate
B3.1.96/ yadā bhedān parityajya buddhyaika iva gṛhyate
BL1538/ vyaktyātmaiva tadā tatra buddhir ekā pravartate
B3.1.97/ bhedarūpair anusyūtaṃ yadaikam iva manyate
BL1540/ samūhāvagrahā buddhir bahubhyo jāyate tadā
B3.1.98/ yadā sahavivakṣāyām ekabuddhinibandhanaḥ
BL1542/ baddhāvayavavicchedaḥ samudāyo+abhidhīyate
B3.1.99/ pratikriyaṃ samāptatvād eko bhedasamanvitaḥ
BL1544/ dvandve dvitvādibhedena tadāsāv upagamyate
B3.1.100/ sakṛtpravṛttāv ekatvam āvṛttau sadṛśātmatām
BL1546/ bhinnātmakānāṃ vyaktīnāṃ bhedāpohāt prapadyate
B3.1.101/ anupravṛtteti yathā- -bhinnā buddhiḥ pratīyate
BL1548/ artho vyāvṛttarūpo+api tathā tattvena gṛhyate
B3.1.102/ sarūpāṇāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ na bhedopanipātinaḥ
BL1550/ vidyante vācakāḥ śabdā nāpi bhedo+avadhāryate
B3.1.103/ jñānaśabdārthaviṣayā viśeṣā ye vyavasthitāḥ
BL1552/ teṣāṃ duravadhāratvāj jñānādyekatvadarśanam
B3.1.104/ jñāneṣv api yathārtheṣu tathā sarveṣu jātayaḥ
BL1554/ saṃsargadarśane santi tāś cārthasya prasādhikāḥ
B3.1.105/ jñeyastham eva sāmānyaṃ jñānānām upakārakam
BL1556/ na jātu jñeyavaj jñānaṃ pararūpeṇa rūpyate
B3.1.106/ yathā jyotiḥ prakāśena nānyenābhiprakāśyate
BL1558/ jñānākāras tathānyena na jñānenopagṛhyate
B3.1.107/ na cātmasamavetasya sāmānyasyāvadhāraṇe
BL1560/ jñānaśaktiḥ samarthā syāj jñātasyānyasya vastunaḥ
B3.1.108/ ayaugapadye jñānānām asyety agrahaṇaṃ na ca
BL1562/ yathopalabdhi smaraṇam upalabdhe ca jāyate
B3.1.109/ ghaṭajñānam iti jñānaṃ ghaṭajñānavilakṣaṇam
BL1564/ ghaṭa ity api yaj jñānaṃ viṣayopanipāti tat
B3.1.110/ yato viṣayarūpeṇa jñānarūpaṃ na gṛhyate
BL1566/ artharūpaviviktaṃ ca svarūpaṃ nāvadhāryate

<B 5>

3.2 dravyasamuddeśaḥ
B3.2.1/ ātmā vastu svabhāvaś ca śarīraṃ tattvam ity api
BL1568/ dravyam ity asya paryāyās tac ca nityam iti smṛtam
B3.2.2/ satyaṃ vastu tadākārair asatyair avadhāryate
BL1570/ asatyopādhibhiḥ śabdaiḥ satyam evābhidhīyate
B3.2.3/ adhruveṇa nimittena devadattagṛhaṃ yathā
BL1572/ gṛhītaṃ gṛhaśabdena śuddham evābhidhīyate
B3.2.4/ suvarṇādi yathā yuktaṃ svair ākārair apāyibhiḥ
BL1574/ rucakādyabhidhānānāṃ śuddham evaiti vācyatām
B3.2.5/ ākāraiś ca vyavacchedāt sārvārthyam avarudhyate
BL1576/ yathaiva cakṣurādīnāṃ sāmarthyaṃ nālikādibhiḥ
B3.2.6/ teṣv ākāreṣu yaḥ śabdas tathābhūteṣu vartate
BL1578/ tattvātmakatvāt tenāpi nityam evābhidhīyate
B3.2.7/ na tattvātattvayor bheda iti vṛddhebhya āgamaḥ
BL1580/ atattvam iti manyante tattvam evāvicāritam
B3.2.8/ vikalparūpaṃ bhajate tattvam evāvikalpitam
BL1582/ na cātra kālabhedo+asti kālabhedaś ca gṛhyate
B3.2.9/ yathā viṣayadharmāṇāṃ jñāne+atyantam asaṃbhavaḥ
BL1584/ tadātmeva ca tat siddham atyantam atadātmakam
B3.2.10/ tathā vikārarūpāṇāṃ tattve+atyantam asaṃbhavaḥ
BL1586/ tadātmeva ca tat tattvam atyantam atadātmakam
B3.2.11/ sat yam ākṛtisaṃhāre yad ante vyavatiṣṭhate
BL1588/ tan nityaṃ śabdavācyaṃ tac chabdāt tac ca na bhidyate
B3.2.12/ na tad asti na tan nāsti na tad ekaṃ na tat pṛthak
BL1590/ na saṃsṛṣṭaṃ vibhaktaṃ na vikṛtaṃ na na cānyathā
B3.2.13/ tan nāsti vidyate tac ca tad ekaṃ tat pṛthak pṛthak
BL1592/ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ ca vibhaktaṃ ca vikṛtaṃ tat tad anyathā
B3.2.14/ tasya śabdārthasaṃbandha- rūpam ekasya dṛśyate
BL1594/ tad dṛśyaṃ darśanaṃ draṣṭā darśane ca prayojanam
B3.2.15/ vikārāpagame satyaṃ suvarṇaṃ kuṇḍale yathā
BL1596/ vikārāpagame satyāṃ tathāhuḥ prakṛtiṃ parām
B3.2.16/ vācyā sā sarvaśabdānāṃ śabdāś ca na pṛthak tataḥ
BL1598/ apṛthaktve ca saṃbandhas tayor nānātmanor iva
B3.2.17/ ātmā paraḥ priyo dveṣyo vaktā vācyaṃ prayojanam
BL1600/ viruddhāni yathaikasya svapne rūpāṇi cetasaḥ
B3.2.18/ ajanmani tathā nitye paurvāparyavivarjite
BL1602/ tattve janmādirūpatvaṃ viruddham upalabhyate

<B 6>

3.3 saṃbandhasamuddeśaḥ
B3.3.1/ jñānaṃ prayoktur bāhyo+arthaḥ svarūpaṃ ca pratīyate
BL1604/ śabdair uccaritais teṣāṃ saṃbandhaḥ samavasthitaḥ
B3.3.2/ pratipattur bhavaty arthe jñāne vā saṃśayaḥ kva cit
BL1606/ svarūpeṣūpalabhyeṣu vyabhicāro na vidyate
B3.3.3/ asyāyaṃ vācako vācya iti ṣaṣṭhyā pratīyate
BL1608/ yogaḥ śabdārthayos tattvam apy ato vyapadiśyate
B3.3.4/ nābhidhānaṃ svadharmeṇa saṃbandhasyāsti vācakam
BL1610/ atyantaparatantratvād rūpaṃ nāsyāpadiśyate
B3.3.5/ upakārāt sa yatrāsti dharmas tatrānugamyate
BL1612/ śaktīnām api sā śaktir guṇānām apy asau guṇaḥ
B3.3.6/ taddharmaṇos tu tācchabdyaṃ saṃyogasamavāyayoḥ
BL1614/ tayor apy upakārārthā niyatās tadupādhayaḥ
B3.3.7/ kā cid eva hi sāvasthā kāryaprasavasūcitā
BL1616/ kasya cit kena cid yasyāṃ saṃyoga upajāyate
B3.3.8/ nirātmakānām utpattau niyamaḥ kva cid eva yaḥ
BL1618/ tenaivāvyapavargaś ca prāptyabhede sa yatkṛtaḥ
B3.3.9/ ātmāntarasya yenātmā tadātmevāvadhāryate
BL1620/ yataś caikatvanānātvaṃ tattvaṃ nādhyavasīyate
B3.3.10/ tāṃ śaktiṃ samavāyākhyāṃ śaktīnām upakāriṇīm
BL1622/ bhedābhedāv atikrāntām anyathaiva vyavasthitām
B3.3.11/ dharmaṃ sarvapadārthānām atītaḥ sarvalakṣaṇaḥ
BL1624/ anugṛhṇāti saṃbandha iti pūrvebhya āgamaḥ
B3.3.12/ padārthīkṛta evānyaiḥ sarvatrābhyupagamyate
BL1626/ saṃbandhas tena śabdārthaḥ pravibhaktuṃ na śakyate
B3.3.13/ samavāyāt sva ādhāraḥ svā ca jātiḥ pratīyate
BL1628/ ekārthasamavāyāt tu guṇāḥ svādhāra eva ye
B3.3.14/ dravyatvasattāsaṃyogāḥ svānyādhāropabandhanāḥ
BL1630/ tatpradeśavibhāgāś ca guṇā dvitvādayaś ca ye
B3.3.15/ ke cit svāśrayasaṃyuktāḥ ke cit tatsamavāyinaḥ
BL1632/ saṃyuktasamaveteṣu samavetās tathāpare
B3.3.16/ svāśrayeṇa tu saṃyuktaiḥ saṃyuktaṃ vibhu gamyate
BL1634/ samavāyasya saṃbandho nāparas tatra dṛśyate
B3.3.17/ saṃbandhasyāviśiṣṭatvān na cātra niyamo bhavet
BL1636/ tasmāc chabdārthayor naivaṃ saṃbandhaḥ parikalpyate
B3.3.18/ adṛṣṭavṛttilābhena yathā saṃyoga ātmanaḥ
BL1638/ kva cit svasvāmiyogākhyo +abhede+anyatrāpi sa kramaḥ
B3.3.19/ prāptiṃ tu samavāyākhyāṃ vācyadharmātivartinīm
BL1640/ prayoktā pratipattā vā na śabdair anugacchati
B3.3.20/ avācyam iti yad vācyaṃ tad avācyatayā yadā
BL1642/ vācyam ity avasīyeta vācyam eva tadā bhavet
B3.3.21/ athāpy avācyam ity evaṃ na tad vācyaṃ pratīyate
BL1644/ vivakṣitāsya yāvasthā saiva nādhyavasīyate
B3.3.22/ tathānyathā sarvathā ca yasyāvācyatvam ucyate
BL1646/ tatrāpi naiva sāvasthā taiḥ śabdaiḥ pratiṣidhyate
B3.3.23/ na hi saṃśayarūpe+arthe śeṣatvena vyavasthite
BL1648/ avyudāse svarūpasya saṃśayo+anyaḥ pravartate
B3.3.24/ yadā ca nirṇayajñāne nirṇayatvena nirṇayaḥ
BL1650/ prakramyate tadā jñānaṃ svadharmeṇāvatiṣṭhate
B3.3.25/ sarvaṃ mithyā bravīmīti naitad vākyaṃ vivakṣyate
BL1652/ tasya mithyābhidhāne hi prakrānto+artho na gamyate
B3.3.26/ na ca vācakarūpeṇa pravṛttasyāsti vācyatā
BL1654/ pratipādyaṃ na tat tatra yenānyat pratipadyate
B3.3.27/ asādhikā pratijñeti neyam evābhidhīyate
BL1656/ yathā tathāsya dharmo+api nātra kaś cit pratīyate
B3.3.28/ vyāpārasyāparo yasmān na vyāpāro+asti kaś ca na
BL1658/ virodham anavasthāṃ vā tasmāt sarvatra nāśrayet
B3.3.29/ indriyāṇāṃ svaviṣayeṣv anādir yogyatā yathā
BL1660/ anādir arthaiḥ śabdānāṃ saṃbandho yogyatā tathā
B3.3.30/ asādhur anumānena vācakaḥ kaiś cid iṣyate
BL1662/ vācakatvāviśeṣe vā niyamaḥ puṇyapāpayoḥ
B3.3.31/ saṃbandhaśabde saṃbandho yogyatāṃ prati yogyatā
BL1664/ mayād yogyatāsaṃvin mātāputrādiyogavat
B3.3.32/ śabdaḥ kāraṇam arthasya sa hi tenopajanyate
BL1666/ tathā ca buddhiviṣayād arthāc chabdaḥ pratīyate
B3.3.33/ bhojanādy api manyante buddhyarthe yad asaṃbhavi
BL1668/ buddhyarthād eva buddhyarthe jāte tad api dṛśyate
B3.3.34/ anityeṣv api nityatvam abhidheyātmanā sthitam
BL1670/ anityatvaṃ svaśaktir vā sā ca nityān na bhidyate
B3.3.35/ śabdenārthasya saṃskāro dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaprayojanaḥ
BL1672/ kriyate so+abhisaṃbandham antareṇa kathaṃ bhavet
B3.3.36/ nāvaśyam abhidheyeṣu saṃskāraḥ sa tathāvidhaḥ
BL1674/ dṛśyate na ca saṃbandhas tathābhūto vivakṣitaḥ
B3.3.37/ sati pratyayahetutvaṃ saṃbandha upapadyate
BL1676/ śabdasyārthe yatas tatra saṃbandho+astīti gamyate
B3.3.38/ nitye+anitye+api vāpy arthe puruṣeṇa kathaṃ ca na
BL1678/ saṃbandho+akṛtasaṃbandhaiḥ śabdaiḥ kartuṃ na śakyate
B3.3.39/ vyapadeśe padārthānām anya sattaupacārikī
BL1680/ sarvāvasthāsu sarveṣām ātmarūpasya darśikā
B3.3.40/ sphaṭikādi yathā dravyaṃ bhinnarūpair upāśrayaiḥ
BL1682/ svaśaktiyogāt saṃbandhaṃ tādrūpyeṇeva gacchati
B3.3.41/ tadvac chabdo+api sattāyām asyaṃ pūrvaṃ vyavasthitaḥ
BL1684/ dharmair upaiti saṃbandham avirodhivirodhibhiḥ
B3.3.42/ evaṃ ca pratiṣedhyeṣu pratiṣedhaprakḷptaye
BL1686/ āśriteṣūpacāreṇa pratiṣedhaḥ pravartate
B3.3.43/ ātmalābhasya janmākhyā satā labhyaṃ ca labhyate
BL1688/ yadi saj jāyate kasmād athāsaj jāyate katham
B3.3.44/ sato hi gantur gamanaṃ sati gamye pravartate
BL1690/ gantṛvac cen na janmārtho na cet tadvan na jāyate
B3.3.45/ upacarya tu kartāram abhidhānapravṛttaye
BL1692/ punaś ca karmabhāvena tāṃ kriyāṃ ca tadāśrayām
B3.3.46/ athopacārasattaivaṃ vidheyās tatra lādayaḥ
BL1694/ janmanā tu virodhitvān mukhyā sattā na vidyate
B3.3.47/ ātmānam ātmanā bibhrad astīti vyapadiśyate
BL1696/ antarbhāvāc ca tenāsau karmaṇā na sakarmakaḥ
B3.3.48/ prāk ca sattābhisaṃbandhān mukhyā sattā kathaṃ bhavet
BL1698/ asaṃś ca nāsteḥ kartā syād upacāras tu pūrvavat
B3.3.49/ tasmād bhinneṣu dharmeṣu virodhiṣv avirodhinīm
BL1700/ virodhikhyāpanāyaiva śabdais tais tair upāśritām
B3.3.50/ abhinnakālām artheṣu bhinnakāleṣv avasthitām
BL1702/ pravṛttihetuṃ sarveṣāṃ śabdānām aupacārikīm
B3.3.51/ etāṃ sattāṃ padārtho hi na kaś cid ativartate
BL1704/ sā ca saṃpratisattāyāḥ pṛthag bhāṣye nidarśitā
B3.3.52/ pradeśasyaikadeśaṃ vā parato vā nirūpaṇam
BL1706/ viparyayam abhāvaṃ vā vyavahāro+anuvartate
B3.3.53/ yathendriyasya vaiguṇyān mātrādhyāropavān iva
BL1708/ jāyate pratyayo+arthebhyas tathaivoddeśajā matiḥ
B3.3.54/ akṛtsnaviṣayābhāsaṃ śabdaḥ pratyayam āśritaḥ
BL1710/ artham āhānyarūpeṇa svarūpeṇānirūpitam
B3.3.55/ rūpaṇavyapadeśābhyaṃ laukike vartmani sthitau
BL1712/ jñānaṃ praty abhilāpaṃ ca sadṛśau bālapaṇḍitau
B3.3.56/ sarvārtharūpatā śuddhir jñānasya nirupaśrayā
BL1714/ tato+apy asya parāṃ śuddhim eke prāhur arūpikām
B3.3.57/ upaplavo hi jñānasya bāhyākārānupātitā
BL1716/ kāluṣyam iva tat tasya saṃsarge vyatibhedajam
B3.3.58/ yathā ca jñānam ālekhād aśuddhau vyavatiṣṭhate
BL1718/ tathopāśrayavān arthaḥ svarūpād viprakṛṣyate
B3.3.59/ evam arthasya śabdasya jñānasya ca viparyaye
BL1720/ bhāvābhāvāv abhedena vyavahārānupātinau
B3.3.60/ yathā bhāvam upāśritya tadabhāvo+anugamyate
BL1722/ tathābhāvam upāśritya tadbhāvo+apy anugamyate
B3.3.61/ nābhāvo jāyate bhāvo naiti bhāvo +anupākhyatām
BL1724/ ekasmād ātmano+ananyau bhāvābhāvau vikalpitau
B3.3.62/ abhāvasyānupākhyatvāt kāraṇaṃ na prasādhakam
BL1726/ sopākhyasya tu bhāvasya kāraṇaṃ kiṃ kariṣyati
B3.3.63/ tasmāt sarvam abhāvo vā bhāvo vā sarvam iṣyate
BL1728/ na tv avasthāntaraṃ kiṃ cid ekasmāt satyataḥ sthitam
B3.3.64/ tasmān nābhāvam icchanti ye loke bhāvavādinaḥ
BL1730/ abhāvavādino vāpi na bhāvaṃ tattvalakṣaṇam
B3.3.65/ advaye caiva sarvasmin svabhāvād ekalakṣaṇe
BL1732/ parikalpeṣu maryādā vicitraivopalabhyate
B3.3.66/ catasro hi yathāvasthā nirupākhye prakalpitāḥ
BL1734/ evaṃ dvaividhyam apy etad bhāvābhāvavyapāśrayam
B3.3.67/ avirodhī virodhī vā sann asan vāpi yuktitaḥ
BL1736/ kramavān akramo vāpi nābhāva upapadyate
B3.3.68/ avirodhī virodhī vā sann asan vāpi tattvataḥ
BL1738/ kramavān akramo vāpi tena bhāvo na vidyate
B3.3.69/ abhāve triṣu kāleṣu na bhedasyāsti saṃbhavaḥ
BL1740/ tasminn asati bhāve+api traikālyaṃ nāvatiṣṭhate
B3.3.70/ ātmatattvaparityāgaḥ parato nopapadyate
BL1742/ ātmatattvaṃ tu parataḥ svato vā nopakalpate
B3.3.71/ tattve virodho nānātva upakāro na kaś ca na
BL1744/ tattvānyatvaparltyāge vyavahāro nivartate
B3.3.72/ yatra draṣṭā ca dṛśyaṃ ca darśanaṃ cāvikalpitam
BL1746/ tasyaivārthasya satyatvaṃ śritās trayyantavedinaḥ
B3.3.73/ sāmānyaṃ vā viśeṣaṃ vā yasmād āhur viśeṣavat
BL1748/ śabdās tasmād asatyeṣu bhedeṣv eva vyavasthitāḥ
B3.3.74/ na hy abhāvasya sadbhāve bhāvasyātmā prahīyate
BL1750/ na cābhāvasya nāstitve bhāvasyātmā prasūyate
B3.3.75/ na śābaleyasyāstitvaṃ bāhuleyasya bādhakam
BL1752/ na śābaleyo nāstīti bāhuleyaḥ prakalpate
B3.3.76/ abhāvo yadi vastu syāt tatreyaṃ syād vicāraṇā
BL1754/ tataś ca tadabhāve+api syād vicāryam idaṃ punaḥ
B3.3.77/ avastu syād atitaṃ yad vyavahārasya gocaraḥ
BL1756/ tatra vastugato bhedo na nirvacanam arhati
B3.3.78/ apade+arthe padanyāsaḥ kāraṇasya na vidyate
BL1758/ atha ca prāgasadbhāvaḥ kāraṇe sati dṛśyate
B3.3.79/ kā tasya prāgavastheti vastvāśritam idaṃ punaḥ
BL1760/ prāg avastheti na hy etad dvayam apy asty avastuni
B3.3.80/ na cordhvam asti nāstīti vacanāyānibandhanam
BL1762/ alaṃ syād apadasthānam etad vācaḥ pracakṣate
B3.3.81/ atyadbhutā tv iyaṃ vṛttir yad abhāgaṃ yad akramam
BL1764/ bhāvānāṃ prāg abhūtānām ātmatattvaṃ prakāśate
B3.3.82/ vikalpotthāpitenaiva sarvo bhāvena laukikaḥ
BL1766/ mukhyeneva padārthena vyavahāro vidhīyate
B3.3.83/ bhāvaśaktim ataś caināṃ manyante nityavādinaḥ
BL1768/ bhāvam eva kramaṃ prāhur na bhāvād aparaḥ kramaḥ
B3.3.84/ kramān na yaugapadyasya kaś cid bhedo+asti tattvataḥ
BL1770/ yathaiva bhāvān nābhāvaḥ kaś cid anyo+avasīyate
B3.3.85/ kālasyāpy aparaṃ kālaṃ nirdiśanty eva laukikāḥ
BL1772/ na ca nirdeśamātreṇa vyatireko+anugamyate
B3.3.86/ ādhāraṃ kalpayan buddhyā nābhāve vyavatiṣṭhate
BL1774/ avastuṣv api notprekṣā kasya cit pratibadhyate
B3.3.87/ tasmāc chaktivibhāgena nityaḥ sadasadātmakaḥ
BL1776/ eko+arthaḥ śabdavācyatve bahurūpaḥ prakāśate
B3.3.88/ vyavahāraś ca lokasya padārthaiḥ parikalpitaiḥ
BL1778/ śāstre padārthaḥ kāryārthaṃ laukikaḥ pravibhajyate

<B 7>

3.4 bhūyodravyasamuddeśaḥ
B3.4.1/ saṃsargarūpāt saṃbhūtāḥ saṃvidrūpād apoddhṛtāḥ
BL1780/ śāstre vibhaktā vākyārthāt prakṛtipratyayārthavat
B3.4.2/ nimittabhūtāḥ sādhutve śāstrād anumitātmakāḥ
BL1782/ ke cit padārthā vakṣyante saṃkṣepeṇa yathāgamam
B3.4.3/ vastūpalakṣaṇaṃ yatra sarvanāma prayujyate
BL1784/ dravyam ity ucyate so+artho bhedyatvena vivakṣitaḥ

<B 8>

3.5 guṇasamuddeśaḥ
B3.5.1/ saṃsargi bhedakaṃ yad yat savyāpāraṃ pratīyate
BL1786/ guṇatvaṃ paratantratvāt tasya śāstra udāhṛtam
B3.5.2/ dravyasyāvyapadeśasya ya upādīyate guṇaḥ
BL1788/ bhedako vyapadeśāya tatprakarṣo+abhidhīyate
B3.5.3/ sarvasyaiva pradhānasya na vinā bhedahetunā
BL1790/ prakarṣo vidyate nāpi śabdasyopaiti vācyatām
B3.5.4/ vidyamānāḥ pradhāneṣu na sarve bhedahetavaḥ
BL1792/ viśeṣaśabdair ucyante vyāvṛttārthābhidhāyibhiḥ
B3.5.5/ vastūpalakṣaṇe tatra viśeṣo vyāpṛto yadi
BL1794/ prakarṣo niyamābhāvāt syād avijñātahetukaḥ
B3.5.6/ sarvaṃ ca sarvato+avaśyaṃ niyamena prakṛṣyate
BL1796/ saṃsargiṇā nimittena nikṛṣṭenādhikena vā
B3.5.7/ nāpekṣate nimittaṃ ca prakarṣe vyāpṛtaṃ yadi
BL1798/ dravyasya syād upādānaṃ prakarṣaṃ praty anarthakam
B3.5.8/ savyāpāro guṇas tasmāt svaprakarṣanibandhanaḥ
BL1800/ dravyātmānaṃ bhinatty eva svaprakarṣaṃ niveśayan
B3.5.9/ arūpaṃ pararūpeṇa dravyam ākhyāyate yathā
BL1802/ aprakarṣaṃ prakarṣeṇa guṇasyāviśyate tathā

<B 9>

3.6 diksamuddeśaḥ
B3.6.1/ dik sādhanaṃ kriyā kāla iti vastvabhidhāyinaḥ
BL1804/ śaktirūpe padārthānām atyantam anavasthitāḥ
B3.6.2/ vyatirekasya yo hetur avadhipratipādyayoḥ
BL1806/ ṛjv ity evaṃ yato+anyena vinā buddhiḥ pravartate
B3.6.3/ karmaṇo jātibhedānām abhivyaktir yadāśrayā
BL1808/ sā svair upādhibhir bhinnā śaktir dig iti kathyate
B3.6.4/ parāparatve mūrtīnāṃ deśabhedanibandhane
BL1810/ tata eva prakalpete kramarūpe tu kālataḥ
B3.6.5/ ākāśasya pradeśena bhāgaiś cānyaiḥ pṛthak pṛthak
BL1812/ sā saṃyogavibhāgānām upādhitvāya kalpate
B3.6.6/ diśo vyavasthā deśānāṃ digvyavasthā na vidyate
BL1814/ śaktayaḥ khalu bhāvānām upakāraprabhāvitāḥ
B3.6.7/ pratyastarūpā bhāveṣu dik pūrvety abhidhīyate
BL1816/ pūrvabuddhir yato dik sā samākhyāmātram anyathā
B3.6.8/ svāṅgād vyavasthā yā loke na tasyāṃ niyatā diśaḥ
BL1818/ pratyaṅmukhasya yat paścāt tat purastād viparyaye
B3.6.9/ deśavyavasthāniyamo dikṣu na vyavatiṣṭhate
BL1820/ rūḍham apy aparatvena pūrvam ity abhidhīyate
B3.6.10/ ato bhāṣitapuṃskatvāt puṃvadbhāvo na sidhyati
BL1822/ asminn arthe na śabdena prasavaḥ kva cid ucyate
B3.6.11/ dikśakter abhidhāne tu niyataṃ diśi darśanam
BL1824/ pūrvādīnāṃ yathā ṣaṣṭer jīvitasyāvadhāraṇe
B3.6.12/ chāyābhābhyāṃ nagādīnāṃ bhāgabhedaḥ prakalpate
BL1826/ ataddharmasu bhāveṣu bhāgabhedo na kalpate
B3.6.13/ paramāṇor abhāgasya diśā bhāgo vidhīyate
BL1828/ bhāgaprakalpanāśaktiṃ prathamāṃ tāṃ pracakṣate
B3.6.14/ adeśāś cāpy abhāgāś ca niṣkramā nirupāśrayāḥ
BL1830/ bhāvāḥ saṃsargirūpāt tu śaktibhedaḥ prakalpate
B3.6.15/ nirbhāgātmakatā tulyā paramāṇor ghaṭasya ca
BL1832/ bhāgaḥ śaktyantaraṃ tatra parimāṇaṃ ca yat tayoḥ
B3.6.16/ yataḥ prakalpate bhedo bhedas tatrāpi dṛśyate
BL1834/ adṛṣṭoparatiṃ bhedam ato+ayuktataraṃ viduḥ
B3.6.17/ sarvatra tasya kāryasya darśanād vibhur iṣyate
BL1836/ vibhutvam etad evāhur anyaḥ kāyavatāṃ vidhiḥ
B3.6.18/ caitanyavat sthitā loke dikkālaparikalpanā
BL1838/ prakṛtiṃ prāṇināṃ tāṃ hi ko+anyathā sthāpayiṣyati
B3.6.19/ saṃkaro vyavahārāṇāṃ prakṛteḥ syād viparyaye
BL1840/ tasmāt tyajann imān bhāvān punar evāvalambate
B3.6.20/ tasyās tu śakteḥ pūrvādi- bhedo bhāvāntarāśrayaḥ
BL1842/ bhinnā dik tena bhedena bhedāyaivopakalpate
B3.6.21/ avadhitvena cāpekṣā- yoge diglakṣaṇo vidhiḥ
BL1844/ pūrvam as yeti ṣaṣṭhy eva dṛṣṭā dharmāntarāśraye
B3.6.22/ pūrvādināṃ viparyāso +adṛṣṭaś cāvadhyasaṃkare
BL1846/ ṛjv etad asyety etac ca liṅgaṃ na vyatikīryate
B3.6.23/ antaḥkaraṇadharmo vā bahir evaṃ prakāśate
BL1848/ asyāṃ tv antarbahirbhāvaḥ prakriyāyāṃ na vidyate
B3.6.24/ ekatvam āsāṃ śaktīnāṃ nānātvaṃ veti kalpane
BL1850/ avastupatite jñātvā satyato na parāmṛśet
B3.6.25/ vikalpātītatattveṣu saṃketopanibandhanāḥ
BL1852/ bhāveṣu vyavahārā ye lokas tatrānugamyate
B3.6.26/ naikatvam asty anānātvaṃ vinaikatvena netarat
BL1854/ paramārthe tayor eṣa bhedo+atyantaṃ na vidyate
B3.6.27/ na śaktīnāṃ tathā bhedo yathā śaktimatāṃ sthitiḥ
BL1856/ na ca laukikam ekatvaṃ tāsām ātmasu vidyate
B3.6.28/ naikatvaṃ vyavatiṣṭheta nānātvaṃ cen na kalpayet
BL1858/ nānātvaṃ cāvahīyeta yady ekatvaṃ na kalpayet

<B 10>

3.7 sādhanasamuddeśaḥ
B3.7.1/ svāśraye samavetānāṃ tadvad evāśrayāntare
BL1860/ kriyāṇām abhiniṣpattau sāmarthyaṃ sādhanaṃ viduḥ
B3.7.2/ śaktimā trāsam ūhasya viśvasyānekadharmaṇaḥ
BL1862/ sarvadā sarvathā bhāvāt kva cit kiṃ cid vivakṣyate
B3.7.3/ sādhanavyavahāraś ca buddhyavasthānibandhanaḥ
BL1864/ sann asan vārtharūpeṣu bhedo buddhyā prakalpyate
B3.7.4/ buddhyā samīhitaikatvān pañcālān kurubhir yadā
BL1866/ punar vibhajate vaktā tadāpāyaḥ pratīyate
B3.7.5/ śabdopahitarūpāṃś ca buddher viṣayatāṃ gatān
BL1868/ pratyakṣam iva kaṃsādīn sādhanatvena manyate
B3.7.6/ buddhipravṛttirūpaṃ ca samāropyābhidhātṛbhiḥ
BL1870/ artheṣu śaktibhedānāṃ kriyate parikalpanā
B3.7.7/ vyaktau padārthe śabdāder janyamānasya karmaṇaḥ
BL1872/ sādhanatvaṃ tathā siddhaṃ buddhirūpaprakalpitam
B3.7.8/ svatantraparatantratve kramarūpaṃ ca darśitam
BL1874/ nirīheṣv api bhāveṣu kalpanopanibandhanam
B3.7.9/ śaktayaḥ śaktimantaś ca sarve saṃsargavādinām
BL1876/ bhāvās teṣv asvaśabdeṣu sādhanatvaṃ nirūpyate
B3.7.10/ ghaṭasya dṛśikarmatve mahattvādīni sādhanam
BL1878/ rūpasya dṛśikarmatve rūpatvādīni sādhanam
B3.7.11/ svaiḥ sāmānyaviśeṣaiś ca śaktimanto rasādayaḥ
BL1880/ niyatagrahaṇā loke śaktayas tās tathāśrayaiḥ
B3.7.12/ indriyārthamanaḥkartṛ- saṃbandhaḥ sādhanaṃ kva cit
BL1882/ yad yadā yadanugrāhi tat tadā tatra sādhanam
B3.7.13/ svaśabdair abhidhāne tu sa dharmonābhidhīyate
BL1884/ vibhaktyādibhir evāsāv upakāraḥ pratīyate
B3.7.14/ nimittabhāvo bhāvānām upakārārtham āśritaḥ
BL1886/ natir āvarjanety evaṃ siddhaḥ sādhanam iṣyate
B3.7.15/ sa tebhyo vyatirikto vā teṣām ātmaiva vā tathā
BL1888/ vyatirekam upāśritya sādhanatvena kalpyate
B3.7.16/ saṃdarśanaṃ prārthanāyāṃ vyavasāye tv anantarā
BL1890/ vyavasāyas tathārambhe sādhanatvāya kalpate
B3.7.17/ pūrvasmin yā kriyā saiva parasmin sādhanaṃ matā
BL1892/ saṃdarśane tu caitanyaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ sādhanaṃ viduḥ
B3.7.18/ niṣpattimātre kartṛtvaṃ sarvatraivāsti kārake
BL1894/ vyāpārabhedāpekṣāyāṃ karaṇatvādisaṃbhavaḥ
B3.7.19/ putrasya janmani yathā pitroḥ kartṛtvam ucyate
BL1896/ ayam asyām iyaṃ tv asmād iti bhedo vivakṣayā
B3.7.20/ guṇakriyāṇāṃ kartāraḥ kartrā nyakkṛtaśaktayaḥ
BL1898/ nyaktāyām api saṃpūrṇaiḥ svair vyāpāraiḥ samanvitāḥ
B3.7.21/ karaṇatvādibhir jñātāḥ kriyābhedānupātibhiḥ
BL1900/ svātantryam uttaraṃ labdhvā pradhāne yānti kartṛtām
B3.7.22/ yathā rājñā niyukteṣu yoddhṛtvaṃ yoddhṛṣu sthitam
BL1902/ teṣu vṛttau tu labhate rājā jayaparājayau
B3.7.23/ tathā kartrā niyukteṣu sarveṣv ekārthakāriṣu
BL1904/ kartṛtvaṃ karaṇatvāder uttaraṃ na virudhyate
B3.7.24/ anāśrite tu vyāpāre nimittaṃ hetur iṣyate
BL1906/ āśritāvadhibhāvaṃ tu lakṣaṇe lakṣaṇaṃ viduḥ
B3.7.25/ dravyādiviṣayo hetuḥ kārakaṃ niyatakriyam
BL1908/ kartā kartrantarāpekṣaḥ kriyāyāṃ hetur iṣyate
B3.7.26/ kriyāyai karaṇaṃ tasya dṛṣṭaḥ pratinidhis tathā
BL1910/ hetvarthā tu kriyā tasmān na sa pratinidhīyate
B3.7.27/ prātilomyānulomyābhyāṃ hetur arthasya sādhakaḥ
BL1912/ tādarthyam ānulomyena hetutvānugataṃ tu tat
B3.7.28/ sarvatra sahajā śaktir yāvaddravyam avasthitā
BL1914/ kriyākāle tv abhivyakter āśrayād upakāriṇī
B3.7.29/ kuḍyasyāvaraṇe śaktir asyādīnāṃ vidāraṇe
BL1916/ sarvadā sa tu san dharmaḥ kriyākāle nirūpyate
B3.7.30/ svāṅgasaṃyoginaḥ pāśā daityānāṃ vāruṇā yathā
BL1918/ vyajyante vijigīṣūṇāṃ dravyāṇāṃ śaktayas tathā
B3.7.31/ taikṣṇyagauravakāṭhinya- saṃsthānaiḥ svair asir yadā
BL1920/ chedyaṃ prati vyāpriyate śaktimān gṛhyate tadā
B3.7.32/ prāṅ nimittāntarodbhūtaṃ kriyāyāḥ kaiś cid iṣyate
BL1922/ sādhanaṃ sahajaṃ kaiś cit kriyānyaiḥ pūrvam iṣyate
B3.7.33/ pravṛttir eva prathamaṃ kva cid apy anapāśritā
BL1924/ śaktīr ekādhikaraṇe srotovad apakarṣati
B3.7.34/ apūrvaṃ kālaśaktiṃ vā kriyāṃ vā kālam eva vā
BL1926/ tam evamlaksanam bhāvam ke cid āhuh katham ca na
B3.7.35/ nityāḥ ṣaṭ śaktayo+anyeṣāṃ bhedābhedasamanvitāḥ
BL1928/ kriyāsaṃsiddhaye+artheṣu jātivat samavasthitāḥ
B3.7.36/ dravyākārādibhedena tāś cāparimitā iva
BL1930/ dṛśyante tattvam āsāṃ tu ṣaṭ śaktīr nātivartate
B3.7.37/ nimittabhedād ekaiva bhinnā śaktiḥ pratīyate
BL1932/ ṣoḍhā kartṛtvam evāhus tatpravṛtter nibandhanam
B3.7.38/ tattve vā vyatireke vā vyatiriktaṃ tad ucyate
BL1934/ śabdapramāṇako lokaḥ sa śāstreṇānugamyate
B3.7.39/ paramārthe tu naikatvaṃ pṛthaktvād bhinnalakṣaṇam
BL1936/ pṛthaktvaikatvarūpeṇa tattvam eva prakāśate
B3.7.40/ yat pṛthaktvam asaṃdigdhaṃ tad ekatvān na bhidyate
BL1938/ yad ekatvam asaṃdigdhaṃ tat pṛthaktvān na bhidyate
B3.7.41/ dyauḥ kṣamā vāyur ādityaḥ sāgarāḥ sarito diśaḥ
BL1940/ antaḥkaraṇatattvasya bhāgā bahir avasthitāḥ
B3.7.42/ kālavicchedarūpeṇa tad evaikam avasthitam
BL1942/ sa hy apūrvāparo bhāvaḥ kramarūpeṇa lakṣyate
B3.7.43/ dṛṣṭo hy avyatireke+api vyatireko+anvaye +asati
BL1944/ vṛkṣādyarthānvayas tasmād vibhaktyartho+anya iṣyate
B3.7.44/ sāmānyaṃ kārakaṃ tasya saptādyā bhedayonayaḥ
BL1946/ ṣaṭ karmākhyādibhedena śeṣabhedas tu saptamī
<B 11>

karmādhikāraḥ
B3.7.45/ nirvartyaṃ ca vikāryaṃ ca prāpyaṃ ceti tridhā matam
BL1948/ tatrepsitatamaṃ karma caturdhānyat tu kalpitam
B3.7.46/ audāsīnyena yat prāpyaṃ yac ca kartur anīpsitam
BL1950/ saṃjñāntarair anākhyātaṃ yad yac cāpy anyapūrvakam
B3.7.47/ satī vāvidyamānā vā prakṛtiḥ pariṇāminī
BL1952/ yasya nāśriyate tasya nirvartyatvaṃ pracakṣate
B3.7.48/ prakṛtes tu vivakṣāyāṃ vikāryaṃ kaiś cid anyathā
BL1954/ nirvartyaṃ ca vikāryaṃ ca karma śāstre pradarśitam
B3.7.49/ yad asaj jāyate sad vā janmanā yat prakāśyate
BL1956/ tan nirvartyaṃ vikāryaṃ ca karma dvedhā vyavasthitam
B3.7.50/ prakṛtyucchedasaṃbhūtaṃ kiṃ cit kāṣṭhādibhasmavat

<B 20>

3.8: kriyāsamuddeśa
B3.8.1/ yāvat siddham asiddhaṃ vā sādhyatvenābhidhīyate
BL2194/ āśritakramarūpatvāt tat kriyeti pratiyate
B3.8.2/ kāryakāraṇabhāvena dhvanatīty āśritakramaḥ
BL2196/ dhvaniḥ kramanivṛttau tu dhvanir ity eva kathyate
B3.8.3/ śvete śvetata ity etac chvetatvena prakāśate
BL2198/ āśritakramarūpatvād abhidhānaṃ pravartate
B3.8.4/ guṇabhūtair avayavaiḥ samūhaḥ kramajanmanām
BL2200/ buddhyā prakalpitābhedaḥ kriyeti vyapadiśyate
B3.8.5/ samūhaḥ sa tatbābhūtaḥ pratibhedam samūhisu
BL2202/ samāpyate tato bhede kālabhedasya saṃbhavaḥ
B3.8.6/ kramāt sadasatāṃ teṣām ātmāno na samūhinām
BL2204/ sadvastuviṣayair yānti saṃbandhaṃ cakṣurādibhiḥ
B3.8.7/ yathā gaur iti samghātaḥ sarvo nendriyagocaraḥ
BL2206/ bhāgaśas tūpalabdhasya buddhau rūpaṃ nirūpyate
B3.8.8/ indriyair anyathāprāptau bhedāmśopanipātibhiḥ
BL2208/ alātacakravad rūpaṃ kriyāṇāṃ parikalpyate
B3.8.9/ yathā ca bhāgāḥ pacater udakāsecanādayaḥ
BL2210/ udakāsecanādināṃ jñeyā bhāgās tathāpare
B3.8.10/ yaś cāpakarṣaparyantam anuprāptaḥ pratīyate
BL2212/ tatraikasmin kriyāśabdaḥ kevale na prayujyate
B3.8.11/ pūrvottarais tathā bhāgaiḥ samavasthāpitakramaḥ
BL2214/ ekaḥ so+apy asadadhyāsād ākhyātair abhidhīyate
B3.8.12/ kālānupāti yad rūpaṃ tad astīty anugamyate
BL2216/ paritas tu paricchinnaṃ bhāva ity eva kathyate
B3.8.13/ vyavahārasya siddhatvān na ceyaṃ guṇakalpanā
BL2218/ upacāro hi mukhyasya saṃbhavād avatiṣṭhate
B3.8.14/ āhitottaraśaktitvāt pratyekaṃ vā samūhinaḥ
BL2220/ anekarūpā lakṣyante kramavanta ivākramāḥ
B3.8.15/ anantaraṃ phalaṃ yasyāḥ kalpate tām kriyām viduḥ
BL2222/ pradhānabhūtāṃ tādarthyād anyāsāṃ tu tadākhyatā
B3.8.16*/ *kriyāpravṛttau yo hetus tadarthaṃ yad viceṣṭitam
BL2224/ anapekṣya prayuñjīta gacchatīty avadhārayan
B3.8.17/ satsu pratyayarūpo+asau bhāvo yāvan na jāyate
BL2226/ tāvat pareṣāṃ rūpeṇa sādhyaḥ sann abhidhīyate
B3.8.18/ siddhe tu sādhanākāṅkṣā kṛtārthatvān nivartate
BL2228/ na kriyāvācināṃ tasmāt prayogas tatra vidyate
B3.8.19/ sa cāpūrvāparibhūta ekatvād akramātmakaḥ
BL2230/ pūrvāparāṇāṃ dharmeṇa tadarthenānugamyate
B3.8.20/ asan nivartate tasmād yat sat tad upalabhyate
BL2232/ tayoḥ sadasatoś cāsāv ātmaika iva gṛhyate
B3.8.21/ jātim anye kriyām āhur anekavyaktivartinīm
BL2234/ asādhyā vyaktirūpeṇa sā sādhyevopalabhyate
B3.8.22/ ante yā vā kriyābhāge jātiḥ saiva kriyā smṛtā
BL2236/ sā vyakter anuniṣpāde jāyamāneva gamyate
B3.8.23/ svavyāpāraviśiṣṭānām sattā vā, kartṛkarmanām
BL2238/ kriyā vyāpārabhedeṣu sattā vā samavāyinī
B3.8.24/ antye vātmani yā sattā sā kriyā kaiś cid iṣyate
BL2240/ bhāva eva hi dhātvartha ity avicchinna āgamaḥ
B3.8.25/ buddhiṃ tajjātim anye tu buddhisattām athāpare
BL2242/ pratyastarūpāṃ bhāveṣu kriyeti pratijānate
B3.8.26/ āvirbhāvatirobhāvau janmanāśau tathāparaiḥ
BL2244/ ṣaṭsu bhāvavikāreṣu kalpitau vyāvahārikau
B3.8.27/ tābhyāṃ sarvapravṛttīnām abhedenopasamgrahaḥ
BL2246/ janmaivāśritasārūpyaṃ sthitir ity abhidhīyate
B3.8.28*/ *jāyamānān na janrnānyad vināśe+apy apadārthatā
BL2248/ ato bhāvavikāreṣu sattaikā vyavatiṣṭhate
B3.8.29/ *pūrvabhāgas tu yaj jātāt taj janmety apadiśyate
BL2250/ āśritakramarūpeṇa nimittatve vivakṣite
B3.8.30/ ākhyātaśabdair artho+asāv evaṃbhūto +abhidhīyate
BL2252/ nāmaśabdāḥ pravartante saṃharanta iva kramam
B3.8.31/ phalaṃ phalāpadeśo vā vastu vā tadvirodhi yat
BL2254/ tad anyad eva pūrveṣāṃ nāga ity apadiśyate
B3.8.32/ naivāsti naiva nāstīti vastuno grahanād vinā
BL2256/ kalpate pararūpeṇa vastv anyad anugamyate
B3.8.33/ bhāvābhāvau ghaṭādinām aspṛśann api pāṇinā
BL2258/ kaś cid vedāprakāśe+api prakāśe tata eva vā
B3.8.34/ vyāpi saukṣmyaṃ kva cid yāti kva cit saṃhanyate punaḥ
BL2260/ akurvāṇo+atha vā kiṃ cit svaśaktyaivaṃ prakāśate
B3.8.35/ sarvarūpasya tattvasya yat krameṇeva darśanam
BL2262/ bhāgair iva prakḷptiś ca tāṃ kriyām apare viduḥ
B3.8.36/ sattā svaśaktiyogena sarvarūpā vyavasthitā
BL2264/ sādhyā ca sādhanaṃ caiva phalaṃ bhoktā phalasya ca
B3.8.37/ kriyām anye tu manyante kva cid apy anapāśritām
BL2266/ sādhanaikārthakāritve pravṛttim anapāyinīm
B3.8.38/ sāmānyabhūtā sā pūrvaṃ bhāgaśaḥ pravibhajyate
BL2268/ tato vyāpārarūpeṇa sādhyeva vyavatiṣṭhate
B3.8.39/ prakṛtiḥ sādhanānāṃ sā prathamaṃ tac ca kārakam
BL2270/ vyāpārāṇāṃ tato+anyatvam aparair upavarṇyate
B3.8.40/ bahūnāṃ saṃbhave+arthānāṃ ke cid evopakārinaḥ
BL2272/ saṃsarge kaś cid esāṃ tu prādhānyena pratīyate
B3.8.41/ sādhyatvāt tatra cākhyātair vyāpārāḥ siddhasādhanāḥ
BL2274/ prādhānyenābhidhīyante phalenāpi pravartitāḥ
B3.8.42/ ekatvāvṛttibhāvābhyāṃ bhedābhedasamanvaye
BL2276/ saṃkhyās tatropalabhyante saṃkhyeyāvayavakriyāḥ
B3.8.43/ siddhasyārthasya pākādeḥ kathaṃ sādhanayogitā
BL2278/ sādhyatve vā tiṅantena kṛtāṃ bhedo na kaś cana
B3.8.44/ tatra kārakayogāyā yady ākhyātaṃ nibandhanam
BL2280/ ṣaṣṭhvāḥ sā lena saṃbandhe vyudastā kartṛkarmanoḥ
B3.8.45/ ekābhidhāna eko+artho yugapac ca dvidharmabhāk
BL2282/ na saṃbhavati siddhatve sa sādhyaḥ syāt kathaṃ punaḥ
B3.8.46/ etāvat sādhanaṃ sādhyam etāvad iti kalpanā
BL2284/ śāstra eva na vākye+asti vibhāgaḥ paramārthataḥ
B3.8.47/ ākhyātaśabde bhāgābhyāṃ sādhyasādhanavartitā
BL2286/ prakalpitā yathā śāstre sa ghañādisv api kramaḥ
B3.8.48/ sādhyatvena kriyā tatra dhāturūpanibandhanā
BL2288/ sattvabhāvas tu yas tasyāḥ sa ghañādinibandhanaḥ
B3.8.49/ bandhutābhedarūpeṇa bandhuśabde vyavasthitā
BL2290/ samūho bandhvavasthā tu pratyayenābhidhīyate
B3.8.50/ tatra yam prati sādhyatvam asiddhā taṃ prati kriyā
BL2292/ siddhā tu yasmin sādhyatvaṃ na tam eva punaḥ prati
B3.8.51/ rājñaḥ putrasya napteti na rājñi vyatiricyate
BL2294/ putrasyārthaḥ pradhānatvaṃ na cāsya vinivartate
B3.8.52/ mṛgo dhāvati paśyeti sādhyasādhanarūpatā
BL2296/ tathā viṣayabhedena saraṇasyopapadyate
B3.8.53/ lakṛtyaktakhalarthānāṃ tathāvyayakṛtām api
BL2298/ rūḍhiniṣṭhāghañādinām dhātuḥ sādhyasya vācakaḥ
B3.8.54/ sādhyasyāpariniṣpatteḥ so+ayam ity anupagrahaḥ
BL2300/ tiṅantair antareṇevam upamānaṃ tato na taiḥ
B3.8.55/ sādhanatvaṃ prasiddhaṃ ca tiṅkṣu saṃbandhināṃ yataḥ
BL2302/ tenādhyāropa eva syād upamā tu na vidyate
B3.8.56/ nyūneṣu ca samāptārtham upamānaṃ vidhīyate
BL2304/ kriyā caivāśraye sarvā tatra tatra samāpyate
B3.8.57/ yenaiva hetunā haṃsaḥ patatīty abhidhīyate
BL2306/ ātau tasya samāptatvād upamārtho na vidyate
B3.8.58/ kriyāṇāṃ jātibhinnānāṃ sādṛśyaṃ nāvadhāryate
BL2308/ siddheś ca prakrame sādhyam upamātum na śakyate
B3.8.59/ vanam vṛkṣā iti yathā bhedābhedavyapāśrayāt
BL2310/ arthātmā bhidyate bhāve sa bāhyābhyantare kramaḥ
B3.8.60/ sāmānye bhāva ity atra yal liṅgam upalabhyate
BL2312/ bhedānāṃ anumeyatvān na tat teṣu vivakṣyate
B3.8.61/ nirdeśe caritārthatvāl liṅgaṃ bhāve +avivaksitam
BL2314/ upamānavidhitvāc ca bhāvād anyat pacādisu
B3.8.62/ bhavatau yat pacādināṃ tāvad atropadiśyate
BL2316/ na ca liṅgam pacādināṃ bhavatau samavasthitam
B3.8.63/ ekaś ca so+arthaḥ sattākhyaḥ katham cit kaiś cid ucyate
BL2318/ liṅgāni cāsya bhidyante pacirūpādibhedavat
B3.8.64/ ācāryo mātulaś ceti yathaiko vyapadiśyate
BL2320/ sambandhibhedād arthātmā sa vidhiḥ paktibhāvayoḥ

<B 21>

3.9: kālasamuddeśaḥ
B3.9.1/ vyāpāravyatirekeṇa kālam eke pracakṣate
BL2322/ nityam ekaṃ vibhu dravyaṃ parimāṇaṃ kriyāvatām
B3.9.2/ diṣṭiprasthasuvarṇādi mūrtibhedāya kalpate
BL2324/ kriyābhedāya kālas tu saṃkhyā sarvasya bhedikā
B3.9.3/ utpattau ca sthitau caiva vināśe cāpi tadvatām
BL2326/ nimittaṃ kālam evāhur vibhaktenātmanā sthitam
B3.9.4/ tam asya lokayantrasya sūtradhāraṃ pracakṣate
BL2328/ pratibandhābhyanujñābhyāṃ tena viśvaṃ vibhajyate
B3.9.5/ yadi na pratibadhnīyāt pratibandhaṃ ca notsṛjet
BL2330/ avasthā vyatikīryeran paurvāparyavinākṛtāḥ
B3.9.6/ tasyātmā bahudhā bhinno bhedair dharmāntarāśrayaiḥ
BL2332/ na hi bhinnam abhinnaṃ vā vastu kiṃ cana vidyate
B3.9.7/ naiko na cāpy aneko+asti na śuklo nāpi cāsitaḥ
BL2334/ dravyātmā sa tu saṃsargād evaṃrūpaḥ prakāśate
B3.9.8/ saṃsargināṃ tu ye bhedā viśeṣās tasya te matāḥ
BL2336/ sa bhinnas tair vyavasthānāṃ kālo bhedāya kalpate
B3.9.9/ viśiṣṭakālasaṃbandhād vṛttilābhaḥ prakalpate
BL2338/ śaktīnāṃ saṃprayogasya hetutvenāvatiṣṭhate
B3.9.10/ janmābhivyaktiniyamāḥ prayogopanibandhanāḥ
BL2340/ nityādhīnasthititvāc ca sthitir niyamapūrvikā
B3.9.11/ sthitasyānugrahas tais tair dharmaiḥ saṃsargibhis tataḥ
BL2342/ pratibandhas tirobhāvaḥ prahāṇam iti cātmanaḥ
B3.9.12/ pratyavasthaṃ tu kālasya vyāpāro+atra vyavasthitaḥ
BL2344/ kāla eva hi viśvātmā vyāpāra iti kathyate
B3.9.13/ mūrtīnāṃ tena bhinnānām ācayāpacayāḥ pṛthak
BL2346/ lakṣyante pariṇāmena sarvāsāṃ bhedayoginā
B3.9.14/ jalayantrabhramāveśa- sadṛśībhiḥ pravṛttibhiḥ
BL2348/ sa kalāḥ kalayan sarvāḥ kālākhyāṃ labhate vibhuḥ
B3.9.15/ pratibhaddhāś ca yās tena citrā viśvasya vṛttayaḥ
BL2350/ tāḥ sa evānujānāti yathā tantuḥ śakuntikāḥ
B3.9.16/ viśiṣṭakālasaṃbandhāl labdhapākāsu śaktiṣu
BL2352/ kriyābhivyajyate nityā prayogākhyena karmaṇā
B3.9.17/ jātiprayuktā tasyāṃ tu phalavyaktiḥ prajāyate
BL2354/ kuto+apy adbhutayā vṛttyā śaktibhiḥ sā niyamyate
B3.9.18/ tatas tu samavāyākhyā śaktir bhedasya bādhikā
BL2356/ ekatvam iva tā vyaktīr āpādayati kāraṇaiḥ
B3.9.19/ athāsmān niyamād ūrdhvaṃ jātayo yāḥ prayojikāḥ
BL2358/ tāḥ sarvā vyaktim āyānti svacche chāyā ivāmbhasi
B3.9.20/ kāraṇānuvidhāyitvād atha kāraṇa pūrvakāḥ
BL2360/ guṇās tatropajāyante svajātivyaktihetavaḥ
B3.9.21/ āśrayāṇāṃ ca nityatvam āśritānāṃ ca nityatā
BL2362/ tā vyaktīr anugṛhṇāti sthitis tena prakalpate
B3.9.22/ anityasya yathotpāde pāratantryaṃ tathā sthitau
BL2364/ vināśāyaiva tat śṛṣṭam asvādhīnasthitiṃ viduḥ
B3.9.23/ sthitaḥ saṃsargibhir bhāvaiḥ svakriyāsv anugṛhyate
BL2366/ naiṣāṃ sattām anudgṛhya vṛttir janmavatāṃ smṛtā
B3.9.24/ jarākhyā kālaśaktir yā śaktyantaravirodhinī
BL2368/ sā śaktīḥ pratibadhnāti jāyante ca virodhinaḥ
B3.9.25/ prayojakās tu ye bhāvāḥ sthitibhāgasya hetavaḥ
BL2370/ tirobhavanti te sarve yata ātmā prahīyate
B3.9.26/ yathaivādbutayā vṛttyā niṣkramaṃ nirnibandhanam
BL2372/ apadaṃ jāyate sarvaṃ tathāsyātmā prahīyate
B3.9.27/ kriyayor apavargiṇyor nānārthasamavetayoḥ
BL2374/ saṃbandhinā vinaikena paricchedaḥ kathaṃ bhavet
B3.9.28/ yathā tulāyāṃ haste vā nānādravyavyavasthitam
BL2376/ gurutvaṃ parimīyeta kālād evaṃ kriyāgatiḥ
B3.9.29/ jahāti sahavṛttāś ca kriyāḥ sa samavasthitāḥ
BL2378/ vrīhir yathodakaṃ tena hāyanākhyāṃ prapadyate
B3.9.30/ pratibandhābhyanujñābhyāṃ vṛttir yā tasya śāsvatī
BL2380/ tayā vibhajyamāno+asau bhajate kramarūpatāṃ
B3.9.31/ kartṛbhedāt tadartheṣu pracayāpacayau gataḥ
BL2382/ samatvaṃ viṣamatvaṃ vā sa ekaḥ pratipadyate
B3.9.32/ kriyābhedād yathaikasmiṃs takṣādyākhyā pravartate
BL2384/ kriyābhedāt tathaikasminn ṛtvādyākhyopajāyate
B3.9.33/ ārambhaś ca kriyā caiva niṣṭhā cety abhidhīyate
BL2386/ dharmāntarāṇām adhyāsa- bhedāt sadasadātmanaḥ
B3.9.34/ yāvāṃś ca dvyaṇukādīnāṃ tāvān himavato+apy asau
BL2388/ na hy ātmā kasya cid bhettuṃ pracetuṃ vāpi śakyate
B3.9.35/ anyais tu bhāvair anyeṣāṃ pracayaḥ parikalpyate
BL2390/ śanair idam idaṃ kṣipram iti tena pratīyate
B3.9.36/ asataś ca kramo nāsti sa hi bhettuṃ na śakyate
BL2392/ sato+api cātmatattvaṃ yat tat tathaivāvatiṣṭhate
B3.9.37/ kriyopādhiś ca san bhūta- bhaviṣyadvartamānatāḥ
BL2394/ ekādaśābhir ākārair vibhaktāḥ pratipadyate
B3.9.38/ bhūtaḥ pañcavidhas tatra bhaviṣyaṃś ca caturvidhaḥ
BL2396/ vartamāno dvidhākhyāta ity ekādaśa kalpanāḥ
B3.9.39/ kāle nidhāya svaṃ rūpaṃ prajñayā yan nigṛhyate
BL2398/ bhāvās tato nivartante tatra saṃkrāntaśaktayaḥ
B3.9.40/ bhāvināṃ caiva yad rūpaṃ tasya ca pratibimbakam
BL2400/ sunirmṛṣṭa ivādarśe kāla evopapadyate
B3.9.41/ tṛṇaparṇalatādīni yathā sroto+anukarṣati
BL2402/ pravartayati kālo+api mātrā mātrāvatāṃ tathā
B3.9.42/ āviśyevānusaṃdhatte yathā gatimatāṃ gatīḥ
BL2404/ vāyus tatraiva kālātmā vidhatte kramarūpatām
B3.9.43/ ayanapravibhāgaś ca gatīś ca jyotiṣāṃ dhruvā
BL2406/ nivṛttiprabhavāś caiva bhūtānāṃ tannibandhanāḥ
B3.9.44/ mātrāṇāṃ pariṇāmā ye kālavṛttyanupātinaḥ
BL2408/ nakṣatrākhyā pṛthak teṣu cihnamātraṃ tu tārakāḥ
B3.9.45/ rutair mṛgaśakuntānāṃ sthāvarāṇāṃ ca vṛttibhiḥ
BL2410/ chāyādipariṇāmaiś ca ṛtudhāmā nirūpyate
B3.9.46/ nirbhāsopagamo yo+ayaṃ kramavān iva dṛśyate
BL2412/ akramasyāpi viśvasya tat kālasya viceṣṭitam
B3.9.47/ dūrāntikavyavasthānam adhvādhikaraṇaṃ yathā
BL2414/ cirakṣipravyavasthānaṃ kālādhikaraṇaṃ tathā
B3.9.48/ tasyābhinnasya kālasya vyavahāre kriyākṛtāḥ
BL2416/ bhedā iva trayaḥ siddhā yāṃl loko nātivartate
B3.9.49/ ekasya śaktayas tisraḥ kālasya samavasthitāḥ
BL2418/ yatsaṃbandhena bhāvānāṃ darśanādarśane satām
B3.9.50/ dvābhyāṃ sa kila śaktibhyāṃ bhāvānāṃ varaṇātmakaḥ
BL2420/ śaktis tu vartamānākhyā bhāvarūpaprakāśinī
B3.9.51/ anāgatā janmaśakteḥ śaktir apratibandhikā
BL2422/ atītākhyā tu yā śaktis tayā janma virudhyate
B3.9.52/ tamaḥprakāśavat tv ete trayo+adhvāno vyavasthitāḥ
BL2424/ akramās teṣu bhāvānāṃ kramaḥ samupalabhyate
B3.9.53/ dvau tu tatra tamorūpāv ekasyālokavat sthitiḥ
BL2426/ atītam api keṣāṃ cit punar viparivartate
B3.9.54/ yugapad vartamānatvaṃ taddharmā pratipadyate
BL2428/ keṣāṃ cid vartamānatvāc caiti tadvad atītatām
B3.9.55/ hetupakārād ākṣipto vartamānatvam āgataḥ
BL2430/ śāntahetūpakāraḥ san punar nopaiti darśanam
B3.9.56/ dve eva kālasya vibhoḥ keṣāṃ cic chaktivartmanī
BL2432/ karoti yābhyāṃ bhāvānām unmīlananimīlane
B3.9.57/ kalābhiḥ pṛthagarthābhiḥ pravibhaktaṃ svabhāvataḥ
BL2434/ ke cid buddhyanusaṃhāra- lakṣaṇaṃ taṃ pracakṣate
B3.9.58/ jñānānugataśaktiṃ vā bāhyaṃ vā satyataḥ sthitam
BL2436/ kālātmānam anāśritya vyavahartuṃ na śakyate
B3.9.59/ tisro bhāvasya bhāvasya keṣāṃ cid bhāvaśaktayaḥ
BL2438/ tābhiḥ svaśaktibhiḥ sarvaṃ sadaivāsti ca nāsti ca
B3.9.60/ sattvād avyatirekeṇa tās tisro+api vyavasthitāḥ
BL2440/ kramas tās tadabhedāc ca sadasattvaṃ na bhidyate
B3.9.61/ darśanādarśanenaikaṃ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaṃ tad eva tu
BL2442/ adhvanām ekatā nāsti na ca kiṃ cin nivartate
B3.9.62/ śaktyātmadevatāpakṣair bhinnaṃ kālasya darśanam
BL2444/ prathamaṃ tad avidyāyāṃ yad vidyāyāṃ na vidyate
B3.9.63/ abhede yadi kālasya hrasvadīrghaplutādiṣu
BL2446/ dṛśyate bhedanirbhāsaḥ sa cirakṣiprabuddhivat
B3.9.64/ hrasvadīrghaplutāvṛttyā nālikāsalilādiṣu
BL2448/ kathaṃ pracayayogaḥ syāt kalpanāmātrahetukaḥ
B3.9.65/ abhivyaktinimittasya pracayena pracīyate
BL2450/ abhinnam api śabdasya tattvam apracayātmakam
B3.9.66/ evaṃ mātrāturīyasya bhedo dāśatayasya vā
BL2452/ parimāṇavikalpena śabdātmani na vidyate
B3.9.67/ anuniṣpādikalpena ye+antarāla iva sthitāḥ
BL2454/ śabdās te pratipattṝṇām upāyāḥ pratipattaye
B3.9.68/ viśiṣṭam avadhiṃ taṃ tam upādāya prakalpate
BL2456/ kālaḥ kālavatām ekaḥ kṣaṇamāsartubhedabhāk
B3.9.69/ buddhyavagrahabhedāc ca vyavahārātmani sthitaḥ
BL2458/ tāvān eva kṣaṇaḥ kālo yugamanvantarāṇi vā
B3.9.70/ pratibandhābhyanujñābhyāṃ nālikāvivarāśrite
BL2460/ yad ambhasi prakṣaraṇaṃ tat kālasyaiva ceṣṭitam
B3.9.71/ alpe mahati vā chidre tatsaṃbandhe na bhidyate
BL2462/ kālasya vṛttir ātmāpi tam evāsyānuvartate
B3.9.72/ ākrīḍa iva kālasya dṛśyate yaḥ svaśaktibhiḥ
BL2464/ bahurūpasya bhāveṣu bahudhā tena bhidyate
B3.9.73/ tvacisārasya vā vṛddhiṃ tṛṇarājasya vā dadhat
BL2466/ tāvat tadvṛddhiyogena kālatattvaṃ vikalpate
B3.9.74/ vyatikrame+api mātrāṇāṃ tasya nāsti vyatikramaḥ
BL2468/ na gantṛgatibhedena mārgabhedo+asti kaś cana
B3.9.75/ udayāstamayāvṛttyā jyotiṣāṃ lokasiddhayā
BL2470/ kālasyāvyatipāte+api tāddharmyam iva lakṣyate
B3.9.76/ ādityagrahanakṣatra- parispandam athāpare
BL2472/ bhinnam āvṛttibhedena kālaṃ kālavido viduḥ
B3.9.77/ kriyāntarapariccheda- pravṛttā yā kriyāṃ prati
BL2474/ nirjñātaparimāṇā sā kāla ity abhidhīyate
B3.9.78/ jñāne rūpasya saṃkrāntir jñānenaivānusaṃhṛtiḥ
BL2476/ ataḥ kriyāntarābhāve sā kriyā kāla iṣyate
B3.9.79/ bhūto ghaṭa itīyaṃ ca sattāyā eva bhūtatā
BL2478/ bhūtā satteti sattāyāḥ sattā bhūtābhidhīyate
B3.9.80/ parato bhidyate sarvam ātmā tu na vikalpyate
BL2480/ parvatādisthitis tasmāt pararūpeṇa bhidyate
B3.9.81/ prasiddhabhedā vyāpārā virūpāvayavakriyāḥ
BL2482/ sāhacaryeṇa bhidyante sarūpāvayavakriyāḥ
B3.9.82*/ *vyavadhānam ivopaiti nivṛtta iva dṛśyate
BL2484/ kriyāsamūho bhujyādir antarālapravṛttibhiḥ
B3.9.83*/ *na ca vicchinnarūpo+api so+avirāmān nivartate
BL2486/ sarvaiva hi kriyānyena samkīrṇevopalabhyate
B3.9.84/ *tadantarāladṛṣṭā vā sarvaivāvayavakriyā *
BL2488/ sādṛśyāt sati bhede tu tadaṅgatvena gṛhyate
B3.9.85/ sad asad vāpi vastu syāt tṛtīyaṃ nāsti kiṃ cana
BL2490/ tena bhūtabhaviṣyantau muktvā madhyaṃ na vidyate
B3.9.86/ nirvṛttirūpam ekasya bhedābhāvān na kalpate
BL2492/ sad asad vāpi tenaikaṃ kramarūpaṃ kathaṃ bhavet
B3.9.87/ bahūnāṃ cānavasthānād ekam evopalabhyate
BL2494/ yathopalabdhi smaraṇaṃ tatra cāpy upapadyate
B3.9.88/ sadasadrūpam ekaṃ syād sarvasyaikatvakalpane
BL2496/ nirvṛttirūpaṃ nirvṛtteḥ sāmānyam atha vā bhavet
B3.9.89/ kāryotpattau samarthaṃ vā svena dharmeṇa tat tathā
BL2498/ ātmatattvena gṛhyeta sā cāsmin vartamānatā
B3.9.90/ kriyāprabandharūpaṃ yad adhyātmaṃ vinigṛhyate
BL2500/ saṃkrāntarūpam ekatra tām āhur vartamānatām
B3.9.91/ kriyātipattir atyantaṃ kriyānutpattilakṣaṇā
BL2502/ na ca bhūtam anutpannaṃ na bhaviṣyat tathāvidham
B3.9.92/ prāg viruddhakriyotpādān nirvṛtte vā virodhini
BL2504/ vyāpāre+avadhibhedena viṣayas tatra bhidyate
B3.9.93/ vyabhicāre nimittasya sādhutvaṃ na prakalpate
BL2506/ bhāvy āsīd iti sūtreṇa tat kāle+anyatra śiṣyate
B3.9.94/ svakāla eva sādhutve kālabhede gatiḥ katham
BL2508/ vākyārthād atadartheṣu viśiṣṭatvaṃ na sidhyati
B3.9.95/ tadarthaś ced avayavo bhāvino bhūtatāgatiḥ
BL2510/ na syād atyantabhūtatvam evaikaṃ tatra saṃbhavet
B3.9.96/ viśiṣṭakālatā pūrvaṃ tathāpi tu viśeṣaṇe
BL2512/ āśrayāt so+antaraṅgatvāt tatra sādhur bhaviṣyati
B3.9.97/ āmiśra eva prakrāntaḥ sa padārthas tathāvidhaḥ
BL2514/ kevalasya vimiśratvaṃ nitye+arthe nopapadyate
B3.9.98/ śuddhe ca kāle vyākhyātam āmiśre na prasidhyati
BL2516/ sādhutvam ayathākālaṃ tat sūtreṇopadiśyate
B3.9.99/ ākhyātapadavācye+arthe nirvartyatvāt pradhānatā
BL2518/ viśeṣaṇaṃ tadākṣepāt tatkāle vyavatiṣṭhate
B3.9.100/ saṃpratyayānukāro vā śabdavyāpāra eva vā
BL2520/ adhyasyate viruddhe+arthe na ca tena virudhyate
B3.9.101/ bhūtaṃ bhaviṣyad ity etau pratyayau vartamānatām
BL2522/ atyajantau prapadyete viruddhāśrayarūpatām
B3.9.103/ adhvano vartamānasya viṣayeṇa bhaviṣyatā
BL2524/ bhāṣye bhaviṣyatkāleti kāryārthaṃ vyapadiśyate
B3.9.104/ icchā cikīrśatīty atra svakālam anurudhyate
BL2526/ bhaviṣyati prakṛtyarthe tatkālaṃ nānurudhyate
B3.9.105/ āśāsyamānatantratvād āśaṃsāyāṃ viparyayaḥ
BL2528/ prayoktṛdharmaḥ śabdārthe śabdair evānuśajyate
B3.9.106/ apchālibījasaṃyoge vartate niṣpadir yadā
BL2530/ tatrāvayavavṛttitvād bhaviṣyatpratiṣedhanam
B3.9.107/ phalaprasavarūpe tu niṣpadau bhūtakālatā
BL2532/ dharmāntareṣu tad rūpam adhyasya parikalpyate
B3.9.108/ upayukte nimittānāṃ vyāpāre phalasiddhaye
BL2534/ tatra rūpaṃ yad adhyastaṃ tatkālaṃ tat pratīyate
B3.9.109/ niṣpattāv avadhiḥ kaś cit kaś cit prativivakṣitaḥ
BL2536/ hetujanmavyapekṣātaḥ phalajanmeti cocyate
B3.9.110/ abahiḥsādhanādhīnā siddhir yatra vivakṣitā
BL2538/ tat sādhanāntarābhāvāt siddham ity apadiśyate
B3.9.111/ tasmād avadhibhedena siddhā mukhyaiva bhūtatā
BL2540/ anāgatatvam astitvaṃ hetudharmavyapekṣaṇe
B3.9.112/ satām indriyasaṃbandhāt saiva sattā viśiṣyate
BL2542/ bhedena vyavahāro hi vastvantaranibandhanaḥ
B3.9.113/ astitvaṃ vastumātrasya buddhyā tu parigṛhyate
BL2544/ yaḥ samāsādanād bhedaḥ sa tatra na vivakṣitaḥ
B3.9.114/ yogād vā strītvapuṃstvābhyāṃ na kiṃ cid avatiṣṭhate
BL2546/ svasminn ātmani tatrānyad bhūtaṃ bhāvi ca kathyate

<B 22>

3.10: puruṣasamuddeśa
B3.10.1/ pratyaktā parabhāvaś cāpy upādhī kartṛkarmanoḥ
BL2548/ tayoḥ śrutiviśeṣeṇa vācakau madhyamottamau
B3.10.2/ sad asad vāpi caitanyam etābhyām avagamyate
BL2550/ caitanyabhāge prathamaḥ puruṣo na tu vartate
B3.10.3/ budhijānāticitibhiḥ prathame puruṣe sati
BL2552/ samjñānārthair na caitanya- syopayogaḥ prakāśyate
B3.10.4/ saṃbodhanārthaḥ sarvatra madhyame kaiś cid iṣyate
BL2554/ tathā saṃbodhane sarvāṃ prathamāṃ yuṣmado viduḥ
B3.10.5/ saṃbodhanaṃ na loke+asti vidhātavyena vastunā
BL2556/ svāhendraśatrur vardhasva yathā rājā bhaveti ca
B3.10.6/ yuṣmadarthasya siddhatvān niyatā cādyudāttatā
BL2558/ yuṣmadaḥ prathamāntasya paraś cen na padād asau
B3.10.7/ guṇapradhānatābhedaḥ puruśādiviparyayaḥ
BL2560/ nirdeśaś cānyathā śāstre nityatvān na virudhyate
B3.10.8/ yathānirdeśam arthāḥ syur yesāṃ śāstraṃ vidhāyakam
BL2562/ kim cit sāmānyam āśritya sthite tu pratipādanam
B3.10.9/ yo+aśve yaḥ pīṭha ity atra bhūtayor aśvapīṭhayoḥ
BL2564/ yathopalakṣaṇārthatvaṃ tathārtheṣv anuśāsanam

<B 23>

3.11: saṃkhyāsamuddeśa
B3.11.1/ saṃkhyāvān sattvabhūto+arthaḥ sarva evābhidhīyate
BL2566/ bhedābhedavibhāgo hi loke saṃkhyānibandhanaḥ
B3.11.2/ sa dharmo vyatirikto vā teṣāṃ ātmaiva vā tathā
BL2568/ bhedahetutvam āśritya saṃkhyeti vyapadiśyate
B3.11.3/ samavetā paricchedye kva cid anyatra sā sthitā
BL2570/ prakalpayati bhāvānāṃ saṃkhyā bhedaṃ tathātmanaḥ
B3.11.4/ paratve cāparatve ca bhede tulyā śrutir yathā
BL2572/ saṃkhyāśabdābhidheyatvaṃ bhedahetos tathā guṇe
B3.11.5/ asvatantre svatantratvaṃ paradharmo yathā guṇe
BL2574/ abhedye bhedyabhāvo+api dravyadharmas tathā guṇe
B3.11.6/ svabuddhyā tam apoddhṛtya loko+apy āgamam āśritaḥ
BL2576/ svadharmād anyadharmeṇa vyācaṣṭe pratipattaye
B3.11.7/ paropakāratattvānāṃ svātantryenābhidhāyakaḥ
BL2578/ śabdaḥ sarvapadārthānā svadharmad viprakṛṣyate
B3.11.8/ yathaivāviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ na kiṃ cid avabhāsate
BL2580/ tathā bhāvo+apy asaṃsṛṣṭo na kaś cid upalabhyate
B3.11.9/ bhedena tu samākhyātaṃ yal loko+apy anuvartate
BL2582/ āgamāc chāstrasadṛśo vyavahāraḥ sa varṇyate
B3.11.10/ buddhau sthiteṣu teṣv evam adhyāropo na durlabhaḥ
BL2584/ paradharmasya na hy atra sadasattvaṃ prayojakam
B3.11.11/ sāmānyeṣv api sāmānyaṃ viśeṣeṣu viśiṣṭatā
BL2586/ saṃkhyāsu saṃkhyā liṅgeṣu liṅgam evaṃ prakalpate
B3.11.12/ ato dravyāśritāṃ saṃkhyām āhuḥ saṃsargavādinaḥ
BL2588/ bhedābhedavyatīteṣu bhedābhedavidhāyinīm
B3.11.13/ ātmāntarānāṃ yenātmā tadrūpa iva lakṣyate
BL2590/ atadrūpeṇa saṃsargāt sā nimittasarūpatā
B3.11.14/ saṃsṛṣṭeṣv api nirbhāge bhūteṣv arthakriyā yathā
BL2592/ sattvādiṣu ca mātrāsu sarvāsv evaṃ pratīyate
B3.11.15/ dvitvādiyonir ekatvaṃ bhedās tatpūrvakā yataḥ
BL2594/ vinā tena na saṃkhyānām anyāsām asti saṃbhavaḥ
B3.11.16/ ekatve buddhisahite nimittaṃ dvitvajanmani
BL2596/ ekatvābhyāṃ samutpannam evaṃ vā tat pratīyate
B3.11.17/ ekatvasamudāyo vā sāpekṣe vā pṛthak pṛthak
BL2598/ ekatve dvitvam ity evaṃ tayor dvivacanaṃ bhavet
B3.11.18/ eko+api guṇabhedena saṅgho bhedaṃ prakalpayet
BL2600/ āśrayāśrayibhedo hi tadāśrayanibandhanaḥ
B3.11.19/ saṃkhyeyasaṅghasaṃkhyāna- saṅghaḥ saṃkhyeti kathyate
BL2602/ vimśatyādisu sānyasva dravyasaṅghasya bhedikā
B3.11.20/ ekaviṃśatisaṃkhvāvāṃ saṃkhyāntarasarūpayoḥ
BL2604/ ekasyāṃ buddhyanāvṛttyā, bhāgayor iva kalpanā
B3.11.21/ asaṃkhyāsamudāyatvāt saṃkhyākāryaṃ vidhīyate
BL2606/ samūhatve tu tan na syāt svāṅgādisamudāyavat
B3.11.22/ saṃkhyeyāntaratantrāsu yā saṃkhyāsu pravartate
BL2608/ āvṛttivargasaṃkhyeyā tāṃ saṃkhyāṃ tādṛśīṃ viduḥ
B3.11.23/ na saṃkhyāyāṃ na saṃkhyeye dvau daśety asti saṃbhavaḥ
BL2610/ bhedābhāvān na saṃkhyāyāṃ virodhān na tadāśraye
B3.11.24/ saṃkhyāyete daśadvargau dvidaśā iti saṃkhyayā
BL2612/ tadrūpe vāpi saṃkhyeya āvṛttiḥ parigaṇyate
B3.11.25/ saṃkhyā nāma na saṃkhyāsti saṃjñaiṣeti yathocyate
BL2614/ rūpaṃ na rūpam apy evaṃ samjñā sā hi sitādiṣu
B3.11.26/ saṃkhyānajātiyogāt tu saṃkhyā saṃkhyeti kathyate
BL2616/ rūpatvajātiyogāc ca rūpe rūpam iti smṛtam
B3.11.27/ nimittam ekam ity atra vibhaktyā nābhidhīyate
BL2618/ tadvatas tu yad ekatvaṃ vibhaktis tatra vartate
B3.11.28/ ekasya pracayo dṛṣṭaḥ samūhaś ca dvayos tathā
BL2620/ nimittavyatirekeṇa saṃkhyānyā bhedikā tataḥ
B3.11.29/ tad ekam api caikatvaṃ vibhaktiśravaṇād ṛte
BL2622/ nocyate tena śabdena vibhaktyā tu sahocyate
B3.11.30/ anvayavyatirekau ca yadi syād vacanāntaram
BL2624/ syātām asati tasmimś ca prakṛtyartho na kalpyate
B3.11.31/ ekatvam eka ity atra śuddhadravyaviśeṣaṇam
BL2626/ saguṇas tu prakṛtyartho vibhaktyarthena bhidyate
B3.11.32/ dvyekayor iti nirdeśāt saṃkhyāmātre+api saṃbhavaḥ
BL2628/ ekādīnāṃ prasiddhyā tu saṃkhyeyārthatvam ucyate

<B 24>

3.12: upagrahasamuddeśa
B3.12.1/ ya ātmanepadād bhedaḥ kva cid arthasya gamyate
BL2630/ anyataś cāpi lādeśān manyante tam upagraham
B3.12.2/ kva cit sādhanam evāsau kva cit tasya viśeṣaṇam
BL2632/ sādhanaṃ tatra karmādi vyaktavāco viśeṣaṇam
B3.12.3/ kriyā viṣayabhedena jīvikādiṣu bhidyate
BL2634/ lādeśaiḥ sa kriyābhedo vākyeṣv api niyamyate
B3.12.4/ dhātvarthas tadviśeṣaś cāpy uktaḥ kva cid upagrahaḥ
BL2636/ dhātvartho gandhanādiḥ syād vyatihāro viśeṣaṇam
B3.12.5/ kriyāpravṛttāv ākhyātā kaiś cit svārthaparārthatā
BL2638/ asati vā sati vāpi vivakṣitanibandhanā
B3.12.6/ kesāṃ cit kartrabhiprāye ṇicā saha vikalpate
BL2640/ ātmanepadam anyesāṃ tadarthā prakṛtir yathā
B3.12.7/ krīṇīṣva vapate dhatte cinoti cinute+api ca
BL2642/ āptaprayogā dṛśyante yeṣu ṇyartho+abhidhīyate
B3.12.8/ saṃvidhānaṃ pacādināṃ kva cid arthaḥ pratīyate
BL2644/ tannimittā yathānyāpi kriyādhiśrayaṇādikā
B3.12.9/ kartrabhiprāyatā sūtre kriyābhedopalakṣaṇam
BL2646/ tathābhūtā kriyā yā hi tatkartā phalabhāg yataḥ
B3.12.10/ yathopalakṣyate kālas tārakādarśanādibhiḥ
BL2648/ tathā phalaviśeṣeṇa kriyābhedo nidarśyate
B3.12.11/ kriyāviśeṣavacane sāmarthyam uparudhyate
BL2650/ kesāṃ cid anye tu kṛtāḥ svariteto ñitas tathā
B3.12.12/ anubandhaś ca siddhe+arthe smṛtyartham anuṣajyate
BL2652/ tulyārtheṣv api cāvaśyaṃ na sarveṣv ekadharmatā
B3.12.13/ dṛśīkṣyoḥ sadṛśe+apy arthe nābhedaḥ pratipūrvayoḥ
BL2654/ ṇyarthopādāyinas tasmān na tulyārthāḥ pacādibhiḥ
B3.12.14/ umbhyarthe vartamānasya karoter bhinnadharmaṇaḥ
BL2656/ ṇyarthopādāyitā tasmān niyatāḥ śabdaśaktayaḥ
B3.12.15/ tathā hy anuprayogasya karoter ātmanepade
BL2658/ pūrvavadgrahaṇaṃ prāpte svaritaṃ samupasthitam
B3.12.16/ ekatve+api kriyākhyāte sādhanāśrayasaṃkhyayā
BL2660/ bhidyate na tu liṅgākhyo bhedas tatra tadāśritaḥ
B3.12.17/ tasmād avasthite+apy arthe kasya cit pratibadhyate
BL2662/ śabdasya śaktiḥ sa tv eṣa śāstre+anvākhyāyate vidhiḥ
B3.12.18/ yasyārthasya prasiddhyartham ārabhyante pacādayaḥ
BL2664/ tat pradhānaṃ phalaṃ teṣāṃ na lābhādi prayojanam
B3.12.19/ yatrobhau svāmidāsau tu prārabhete saha kriyām
BL2666/ yugapad dharmabhedena dhātus tatra na vartate
B3.12.20/ yatra pratividhānārthaḥ pacis tatrātmanepadam
BL2668/ parasmaipadam anyatra saṃskārādyabhidhāyini
B3.12.21/ saṃvidhātuś ca sāṃnidhyād dāse dharmo +anusajyate
BL2670/ plakṣaśabdasya sāṃnidhyān nyagrodhe plakṣatā yathā
B3.12.22/ puroḍāśābhidhānaṃ ca dhānādiṣu yathā sthitam
BL2672/ chattriṇā cābhisaṃbandhāc chattriśabdābhidheyatā
B3.12.23/ arthāt pratītam anyonyaṃ pārārthyam avivakṣitam
BL2674/ ity ayaṃ śeṣaviṣayaḥ kaiś cid atrānuvarṇyate
B3.12.24/ atha pratividhātā yo halaiḥ kṛṣati pañcabhiḥ
BL2676/ bhāṣye nodāhṛtaṃ kasmāt prāptaṃ tatrātmanepadam
B3.12.25/ pratītatvāt tadarthasya śeṣatvaṃ yadi kalpyate
BL2678/ na syāt prāptavibhāṣāsau svaritetāṃ nivartikā
B3.12.26/ śuddhe tu saṃvidhānārthe kaiś cid atreṣyate kṛṣiḥ
BL2680/ taddharmā yajir ity evaṃ na syāt tatrātmanepadam
B3.12.27/ atra tūpapadenāyam arthabhedaḥ pratīyate
BL2682/ prāpte vibhāṣā kriyate tasmān nātrātmanepadam

<B 25>

3.13: liṅgasamuddeśa
B3.13.1/ stanakeśādisaṃbandho viśiṣṭā vā stanādayaḥ
BL2684/ tadupavyañjanā jātir guṇāvasthā guṇās tathā
B3.13.2/ śabdopajanito+arthātmā śabdasaṃskāra ity api
BL2686/ liṅgānāṃ liṅgatattvajñair vikalpāḥ sapta darśitāḥ
B3.13.3/ upādānavikalpāś ca liṅgānāṃ sapta varṇitāḥ
BL2688/ vikalpasaṃniyogābhyāṃ ye śabdeṣu vyavasthitāḥ
B3.13.4/ tisro jātaya evaitāḥ kesāṃ cit samavasthitāḥ
BL2690/ aviruddhā, viruddhābhir gomahiṣyādijātibhiḥ
B3.13.5/ hastinyāṃ vaḍavāyāṃ ca strīti buddheḥ samanvayaḥ
BL2692/ atas tāṃ jātim icchanti dravyādisamavāyinīm
B3.13.6/ paratantrasya yal liṅgam apoddhāre vivakṣite
BL2694/ tatrāsau śabdasaṃskāraḥ śabdair eva vyapāśritaḥ
B3.13.7/ buddhyā kalpitarūpeṣu liṅgeṣv api ca saṃbhavaḥ
BL2696/ strītvādīnāṃ vyavasthā hi sā liṅgair vyapadiśyate
B3.13.8/ yathā salilanirbhāsā mṛgatṛṣṇāsu jāyate
BL2698/ jalopalabdhyanuguṇād bījād buddhir jale+asati
B3.13.9/ tathaivāvyapadeśyebhyo hetubhyas tārakādiṣu
BL2700/ mukhyebhya iva liṅgebhyo bhedā loke vyavasthitāḥ
B3.13.10/ vyakteṣu vyaktarūpāṇāṃ stanādīnāṃ tu darśanāt
BL2702/ avyaktavyañjanāvyakter jātir na parikalpyate
B3.13.11/ astitvaṃ ca pratijñāya sadādarśanam icchataḥ
BL2704/ atyantādarśane na syād asattvaṃ prati niścayaḥ
B3.13.12/ na cālam anumānāya śabdo+adarśanapūrvakaḥ
BL2706/ siddhe hi darśane kiṃ syād anumānaprayojanam
B3.13.13/ āvirbhāvas tirobhāvaḥ sthitiś cety anapāyinaḥ
BL2708/ dharmā mūrtiṣu sarvāsu liṅgatvenānudarśitāḥ
B3.13.14/ sarvamūrtyātmabhūtānāṃ śabdādināṃ guṇe guṇe
BL2710/ trayaḥ sattvādidharmās te sarvatra samavasthitāḥ
B3.13.15/ rūpasya cātmamātrānāṃ śuklādināṃ pratikṣaṇam
BL2712/ kā cit pralīyate kā cit kathaṃ cid abhivardhate
B3.13.16/ kvathitodakavac caiṣām anavasthitavṛttitā
BL2714/ ajasraṃ sarvabhāvānāṃ bhāṣya evopavarṇitā
B3.13.17/ pravṛtter ekarūpatvaṃ sāmyaṃ vā sthitir ucyate
BL2716/ avirbhāvatirobhāva- pravṛttyā vāvatiṣṭhate
B3.13.18/ guṇā ity eva buddher vā nimittatvaṃ sthitir matā
BL2718/ sthiteś ca sarvaliṅgānāṃ sarvanāmatvam ucyate
B3.13.19/ sthiteṣu sarvaliṅgeṣu vivakṣāniyamāśrayaḥ
BL2720/ kasya cic chabdasaṃskāre vyāpāraḥ kva cid iṣyate
B3.13.20/ saṃnidhāne nimittānāṃ kiṃ cid eva pravartakam
BL2722/ yathā takṣādiśabdānāṃ lingeṣu niyamas tathā
B3.13.21/ bhāvatattvadṛśaḥ śiṣṭāḥ śabdārtheṣu vyavasthitāḥ
BL2724/ yad yad dharme+aṅgatām eti liṅgaṃ tat tat pracakṣate
B3.13.22/ svarabhedād yathā śabdāḥ sādhavo viṣayāntare
BL2726/ liṅgabhedāt tathā siddhāt sādhutvam anugamyate
B3.13.23/ prayogo viprayogaś ca loke yatropalabhyate
BL2728/ śāstram ārabhyate tatra na prayogāviparyaye
B3.13.24/ upādhibhedād artheṣu guṇadharmasya kasya cit
BL2730/ nimittabhāvaḥ sādhutve vivakṣā ca vyavasthitā
B3.13.25/ himāraṇye mahattvena yukte strītvam avasthitam
BL2732/ hrasvopādhiviśiṣṭāyāḥ kuṭyāḥ prasavayogitā
B3.13.26/ śabdāntarānāṃ bhinne+artha upāyāḥ pratipattaye
BL2734/ ekatām iva niścitya laghvartham upadarśitāḥ
B3.13.27/ utpattiḥ prasavo+anyeṣāṃ nāśaḥ saṃstyānam ity api
BL2736/ ātmarūpaṃ tu bhāvānāṃ sthitir ity apadiśyate
B3.13.28/ dṛṣṭaṃ nimittaṃ kesāṃ cij jātyādivad avasthitam
BL2738/ dṛṣṭavac chabdasaṃskāra- mātraṃ tu parikalpitam
B3.13.29/ yathā prasiddhe+apy ekatve nānātvābhiniveśinaḥ
BL2740/ nānātvaṃ janayantīva śabdā liṅge+api sa kramaḥ
B3.13.30/ idaṃ veyam ayaṃ veti śabdasaṃskāramātrakam
BL2742/ nimittadarśanād arthe kaiś cit sarvatra varṇyate
B3.13.31/ nāvaśyaṃ viṣayatvena nimittaṃ vyavatiṣṭhate
BL2744/ indriyādi yathādṛṣṭaṃ bhedahetus tad iṣyate

<B 26>

3.14: vṛttisamuddeśa
B3.14.1/ kutsāpraśaṃsātiśayaiḥ samāptārthaṃ tu yujyate
BL2746/ padaṃ svārthādayaḥ sarve yasmāt kutsādihetavaḥ
B3.14.2/ devadattādikutsāyāṃ vartate kutsitaśrutiḥ
BL2748/ kutsitasthā tu yā kutsā tadarthaḥ ko vidhīyate
B3.14.3/ prakṛṣṭa iti śuklādi- prakarṣasyābhidhāyakaḥ
BL2750/ prakṛṣṭasya prakarṣe tu tarabādir vidhīyate
B3.14.4/ kutsitatvena kutsyo vā na samyag vāpi kutsitaḥ
BL2752/ svaśabdābhihite kena viśiṣṭo+arthaḥ pratīyate
B3.14.5/ na ca sāṃpratikī kutsā bhedābhāvāt pratīyate
BL2754/ pūjyate kutsitatvena praśastatvena kutsyate
B3.14.6/ viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyatvaṃ padayor upajāyate
BL2756/ na prātipadikārthaś ca tatraiva vyatiricyate
B3.14.7/ viśeṣyaṃ syād anirjñātaṃ nirjñāto+artho viśeṣaṇam
BL2758/ parārthatvena śeṣatvaṃ sarveṣām upakāriṇām
B3.14.8/ vibhaktibhedo niyamād guṇaguṇyabhidhāyinoḥ
BL2760/ sāmānādhikaraṇyasya prasiddhir dravyaśabdayoḥ
B3.14.9/ dravye+anirjñātajātīye kṛṣṇaśabdaḥ prayujyate
BL2762/ anirjñātaguṇe caivaṃ tilaśabdaḥ pravartate
B3.14.10/ sāmānyānām asaṃbandhāt tau viśeṣe vyavasthitau
BL2764/ rūpābhedād viśeṣaṃ tam abhivyaṅktuṃ na śaknutaḥ
B3.14.11/ tāv eva saṃnipatitau bhedena pratipādane
BL2766/ avacchedam ivādhāya saṃśayaṃ vyapakarṣataḥ
B3.14.12/ dravyātmā guṇasaṃsarga- bhedād āśrīyate pṛthak
BL2768/ jātisaṃbandhabhedāc ca dvitīya iva gṛhyate
B3.14.13/ nimittair abhisaṃbandhād yā nimittasarūpatā
BL2770/ tayaikasyāpi nānātvaṃ rūpabhedāt prakalpate
B3.14.14/ dravyāvasthā tṛtīyā tu yasyāṃ saṃsṛjyate dvayam
BL2772/ tayor avasthayor bhedād āśrayatve niyujyate
B3.14.15/ buddhyaikaṃ bhidyate bhinnam ekatvaṃ copagacchati
BL2774/ buddhyāvasthā vibhajyante sā hy arthasya vidhāyikā
B3.14.16/ vyapadeśivad ekasmin buddhyā nānātvakalpanā
BL2776/ tayā kalpitabhedaḥ sann arthātmā vyapadiśyate
B3.14.17/ kriyābhedena dṛṣṭānām aśmādīnāṃ punaḥ punaḥ
BL2778/ kiṃ cid darśanam anyena darśanenāpadiśyate
B3.14.18/ prayogabhedād dhātūnāṃ prakalpya bahurūpatām
BL2780/ bhedābhedāv upādāya kva cid ekāctvam ucyate
B3.14.19/ anvayavyatirekābhyām arthavān parikalpitaḥ
BL2782/ eko dhātvarthavigamād varṇatvenopacaryate
B3.14.20/ dravyātmānas trayas tasmād buddhau nānā vyavasthitāḥ
BL2784/ āśrayāśrayidharmeṇety ayaṃ pūrvebhya āgamaḥ
B3.14.21/ sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca śabdayoḥ kaiś cid iṣyate
BL2786/ viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyatvaṃ saṃjñāsaṃjñitvam eva ca
B3.14.22/ keṣāṃ cij jātiguṇayor ekārthasamavetayoḥ
BL2788/ vṛttiḥ kṛṣṇatileṣv iṣṭā śabde dravyābhidhāyini
B3.14.23/ saṃs tu rūparasādinām āśrayo nābhidhīyate
BL2790/ dravyābhidhānena vinā tatas te dvandvabhāvinaḥ
B3.14.24/ dravyābhidhāyī kṛṣṇādir ākāṅkṣāvān pravartate
BL2792/ nimittānuvidhāyitvāt tat tilādau na vidyate
B3.14.25/ evaṃ jātimati dravye pratyāsanne kriyāṃ prati
BL2794/ guṇadharma guṇāviṣṭaṃ dravyaṃ bhedāya kalpate
B3.14.26/ guṇamātrābhidhāyitvaṃ ke cid icchanti vṛttiṣu
BL2796/ ajāśvādiṣu saṃbandhād rūḍhīnām iva rūḍhibhiḥ
B3.14.27/ tile pūrvam upātte vā tatraiva matub iṣyate
BL2798/ sa ca dharmaḥ samāseṣu guṇas tasmād viśeṣaṇam
B3.14.28*/ [paṭvīmṛdvyoḥ samāse tu yady apy ekārthavṛttitā
BL2800/ bhinnam atrādhikaraṇaṃ prāg vṛttes tac ca gṛhyate
B3.14.29/ anusyūteva bhedābhyām ekā prakhyopajāyate
BL2802/ yadā sahavivakṣāṃ tām āhur dvandvaikaśeṣayoḥ
B3.14.30/ itaretarayogas tu bhinnasaṅghābhidhāyinām
BL2804/ pratyekaṃ ca samūho+asau samūhiṣu samāpyate
B3.14.31/ vyāpārasamudāyasya yathādhiśrayaṇādiṣu
BL2806/ pratyekaṃ jātivad vṛttis tathā dvandvapadeṣv api
B3.14.32/ śauṇḍārdharcapuroḍāśa- cchattriṇo+atra nidarśanam
BL2808/ te viṣṇumitrā iti ca bhinneṣu sahacāriṣu
B3.14.33/ arthāntarābhidhāyitvaṃ tathārthāntaravartinām
BL2810/ yābhyāṃ caikam anekārthaṃ tābhyām evāparaṃ padam
B3.14.34/ samudāyāntaratvāc ca tādṛśo+artho na laukikaḥ
BL2812/ anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ śāstrārtho+api na dṛśyate
B3.14.35/ duḥkhā durupapādā ca tasmād bhāṣye+apy udāhṛtā
BL2814/ yugapadvācitā sā tu vyavahārārtham āśritā
B3.14.36/ samudāyam upakramya padaṃ tasyāṃ prayujyate
BL2816/ vibhāgena samākhyāne tatas tad dvyartham ucyate
B3.14.37/ vākye+api niyatā dharmāḥ ke cid vṛttau dvayos tathā
BL2818/ te tv abhedena sāmarthya- mātra evopavarṇitāḥ
B3.14.38/ vṛttau viśeṣavṛttitvād bhede sāmānyavācitā
BL2820/ upamānasamāsādau śyāmādīnām udāhṛtā
B3.14.39/ vṛttir anyapadārthe yā tasyā vākyeṣv asaṃbhavaḥ
BL2822/ cārthe dvandvapadānāṃ ca bhede vṛttir na vidyate
B3.14.40/ bhede sati nirādīnāṃ krāntādyartheṣv asaṃbhavaḥ
BL2824/ prāg vṛtter jātivācitvaṃ na ca gaurakharādiṣu
B3.14.41/ krīḍāyā, jīvikāyāś ca vākyenāvacanāt tathā
BL2826/ na nityagrahaṇaṃ yuktaṃ kauṭilye yaṅvidhau yathā
B3.14.42/ nirdhāraṇādiviṣaye vyapekṣaiva yataḥ sthitā
BL2828/ samāsapratiṣedhānāṃ tato nāsti prayojanam
B3.14.43/ vidhibhiḥ pratiṣedhaiś ca bhedābhedanidarśanam
BL2830/ kṛtaṃ dvandvaikavadbhāve saṅghavṛttyupadeśavat
B3.14.44/ sāmarthyam aviśeṣoktam api lokavyavasthayā
BL2832/ vṛttyavṛttyoḥ prayogajñair vibhaktaṃ pratipattṛbhiḥ
B3.14.45/ arthasya vinivṛttatvāl lugādi na virudhyate
BL2834/ ekārthībhāva evātaḥ samāsākhyā vidhīyate
B3.14.46/ vyavasthitavibhāṣā ca sāmānye kaiś cid iṣyate
BL2836/ tathā vākyaṃ vyapekṣāyāṃ samāso+anyatra śiṣyate
B3.14.47/ tulyaśrutitvāt tattve+api rājādīnām upāśrite
BL2838/ vṛttau viśeṣaṇākāṅkṣā- gamakatvān nivartate
B3.14.48/ saṃbandhiśabdaḥ sāpekṣo nityaṃ sarvaḥ prayujyate
BL2840/ svārthavat sā vyapekṣāsya vṛttāv api na hīyate
B3.14.49/ samudāyena saṃbandho yesāṃ gurukulādinā
BL2842/ saṃspṛśyāvayavāṃs te+api yujyante tadvatā saha
B3.14.50/ abudhān praty upāyāś ca vicitrāḥ pratipattaye
BL2844/ śabdāntaratvād atyanta- bhedo vākyasamāsayoḥ
B3.14.51/ asamāse samāse ca gorathādiṣv adarśanāt
BL2846/ yuktādināṃ na śāstreṇa nivṛttyanugamaḥ kṛtaḥ
B3.14.52/ śabdāntaratvād yuktādiḥ kva cid vākye prayujyate
BL2848/ praparṇaprapalāśādau gataśabdaś ca vṛttiṣu
B3.14.53/ viśeṣaṇaviśesyatvaṃ kaiś cid ekas tathāśrayaḥ
BL2850/ upāye tattvadarśitvād iṣyate vṛttivākyayoḥ
B3.14.54/ padaṃ yathaiva vṛkṣādi viśiṣṭe+arthe vyavasthitam
BL2852/ nīlotpalādy api tathā bhāgābhyāṃ vartate vinā
B3.14.55/ śrotriyakṣetriyādināṃ na ca vāsiṣṭhagārgyavat
BL2854/ bhedena pratyayo loke tulyarūpāsamanvayāt
B3.14.56/ saptaparṇādivad bhedo na vṛttau vidyate kva cit
BL2856/ rūḍhyarūḍhivibhāgo+api kriyate pratipattaye
B3.14.57/ yā sāmānyāśrayā saṃjñā viśeṣaviṣayā ca yā
BL2858/ bahulagrahaṇān nāsti pravṛttir ubhayos tayoḥ
B3.14.58/ susūkṣmajaṭakeśādau samāso+avayave yadi
BL2860/ syāt syāt tatrāntaraṅgatvād bādhako+avayavasvaraḥ
B3.14.59/ samudāyasya vṛttau ca naikadeśo vibhāṣyate
BL2862/ bheda eva vibhāṣāyā niyato viṣayo yataḥ
B3.14.60/ yataś cāviṣayaḥ so+asyās tasmān nāsty akṛtārthatā
BL2864/ abhedaprakrame+atyantaṃ bhedānām apasāraṇāt
B3.14.61/ mahākaṣṭaśritety evaṃ na syād bhedaḥ padatraye
BL2866/ vṛttāv avayavasyāttvaṃ yasmān na pratiṣidhyate
B3.14.62/ mahāraṇyam atīte tu tripadād bhidyate svaraḥ
BL2868/ yasmāt tatrāntaraṅgatvād bādhako+avayavasvaraḥ
B3.14.63/ satiśiṣṭabaliyastvāt thāthādisvara eva tu
BL2870/ dvipade tena yagapat tritayaṃ na samasyate
B3.14.64/ yeṣām apūjyamānatvaṃ parārthānugamātmake
BL2872/ viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyatvam api teṣāṃ na kalpate
B3.14.65/ viśeṣaḥ śrūyamāṇo+api pradhāneṣu guṇeṣu vā
BL2874/ śabdāntaratvād vākye tu vṛttau nityaṃ na vidyate
B3.14.66/ viśeṣakarmasaṃbandhe nirbhukte+api kṛtādibhiḥ
BL2876/ viśeṣanirapekṣo+anyaḥ kṛtaśabdaḥ pravartate
B3.14.67/ akarmakatve saty evaṃ ktāntaṃ bhāvābhidhāyi tat
BL2878/ tataḥ kriyāvatā kartrā yogo bhavati karmaṇām
B3.14.68/ avigrahā gatādisthā yathā grāmādikarmabhiḥ
BL2880/ saṃbadhyate kriyā tadvat kṛtapūrvyādiṣu sthitā
B3.14.69/ muṇḍisūtrvādayo+asadbhir bhāgair anugatā iva
BL2882/ vibhaktāḥ kalpitātmāno dhātavaḥ kuṭṭicarcivat
B3.14.70/ putrīyatau na putro+asti viśeṣecchā tu tādṛśī
BL2884/ vinaiva putrānugamād yā putre vyavatiṣṭhate
B3.14.71/ prāṇair vinā yathā dhārir jīvatau prāṇakarmakaḥ
BL2886/ na cātra dhārir na prāṇā jīvatis tu kriyāntaram
B3.14.72/ tathā vineṣiputrābhyāṃ putrīyāyāṃ kriyāntaram
BL2888/ anvākhyānāya bhedās tu sadṛśāḥ pratipādakāḥ
B3.14.73/ ākṣepāc ca prayoge.na viṣayāntaravartinā
BL2890/ sad apīcchākyacaḥ karma vākya eva prayujyate
B3.14.74/ prasiddhena hṛtaḥ śabdo bhāvagarhābhidhāyinā
BL2892/ abhyāse tulyarūpatvān na yaṅantaḥ prayujyate
B3.14.75/ śabdā yathā vibhajyante bhāgair iva vikalpitaiḥ
BL2894/ anvākhyeyās tathā śāstram atidūre vyavasthitam
B3.14.76/ arthasyānugamaṃ kaṃ cid dṛṣṭvaiva parikalpitam
BL2896/ padaṃ vākye pade dhātur dhātau bhāgaś ca muṇḍivat
B3.14.77/ aviprayogaḥ sādhutve vyutpattir anavasthitā
BL2898/ upāyān pratipattīnāṃ nābhimanyeta satyataḥ
B3.14.78/ yathaiva ḍitthe davatiḥ pācake pacatis tathā
BL2900/ ḍayatiś ca paciś caiva dvāv apy etāv alaukikau
B3.14.79/ prakṛtipratyayāv ūhyau padāt tābhyāṃ padaṃ tathā
BL2902/ anubandhasvarādibhyaḥ śiṣṭaiḥ śāstraṃ na tān prati
B3.14.80/ śāstradṛṣṭis tu śāstrasya prāptimātre+apy aniścite
BL2904/ yujyate pratyavāyena śāstraṃ cakṣur apaśyatām
B3.14.81/ arthāntarābhidhānāc ca paurvāparyaṃ na bhidyate
BL2906/ rājadantāhitāgnyādi- rājāśvādiṣu sarvathā
B3.14.82/ vinaiva pratyayair vṛttau ye bhinnārthābhidhāyinaḥ
BL2908/ gargādayo lukā teṣāṃ sādhutvam anugamyate
B3.14.83*/ [so+ayam ity abhisaṃbandhāt pratyayena vinā yadi
BL2910/ bhṛgvādayaḥ prayujyeran nāpatye niyamo bhavet
B3.14.84/ so+ayam ity abhisaṃbandhe liṅgopavyañjanād ṛte
BL2912/ praṣṭhādiṣu na jāyaiva niyamena pratīyate
B3.14.85/ mānameyābhisaṃbandha- viśeṣe+aṅgīkṛte tathā
BL2914/ prasthādīnām asādhutvaṃ taddhitena vinā bhavet
B3.14.86/ taddhito yogabhedena vākyaṃ vā syād vibhāṣitam
BL2916/ parimāṇādhike tatra prathamā śiṣyate punaḥ
B3.14.87/ vyatiriktasya sādhutve tad eva ca nidarśanam
BL2918/ yujyate+aṅgīkṛtādhikyaṃ tat sarvābhir vibhaktibhiḥ
B3.14.88/ śuklādiṣu matublopo vyatirekasya darśanāt
BL2920/ asādhutvanivṛttyarthaṃ sādhavas te bidādivat
B3.14.89/ viśeṣaṇād viśeṣye+arthe tadbhāvābhyuccaye sati
BL2922/ punaś ca pratisaṃhāre vṛttim eke pracakṣate

BL3416/ upakāri guṇaḥ śeṣaḥ parārtha iti kalpanā
