BL3652/ dravyavad guṇaśabde+api sa nimittam apekṣate
B3.14.455/ yo ya uccāryate śabdaḥ sa svarūpanibandhanaḥ
BL3654/ yathā tathopamāneṣu vyapekṣa na nivartate
B3.14.456/ kriyāvṛttes tṛtīyāntasy- aivaṃ cāsaṃbhave sati
BL3656/ prasiddhanyāyakaraṇo bhāṣye yujir udāhṛtaḥ
B3.14.457/ antarbhūte tu karaṇe prayogo na punar bhavet
BL3658/ nyāyenāyuktam ity atra jīvatau prāṇakarmavat
B3.14.458/ śāstrābhyāsāc ca bhedo+ayam ayuktam iti varṇyate
BL3660/ aśobhanam asaṃbaddham iti rūḍhir vyavasthitā
B3.14.459/ vivibhaktiḥ prakṛtyarthaṃ praty upādhiḥ kathaṃ bhavet
BL3662/ vibhaktipariṇāme ca prakalpyaṃ viṣayāntaram
B3.14.460/ vibhaktyantarayogo hi yasya tad viṣayāntare
BL3664/ vibhaktyantarasaṃbandhaḥ sāmarthyād anumīyate
B3.14.461/ sārūpyāt tu tad evedam iti tatropacaryate
BL3666/ śabdāntaraṃ vibhaktyā tu yuktaṃ śāstre tad aśrutam
B3.14.462/ prakṛtiś cet tṛtīyāntā tenety asmāt pratīyate
BL3668/ kriyeti prathamāntā sā kathaṃ bhavitum arhati
B3.14.463/ kriyayeti tṛtīyā ca prayoge kasya kalpyatām
BL3670/ tenety asya hi saṃbandhaḥ sūtrasthena na vidyate
B3.14.464/ sopaskāreṣu sūtreṣu vākyaśeṣaḥ samarthyate
BL3672/ tena yat tat tṛtīyāntaṃ kriyā cet seti gamyate
B3.14.465/ upādheḥ kasya cid vākye prayoga upalabhyate
BL3674/ pratīyamānadharmānyo na kadā cit prayujyate
B3.14.466/ nīlam utpalam ity atra na viśeṣye na bhedake
BL3676/ kaś cit taddharmavacano vākye śabdaḥ prayujyate
B3.14.467/ atyantānugamāt tatra na sūtre na ca vigrahe
BL3678/ vibhaktipariṇāmena kiṃ cid asti prayojanam
B3.14.468/ tṛtīyāntaṃ kriyety etad vigrahe na prayujyate
BL3680/ yathā daṇḍaḥ praharaṇaṃ krīḍāyām iti dṛśyate
B3.14.469/ ghavidhau yac ca saṃjñāyām iti sūtra udāhṛtam
BL3682/ upādānaṃ prayogeṣu tasyātyantaṃ na vidyate
B3.14.470/ yair aprayuktaiḥ saṃskāraḥ pradhāneṣu pratīyate
BL3684/ te bhede+api vibhaktīnāṃ nirdiśyanta upādhayaḥ
B3.14.471/ samudāyeṣu vartante bhāvānāṃ sahacāriṇām
BL3686/ śabdās tat tv avivakṣāyāṃ samuccayavikalpayoḥ
B3.14.472/ samuccayas tu kriyate yeṣu pratyarthavṛttiṣu
BL3688/ bhedādhiṣṭhānayā yogas tesāṃ bhavati saṃkhyayā
B3.14.473/ sarvair viśiṣṭās tair arthair janyante sahacāribhiḥ
BL3690/ buddhayaḥ pratipattṝṇāṃ śabdārthāṃs tān ato viduḥ
B3.14.474/ saṃsṛṣṭāḥ pratyayeṣv arthāḥ sarva evopakārinaḥ
BL3692/ teṣāṃ pratyayarūpeṇa sarveṣāṃ śabdavācyatā
B3.14.475/ kevalānāṃ tu bhāvānāṃ na rūpam avadhāryate
BL3694/ anirūpitarūpeṣu teṣu śabdo na vartate
B3.14.476/ pūrvaśabdaprayogāc ca samūhān na nivartate
BL3696/ vartate+avayave nāpi nopāttaṃ tyajate kva cit
B3.14.477/ samudāyābhidhāyi ca yadi bhedaṃ viśeṣayet
BL3698/ tatrātulyavibhaktitvaṃ pūrvakāyādivad bhavet
B3.14.478/ samūhe ca pradeśe ca pañcālā iti dṛśyate
BL3700/ tathā viśeṣaṇaṃ sarva ity etad upapadyate
B3.14.479/ tathārdhapippalīty atra jātyantaranivṛttaye
BL3702/ ardhaṃ ca pippalī ceti khande śabdaḥ pratīyate
B3.14.480/ pañcālānāṃ pradeśo+api bhinno janapadāntarāt
BL3704/ tatrānyasya nivṛttyarthe śabde bhedo na gamyate
B3.14.481/ prasiddhās tu viśeṣeṇa samudāye vyavasthitāḥ
BL3706/ pradeśe darśanaṃ teṣām arthaprakaraṇādibhiḥ
B3.14.482/ yad upavyañjanaṃ jāteḥ sahacāri ca karmasu
BL3708/ tatra vā rūḍhasaṃbandhaṃ yat prāyeṇopalakṣitam
B3.14.483/ samudāyaḥ pradeśo vety evaṃ tasminn anāśrite
BL3710/ arthātmany aviśeṣeṇa vartante brāhmaṇādayaḥ
B3.14.484/ yaś ca tulyaśrutir dṛṣṭaḥ samudāye vyavasthitaḥ
BL3712/ tenopacaritaikatvaṃ pradeśe+apy upalabhyate
B3.14.485/ saṃskārād upaghātād vā vṛtto +aktaparimāṇake
BL3714/ tailādau jātiśabdo+atra sāmarthyād avasīyate
B3.14.486/ na jātiguṇaśabdeṣu mūrtibhedo vivakṣitaḥ
BL3716/ te jātiguṇasaṃbandha- bhedamātranibandhanāḥ
B3.14.487/ kṛṣṇādivyapadeśaś ca sarvāvayavavṛttibhiḥ
BL3718/ guṇais te+apy ekadeśasthāḥ paṭādīnāṃ viśeṣakāḥ
B3.14.488/ paṭāvayavavṛttās tu yadā tatra paṭādayaḥ
BL3720/ tadā tailādivat teṣāṃ jātiśabdatvam ucyate
B3.14.489/ nivṛttyarthā śrutir yeṣāṃ bhedas teṣv anapekṣitaḥ
BL3722/ pradeśe samudāye vā guṇo+anyeṣāṃ nivartakaḥ
B3.14.490/ brāhmaṇādhyayane tatra vartate brāhmaṇaśrutiḥ
BL3724/ sādṛśyaṃ tatra dṛṣṭaṃ hi kṣatriyādhyayanādibhiḥ
B3.14.491/ brāhmaṇādhyayane vṛttir yadi syād brāhmaṇaśruteḥ
BL3726/ vaktavyaṃ kena dharmeṇa tulyatvaṃ kriyayor iti
B3.14.492/ adhyetari yadā vṛttir ucyate brāhmaṇaśruteḥ
BL3728/ nimittatvaṃ tadopaiti kriyaivādhyetari sthitā
B3.14.493/ simhaśabdena saṃbandhe gauryamātrābhidhāyinā
BL3730/ caitrāt ṣaṣṭhī prasajyeta yoge śattryādibhir yathā
B3.14.494/ brāhmaṇāyeva dātavyaṃ vaiśyāyety evamādiṣu
BL3732/ saṃpradānādiyogaś ca kriyāmātre na kalpate
B3.14.495/ kriyāmātrābhidhāyitvād avyayeṣu vater na ca
BL3734/ pāṭhaḥ kadā cit kartavyas tulyau pakṣāv ubhau yataḥ
B3.14.496/ jahāti jātiṃ dravyaṃ vā tasmān nāvayave sthitaḥ
BL3736/ kriyāyās tu śrutir yasmāt tadvaty arthe+avatiṣṭhate
B3.14.497/ akriyāṇāṃ nivṛttyarthā, yataś cātra kriyāśrutiḥ
BL3738/ kriyopalakṣite tasmāt kriyāśabdaḥ pratīyate
B3.14.498/ hotavyādiṣu yasmāc ca kriyānyā brāhmaṇādivat
BL3740/ apekṣaṇīyā śuddhe+arthe tasmād vṛttir na kasya cit
B3.14.499/ sarvaṃ vāpy ekadeśo vā yasminn āśriyate kva cit
BL3742/ viśeṣavṛttiṃ taṃ sarvam āhur bhede vyavasthitam
B3.14.500/ samuccayo vikalpo vā prakārāḥ sarva eva vā
BL3744/ viśeṣā iti varṇyante sāmānyaṃ vāvikalpitam
B3.14.501/ na hi brāhmaṇa ity atra bhedaḥ kaś cid apāśritaḥ
BL3746/ apākṛto vā tenāyaṃ samudāye vyavasthitaḥ
B3.14.502/ kriyā tv āśrīyate yasmin sa bhedo +adhyavasīyate
BL3748/ tathānyathā sarvathā cety aprayoge na vidyate
B3.14.503/ upamāne kriyāvṛttim upameye kriyāśrutiḥ
BL3750/ pratyāyayantī bhedasya karotīva padārthatām
B3.14.504/ vyāpāreṇaiva sādṛśye vyāpārasya vivakṣite
BL3752/ kriyāvadvacanāc chabdāt pratyayaḥ pratipādyate
B3.14.505/ kriyāvato+api sādṛśye vaktum iṣṭe kriyāvatā
BL3754/ adhyetā brāhmaṇa iva pratyayo na nivartate
B3.14.506/ adhīte tulya ity evaṃ puṃlliṅgena viśeṣaṇam
BL3756/ kriyāvati kriyāyāṃ tu tulyaśabde napuṃsakam
B3.14.507/ prakṛtyarthe viśiṣṭe+api pratyayārthāviśeṣaṇāt
BL3758/ putreṇa tulyaḥ kapila iti vṛttiḥ prasajyate
B3.14.508/ yāḥ putre rūḍhasaṃbandhāḥ kriyā loke vivakṣitāḥ
BL3760/ tābhiḥ kriyāvataḥ putrād guṇatulye vatir bhavet
B3.14.509/ antarbhūtaṃ nimittaṃ ca rūḍhiśabdeṣu yady api
BL3762/ kriyās tu sahacāriṇyo rūḍhāḥ santi padārthavat
B3.14.510/ kramaṃ tu yadi bādhitvā pratyayārthaviśeṣaṇam
BL3764/ pradhānānugrahāt sāmyād vibhakteś cāvatiṣṭhate
B3.14.511/ prakṛter aviśiṣṭatvāt kriyātulye prasajyate
BL3766/ putrādau guṇaśabdebhyaḥ pūrvoktasya viparyaye
B3.14.512/ sthūlena tulyo yātīti bahiraṅgā kriyāśrutiḥ
BL3768/ animittaṃ vates tulyaṃ yātīty atreṣyate vatiḥ
B3.14.513/ dvayaṃ viśeṣyate tena yad ekatra viśeṣaṇaṃ
BL3770/ tulyaśabdo hi taṃ dharmam ubhayastham apekṣate
B3.14.514/ ekaḥ samāno dharmaś ced upamānopameyayoḥ
BL3772/ tulayā saṃmitaṃ tulyam iti tatropapadyate
B3.14.515/ sūtre śrutaś ca dviṣṭho+asāv abhedena pratīyate
BL3774/ na ca sāmānyaśabdatvād aśrutā gamyate kriyā
B3.14.516/ aśrutāś ca pratīyante nideśasthāyitādayaḥ
BL3776/ ye dharmā niyatās teṣāṃ putrādiṣu na vidyate
B3.14.517/ anāśritakriyas tasmān na tulyo+asti kriyāvatā
BL3778/ kriyāyāḥ śravaṇe sāpi kriyāvattā pratīyate
B3.14.518/ dvayoḥ pratividhānāc ca jyāyastvam abhidhīyate
BL3780/ nityāsattvābhidhāyitvāt pratyayārthaviśeṣaṇe
B3.14.519/ asattvabhūto vyāpāraḥ kevalaḥ pratyaye yataḥ
BL3782/ vidyate lakṣaṇārthatvaṃ nāsti tena kriyāśruteḥ
B3.14.520/ kriyāvatas tu grahaṇāt prakṛtyarthaviśeṣaṇe
BL3784/ kriyāmātrena tulyatve siddhāsattvābhidhāyitā
B3.14.521/ yadā kriyānimittaṃ tu sādṛśyaṃ syāt kriyāvatoḥ
BL3786/ kriyāvato+abhidheyatvāt tadā dravyābhidhāyitā
B3.14.522/ avyayeṣu vateḥ pāṭhaḥ kāryas tatra svarādivat
BL3788/ brāhmaṇena samo+adhyetety atra ca pratyayo bhavet
B3.14.523/ sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca vatyarthenāpadiśyate
BL3790/ tulyam ity anyathā kalpyo vākyaśeṣo+aśruto bhavet
B3.14.524/ kriyāvatoś ca sādṛśye pratyayārthaviśeṣaṇe
BL3792/ adhyetrā sadṛśo+adhyetety atra nāsti vater vidhiḥ
B3.14.525/ tulyārthair iti yā tasyās tṛtīyāyā na bhidyate
BL3794/ artho bhede+api sarvābhir itarābhir vibhaktibhiḥ
B3.14.526/ bhojyate brāhmaṇa iva tulyaṃ bhuktaṃ dvijātinā
BL3796/ paśyati brāhmaṇam iva tulyaṃ vipreṇa paśyati
B3.14.527/ brāhmaṇeneva vijñātaṃ tulyaṃ jñātaṃ dvijātinā
BL3798/ dīyatāṃ brāhmaṇāyeva tulyaṃ vipreṇa dīyatām
B3.14.528/ brāhmaṇād iva vaiśyāt tvam adhīṣvādhyayanaṃ bahu
BL3800/ ity evamādibhir bhedas tṛtīyāyā na kaś cana
B3.14.529/ tulyaṃ madhurayādhīye mātrā tulyaṃ smarāmi tām
BL3802/ madhurāyāś ca mātuś ca kathaṃ sādṛśyakalpanā
B3.14.530/ madhurāviṣayaḥ pāṭhaḥ smaraṇaṃ mātṛkarmakam
BL3804/ madhurāmātṛśabdābhyām abhedenābhidhīyate
B3.14.531/ uṣṭrāvayavatulyeṣu mukheṣūṣṭraśrutir yathā
BL3806/ vartate gṛhatulye ca prāsāde madhurāśrutiḥ
B3.14.532/ yathādhyayanayoḥ sāmyam adhyetror apadiśyate
BL3808/ tathā kriyāgatair dharmair ucyante sādhanāśrayāḥ
B3.14.533/ ivārthe yac ca vacanaṃ pūrvasūtre ca yo vidhiḥ
BL3810/ kriyāśabdaśrutau bhedo na kaś cid vidyate tayoḥ
B3.14.534/ yady apy upādhir anyatra niyato na prayujyate
BL3812/ rūpābhedāt tv anirjñātā kriyātra śrūyate punaḥ
B3.14.535/ yathā vyutparayaḥ pucchau kyaṅante sudurādayaḥ
BL3814/ saty api pratyayārthatve bhedābhāvād udāhṛtāḥ
B3.14.536/ evaṃ ca sati pūrveṇa siddho+atrāpi vater vidhiḥ
BL3816/ niyame vābhidhāne vā bhidyate na kriyāśrutiḥ
B3.14.537/ ive dravyādiviṣayaḥ pratyayaḥ punar ucyate
BL3818/ kriyāṇām eva sadṛśve pūrvasūtre vidhīyate
B3.14.538/ madhurāyām iva gṛhā brāhmaṇasyeva pāṇḍurāḥ
BL3820/ ity atra dravyaguṇayoḥ pūrveṇa na vatir bhavet
B3.14.539/ ārambhasyākriyārthatve nārtho yogena vidyate
BL3822/ ṛte kriyāyā grahaṇāt pūrvayogena sidhyati
B3.14.540/ madhurāvayave vṛttir vvākhyātā madhurāśruteḥ
BL3824/ brāhmaṇāvayavān dantān vakṣyati brāhmaṇaśrutiḥ
B3.14.541/ na kā cid ivayoge tu bāhyāt saṃbandhino
BL3826/ ṣaṣṭhī vidhīyate tatra pūrveṇa pratyayo bhavet
B3.14.542/ ādhikyaṃ tulyaśabdena saṃbandha upajāyate
BL3828/ ṣaṣṭhītṛtīye tatra stas tulyaśabdo hi vācakaḥ
B3.14.543/ ivaśabdaprayoge tu bāhyāt saṃbandhino vinā
BL3830/ nādhikyam upamāne+asti dyotakaḥ sa prayujyate
B3.14.544/ ive yo vyatireko+atra sa prāsādādihetukaḥ
BL3832/ tulye tadviṣayāpekṣam ādhikyam upajāyate
B3.14.545/ gavayena samo+anadvān iti vṛttis tathā bhavet
BL3834/ na tv asti gaur ivety atra vyatireka ivāśrayaḥ
B3.14.546/ upameyena saṃbandhāt prāk prāsādādihetuke
BL3836/ vyatireke vater bhāvo na tulyārthatvahetuke
B3.14.547/ ivaśabdena saṃbandhe na tṛtīyā vidhīyate
BL3838/ prakṛtāṃ tām atas tyaktvā vibhaktyantaraṃ āśritam
B3.14.548/ saptamy api na tatrāsti jñāpakārthā tu sā kṛtā
BL3840/ iṣṭā sā śeṣaviṣaye niyatāsu vibhaktiṣu
B3.14.549/ yadi tu vyatirekeṇa viṣaye+asmin vibhaktayaḥ
BL3842/ pravarteraṃs tṛtīyaiva vyabhicāraṃ pradarśayet
B3.14.550/ vyabhicāre tathā siddhe saptamīgrahaṇād vinā
BL3844/ saptamy evocyate sarvā na santy anyā vibhaktayaḥ
B3.14.551/ atyantam atra viṣaye saptamyā jñāpakārthayā
BL3846/ bādhitā vinivarteta ṣaṣṭhī sā gṛhyate punaḥ
B3.14.552/ pūrvābhyām eva yogābhyāṃ vigrahāntarakalpanāt
BL3848/ arhārthe+api vatiḥ siddhaḥ sa tv ekena nidarśyate
B3.14.553/ tena tulyam iti prāpte kriyopādhiḥ prasidhyati
BL3850/ rājavad vartate rājety atra bhede vivakṣite
B3.14.554/ rājatvena prasiddhā ye pṛthuprabhṛtayo nṛpāḥ
BL3852/ yudhiṣṭhirāntās te+anyeṣām upamānaṃ mahīkṣitām
B3.14.555/ siddhyasiddhikṛto bheda upamānopameyayoḥ
BL3854/ sarvatraiva yato+asiddhaṃ prasiddhenopamīyate
B3.14.556/ rājavad rūpam asyeti rājany eva vivakṣite
BL3856/ akriyārthena yogena dvitīyena bhaviṣyati
B3.14.557/ upamānāvivakṣāyāṃ niyamārtho+ayam ucyate
BL3858/ dharmo+arhatikriyākartā tadarthaṃ vacanaṃ punaḥ
B3.14.558/ kṛtahastavad ity etat prasiddheṣv eva dṛśyate
BL3860/ rājatvena prasiddhe ca rājñi rājavad ity api
B3.14.559/ arājñi yeṣāṃ dharmāṇāṃ dṛṣṭo+atyantam asaṃbhavaḥ
BL3862/ te rājani niyamyante tyajyante vyabhicāriṇaḥ
B3.14.560/ arhateś ca kriyā kartrī yā tasyāṃ vatir iṣyate
BL3864/ rājānam arhati cchattram iti na tv evamādiṣu
B3.14.561/ prayuktānāṃ hi śabdānāṃ śāstreṇānugamaḥ satām
BL3866/ chattrādyarthe tu vacane pratyākhyānaṃ na saṃbhavet
B3.14.562/ tadarham iti nārabdhaṃ sūtraṃ vyākaraṇāntare
BL3868/ saṃbhavaty upamātrāpi bhedasya parikalpanāt
B3.14.563/ ekasya kāryanirjñānāt siddhasya viṣayāntare
BL3870/ taddharmatvavivakṣāyāṃ buddhyā bhedaḥ prakalpyate
B3.14.564/ sūtrārambhān na caitasmād ivaśabdasya vidyate
BL3872/ prayogaḥ so+api caitasya viṣaye vidyate vateḥ
B3.14.565/ dasyuhendra ivety etad aindramantre prayujyate
BL3874/ anyatra dṛṣṭakarmendro yathety asmin vivakṣite
B3.14.566/ pūrvām avasthām āśritya yāvasthā vyapadiśyate
BL3876/ sadṛśas tvaṃ tavaiveti tatraivam abhidhīyate
B3.14.567/ prasiddhabhedaṃ yatrānyad upamānaṃ na vidyate
BL3878/ upameyasya tatrātmā svabuddhyā pravibhajyate
B3.14.568/ yo+api svābhāviko bhedaḥ so+api buddhinibandhanaḥ
BL3880/ tenāsmin viṣaye bhinnam abhinnaṃ vā na vidyate
B3.14.569/ aṅgadī kuṇḍalī ceti darśayan bhedahetubhiḥ
BL3882/ caitram īdṛśam ity āha buddhyavasthāparigrahāt
B3.14.570/ etaiḥ śabdair yathābhūtaḥ pratyayātmopajāyate
BL3884/ tatpratyayānukāreṇa viṣayo+apy upapadyate
B3.14.571/ buddhyavasthāvibhāgena bhedakāryaṃ pratīyate
BL3886/ janyanta iva śabdānām arthāḥ sarve vivakṣayā
B3.14.572/ tathāvidhe+api bāhye+arthe bhidyante yatra buddhayaḥ
BL3888/ na tatra kaś cit sādṛśyaṃ sad api pratipadyate
B3.14.573/ atyantaṃ viṣaye bhinne yāvat prakhyā na bhidyate
BL3890/ na tāvat pratyabhijñānaṃ kasya cid vinivartate
B3.14.574/ ayam eva tu sūtreṇa bhedo bhedena darśitaḥ
BL3892/ prasiddham api durjñānam abudhaḥ pratipadyate
B3.14.575/ vaiyākaraṇavad brūte na vaiyākaraṇaḥ sadā
BL3894/ vaiyākaraṇavad brūṣvety ataḥ so+apy abhidhīyate
B3.14.576/ ke cit pumāṃso bhāṣante strīvat puṃvac ca yoṣitaḥ
BL3896/ vyabhicāre svadharmo+api punas tenopadiśyate
B3.14.577/ sadṛśas tvaṃ tavaiveti loke yad abhidhīyate
BL3898/ upamānāntaraṃ tatra prasaktaṃ vinivartate
B3.14.578/ yuktam aupayikaṃ rājña ity arthasya nidarśane
BL3900/ upamānāvivakṣāyāṃ tadarham iti paṭhyate
B3.14.579/ prasaktānuprasaktas tu vatiśeṣo+abhidhīyate
BL3902/ upamānābhisaṃbandhād asmin vatir udāhṛtaḥ
B3.14.580/ pradhānakalpanābhāve guṇaśabdasya darśanāt
BL3904/ upasargād vatau siddhā dhātau dhātvarthakalpanā
B3.14.581/ svaṃ rūpam iti caitasminn arthasyāpi parigrahaḥ
BL3906/ rūpavaj jñāpitas tasmād āsanno+artho grahīṣyate
B3.14.582/ dhātvarthenopajanitaṃ sādhanatvena sādhanam
BL3908/ dhātunā kṛtam ity evam asmin sūtre pratīyate
B3.14.583/ yaḥ śabdaś caritārthatvād atyantaṃ na prayujyate
BL3910/ viṣaye+adarśanāt tatra lopas tasyābhidhīyate
B3.14.584/ kriyāyāṃ sādhane dravye prādayo ye vyavasthitāḥ
BL3912/ tebhyaḥ sattvābhidhāyibhyo vatiḥ svārthe vidhīyate
B3.14.585/ pratyayena vinā prādis tatrārthe na prayujyate
BL3914/ bhedena tu samākhyāne vibhāgaḥ parikalpitaḥ
B3.14.586/ anaṅgīkṛtasattvaṃ tu yadi gṛhyeta sādhanam
BL3916/ vibhaktibhir niyogaḥ syād yathaiva tasilādiṣu
B3.14.587/ pāṭhād yair avibhaktitvaṃ vatyanteṣv anugamyate
BL3918/ teṣām udvata ity atra vaktavyā savibhaktitā
B3.14.588/ vatyarthaṃ nāvagāhete puṃvad ity asya darśanāt
BL3920/ nañsnañāv apavādasya bādhakaṃ tan nipātanam
B3.14.589/ etam utkrāmato nūnaṃ vatyarthaṃ nañsnañāv iti
BL3922/ tayoḥ pravṛttāv utsargo bādhanān nopapadyate
B3.14.590/ nañsnañau vihitau yena sa yogo nāvagāhate
BL3924/ vatiprakaraṇaṃ tad dhi liṅgam evaṃ samarthyate
B3.14.591/ abhedenopamānasya bhinnārthopanipātitā
BL3926/ ūhas tathopamānānām aṅgavan nopalabhyate
B3.14.592/ gāvedhuke carau dṛṣṭā govikartākṣavāpayoḥ
BL3928/ paśū rudra iva hy etāv ity ekavacanaśrutiḥ
B3.14.593/ upamānasya bhedāc ca bahuṣu syād año vidhiḥ
BL3930/ kāśyapā iti lopaḥ syāt tathā pratikṛtiṣv api
B3.14.594/ evaṃ tu yuktavadbhāvād atraikavacanaṃ bhavet
BL3932/ lum manuṣye tathoktaṃ syāl liṅgasyaikasya siddhaye
B3.14.595/ upameyeṣu bhinneṣu kiṃ cid ekaṃ pravartate
BL3934/ pratyayasya vidhau tatra nityaṃ yuktavad iṣyate
B3.14.596/ yadā pratyupameyaṃ tu tad ekaikam avasthitam
BL3936/ tadā bāhyārthabhedena taddhitāntaṃ pracīyate
B3.14.597/ yathā samūhapracaye dvigūnāṃ bhinnasaṃkhyatā
BL3938/ pañcapūlyādiṣu tathā lubantapracayo bhavet
B3.14.598/ pracaye bhidyamāne tu saṃkhyā pūleṣu bhidyate
BL3940/ arthabhedo lubanteṣu naivaṃ kaś cana dṛśyate
B3.14.599/ yeṣūpameyavacanaḥ śabdo+anyo na prayujyate
BL3942/ upamānasya tatrānyaiḥ saṃkhyāyā bheda iṣyate
B3.14.600/ yathā guḍatilādīnāṃ prayogād ekasaṃkhyatā
BL3944/ pākāder aprayoge tu bhinnā saṃkhyābhidhīyate
B3.14.601/ yaḥ saṃbandhigato bhedaḥ sa prayoge pratīyate
BL3946/ saṃbandhinām ato bheda upameye na gamyate
B3.14.602/ tasmāt sāmānyaśabdatva- prasaṅgavinivṛttaye
BL3948/ upameyagato bheda upamāneṣu dṛśyate
B3.14.603/ upamānaṃ samastānām abhinnam śrūyate kva cit
BL3950/ bhinnānām upameyanām ekaikam vopamīyate
B3.14.604/ yathā garuḍa ity etad vyūhāpekṣaṃ prayujyate
BL3952/ ekena yatra sādṛśyaṃ vainateyena hastinām
B3.14.605/ ekasyāpi pratīyeta bhinnā pratikṛtiḥ saha
BL3954/ kāśyapasyeti tenāyaṃ pratyekam avatiṣṭhate
B3.14.606/ meghāḥ śaila ivety ukte samastānāṃ pratīyate
BL3956/ sādṛśyam giriṇaikena pratyekaṃ tena bhidyate
B3.14.607/ chāpekṣā tadviṣayatā vidheyatvān na gamyate
BL3958/ kākatālīyam ity atra prasiddham hy upalakṣaṇam
B3.14.608/ rājāśvādiś ca viṣayaḥ syād anyo vety aniścitam
BL3960/ tena cchasya vidhānāt prāg vyapadeśo na vidyate
B3.14.609/ dvayor ivārthayor atra nimittatvaṃ pratīyate
BL3962/ ekenāvayavo yuktaḥ pratyayo+anyena yujyate
B3.14.610/ caitrasya tatrāgamanaṃ kākasyāgamanaṃ yathā
BL3964/ dasyor abhinipātas tu tālasya patanaṃ yathā
B3.14.611/ saṃnipāte tayor yānyā kriyā tatropajāyate
BL3966/ vadhādir upameye+arthe tayā chavidhir iṣyate
B3.14.612/ kriyāyāṃ samavetāyāṃ dravyaśabdo +avatiṣṭhate
BL3968/ pātāgamanayoḥ kāka- tālaśabdau tathā sthitau
B3.14.613/ yad anvākhyāyakaṃ vākyaṃ tad evaṃ parikalpyate
BL3970/ prayogavākvaṃ yal loke tad evaṃ na prayujyate
B3.14.614/ yayor atarkitā prāptir dṛśyate kākatālavat
BL3972/ tayoḥ samāsaprakṛter vṛttir abhyupagamyate
B3.14.615/ kākasya tālena yathā vadho yasya tu dasyunā
BL3974/ tatra citrīkṛte+anyasminn upameye cha iṣyate
B3.14.616/ cañcatprakāraś cañcatko bṛhatka iti cāpare
BL3976/ maṇimaḍḍūkakhadyotān sādṛśvena pracakṣate
B3.14.617/ tatronmeṣanimeṣābhyāṃ khadyota upamīyate
BL3978/ śvāsaprabandhair maṇḍūkaḥ spandamānaprabho maṇiḥ
B3.14.618/ pravikāsiprabho+alpo+api mahān ya upalabhyate
BL3980/ bṛhatka iti tatraiṣa maṇau śabdaḥ prayujyate
B3.14.619/ sādṛśyam eva sarvatra prakāraḥ kaiś cid iṣyate
BL3982/ bhede+api tu prakārākhyā kaiś cid abhyupagamyate
B3.14.620/ prakāravacanaḥ kaś cit prakāravati saṃsthitaḥ
BL3984/ prakāramātre vartitvā kaś cit tadvati vartate
B3.14.621/ sādṛśyagrahaṇaṃ sūtre sadṛśasyopalakṣaṇam
BL3986/ tulyayor avyayībhāve sahaśabdo+abhidhāyakaḥ
B3.14.622/ vipsāsādṛśyayor vṛttir yā yathārthābhidhāyinaḥ
BL3988/ sa cāyam avyayībhāve bhedo bhedena darśitaḥ
B3.14.623/ sādṛśyaṃ yogyatā kaiś cid anāv abhyupagamyate
BL3990/ yat tu mūrtigataṃ sāmyaṃ tat sahenābhidhīyate
B3.14.624/ itthaṃbhāve+api sādṛśyaṃ buddhyavasthānibandhanam
BL3992/ grahaṇe bhedamātrasya tatrānyaivābhidhīyate
B3.14.625/ gaur vāhīka iti dvitve sādṛśyaṃ pratyudāhṛtam
BL3994/ śuklādau sati niṣpanne vāhīko na dvir ucyate
